# Awesome Jev [![Awesome](https://awesome.re/badge.svg)](https://awesome.re)

> Discover Jev-powered apps, developer resources, and runnable decision examples.

[Jev](https://docs.typesafe.ai/introduction) is TypeSafe AI's System One model: give it state and typed questions, then use the returned choices, scores, and probabilities in your code. This independent community directory collects applications that use Jev, resources for developers, and small, inspectable workflows to learn from.

**[Explore Jev-powered apps](community/projects/apps/README.md)** · **[Share your app](CONTRIBUTING.md#list-a-jev-powered-app)**

**Try the beta web UI at [JevList](https://jevlist.ai/).** Explore the projects in this directory through a searchable web interface. We're continually improving the experience—take a look and let us know what you think!

## Explore Jev with our skill

Use **Awesome Jev Guide** to understand Jev and explore solutions that fit your needs. Describe your workflow in plain language, and your coding agent will help you choose an example, adapt a project, or build a suitable starter—with guided setup along the way.

**1. Install the skill.** With Node.js 22+ installed, run this in your project directory:

```sh
npx skills add AppitStudio/awesome-jev --skill awesome-jev-guide
```

Select your coding agent when prompted. For Codex, you can add `--agent codex`. No TypeSafe account or API key is needed to explore ideas or run the offline examples.

**2. Open your coding agent in that project and try a prompt.**

New to Jev? Start here:

```text
Use awesome-jev-guide. Explain Jev simply, help me understand what I can
build with it, and walk me through the easiest example. I don't have
an API key yet.
```

Have a workflow in mind? Fill in the brackets:

```text
Use awesome-jev-guide. Today I [describe the work I do manually].
I want to [describe the result]. I use [language, framework, or tools].
Help me find the best starting point in this repo and explain what
needs adapting. Ask me anything essential that's missing.
```

Ready to build?

```text
Use awesome-jev-guide to build a small starter for [my workflow] using
[my stack]. Start with a working offline demo, explain what I should
customize, then guide me through API-key setup and a first live check
when I'm ready.
```

**3. Follow the next step together.** The guide uses current TypeSafe documentation and explains what already works, what needs building, and how to handle uncertain results. When you want live access, it walks you through private key setup—never paste a key into chat.

See the [full onboarding guide](docs/using-the-guide.md) for installation options, demos, and setup details.

## Start here

**[Explore what you can build](docs/explore-use-cases.md)** — choose a real implementation by the result you want, then follow its guide, worked example, and setup path.

- [Introduction](https://docs.typesafe.ai/introduction) - Understand Jev's state-and-questions interface and its three decision primitives.
- [Official quick start](https://docs.typesafe.ai/introduction/quickstart) - Make a first request using Python or HTTP.
- [Run an example locally](docs/getting-started.md) - Try a complete workflow with synthetic mock responses, without an account, an API key, or package installation.
- [Choose a decision pattern](docs/decision-patterns.md) - Match a task to Choice, Score, or Noul and define what happens when the result is uncertain.
- [HTTP API reference](https://docs.typesafe.ai/api) - Check the wire format, authentication, answer fields, and error responses.

## Official SDKs and tools

These resources are maintained by TypeSafe.

- [Agent skill](https://docs.typesafe.ai/agent-skill) - Official docs for giving a coding agent API context and guidance when designing narrow System One / Jev decision questions (Claude Code plugin and skills.sh install paths).
- [JavaScript SDK](https://github.com/typesafe-ai/typesafe-sdk-js) - JavaScript and TypeScript client that infers answer types from the supplied questions.
- [Python SDK](https://github.com/typesafe-ai/typesafe-sdk-python) - Synchronous and asynchronous clients with typed answers and configurable retries.
- [System One Adapter](https://github.com/typesafe-ai/system-one-adapter-python) - Run a similar typed-question interface against other LLM providers for comparisons; those responses do not come from Jev.
- [TypeSafe Agent Skills](https://github.com/typesafe-ai/skills) - Official Claude Code / skills.sh agent skill for designing typed System One / Jev decisions; [docs](https://docs.typesafe.ai/agent-skill).

## Community projects

The [community knowledge base](community/knowledge-base/README.md) turns credited articles into original implementation guides with source links, project connections and stated validation limits.

**[Browse the project directory](community/projects/README.md)** for separate app and tool directories, each with full project pages covering use cases, setup, examples, limitations, and review evidence. The links below still take you directly to the upstream projects.

Checks are tied to reviewed versions; see each page and the [validation scope](docs/validation.md#community-project-checks). Mocked tests and reported live smoke checks do not establish model quality on your workload.

### Apps powered by Jev

- [1 Million Emojis](https://github.com/cwdx/1-million-emojis) - `Open source` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Shared million-emoji canvas where TypeSafe Jev paints alongside humans from stroke context. [Try app](https://chriswijnia.com/lab/emoji) · [Project guide](community/projects/apps/1-million-emojis.md).
- [AI Model Picker](https://github.com/Gavinluo19/ai-model-picker) - `Open source` · `Free`. macOS task-to-model picker that opens the task in your existing AI app; optional TypeSafe Jev classification. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/ai-model-picker.md).
- [AI Plays Pokémon (kotlinds)](https://github.com/kotlinds/ai-plays-pokemon) - `Source available` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Kotlin desktop app that puts an AI in front of Pokémon HeartGold on a Nintendo DS emulator: the game RAM is described as JSON, and a Jev backend picks each action from a closed list with per-option probabilities, optionally with an LLM planner in hybrid mode. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/ai-plays-pokemon.md).
- [AI, SI, or Whatever](https://github.com/SjJ1017/ai-si-or-whatever) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Chrome extension where TypeSafe Jev can choose AI/SI/alternating/custom webpage terminology per page. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/ai-si-or-whatever.md).
- [AI;DR](https://github.com/duyet/aidr) - `Source available` · `Free`. Ranked daily AI-news site and digest whose importance scoring can use Jev through AnyRouter, with a chat-model rubric as backup. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/aidr.md).
- [Albatross](https://github.com/morganlinton/Albatross) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Terminal-first coding harness (bring your own model, key or MCP server) with an experimental Jev Mode that puts TypeSafe's decision model before the main LLM. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/albatross.md).
- [Anki Review-Finder](https://github.com/elias170105/anki-review-finder) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Alpha CLI that audits Anki flashcards through AnkiConnect with TypeSafe Jev, flagging structural defects such as answer leaks, missing context, scope problems and binary questions, then writes priority and defect tags back to Anki (never card text) and produces an HTML report. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/anki-review-finder.md).
- [Aperture (ranked reading feed)](https://github.com/richfairhurst/aperture) - `Open source` · `Free`. Single-user, self-hosted intelligence feed that imports Readwise Reader documents and optional public sources (Reddit, GitHub Trending, YouTube, reports, CVEs), has Jev rate each item for quality, personal utility and actionability, and shows a ranked, explainable reading queue with summaries. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/aperture-reading-feed.md).
- [Aplausômetro](https://github.com/rf-camillo/jev-aplausometro) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Paste a social post and watch a simulated audience react in real time with TypeSafe Jev calibrated probabilities. [Try app](https://aplausometro.rafaelcamillo.com.br) · [Project guide](community/projects/apps/jev-aplausometro.md).
- [Apparite (jev2ui)](https://github.com/dglazkov/jev2ui) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Local design-mock lab: TypeSafe Jev chooses IA/anatomy; Gemini writes copy; code assembles A2UI-inspired mocks. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/jev2ui.md).
- [arJev](https://github.com/harmoniqs/arjev) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Daily arXiv digest engine: lexical front-line plus TypeSafe Jev confidence-gated rerank against an Obsidian vault. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/arjev.md).
- [AskJev (openconstruct)](https://github.com/openconstruct/askjev) - `Open source` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Static single-page yes/no UI: OpenRouter Decisions + TypeSafe Jev Noul; key stays in browser localStorage. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/openconstruct-askjev.md).
- [Auto-Oui](https://github.com/jasondupontbizz/auto-oui) - `Open source` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Chrome extension (French UI) that watches a tab you enable and answers "Oui" — or clicks the approve button — when an AI agent on supported sites stops to ask for permission; detection is local rules by default with no network calls, and an optional Jev mode with your TypeSafe key judges ambiguous wording against a probability threshold (0.90 default). The README warns to keep it off for payments, messages, deletions and production actions. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/auto-oui.md).
- [axo-launcher](https://github.com/axolutions/axo-launcher) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Minimalist Android launcher where TypeSafe Jev settles ambiguous quick-capture phrases (Portuguese docs). [Project guide](community/projects/apps/axo-launcher.md).
- [Baton (notch app)](https://github.com/jeanlucandre-afk/baton) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. macOS notch app that alerts you when AI chats finish or need you; TypeSafe Jev decides which. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/baton-notch.md).
- [Beat Jev](https://github.com/ojusave/beat-jev) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Penalty shootout against TypeSafe Jev on Render Workflows with Python and TypeScript examples. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/beat-jev.md).
- [beebots](https://github.com/imikerussell/beebots) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Three OKX paper-trading bees; every trade decision via TypeSafe Jev (OpenAI only for bee design/portraits). Not financial advice. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/beebots.md).
- [betterx](https://github.com/kalowery/betterx) - `Source available` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Chrome/Brave extension that classifies posts and replies on x.com with TypeSafe Jev as you browse — seven questions per post in one ~150–250 ms request (tone, post type, argument quality, racist, contempt for a nationality or immigrants, antisemitic, sexually explicit) — and labels, dims, blurs or hides them by your rules. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/betterx.md).
- [Bilibili noise filter for Firefox (JEV)](https://github.com/Ray4AI/jev-bili-filter) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Firefox (and Chrome) port of littlewindy123/jev-bili-filter: toggles for spoilers, trolling, ads and partisan chants, plus your own one-sentence rules, filter Bilibili comments and danmaku with TypeSafe Jev; adds local keywords that work without a key, per-item Canvas danmaku filtering, a block log and usage tracking. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/ray4ai-jev-bili-filter.md).
- [B站降噪 · JEV (Bilibili noise filter)](https://github.com/littlewindy123/jev-bili-filter) - `Open source` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Original Chrome/Edge extension (Chinese, with English README) that filters Bilibili comments and plain-text danmaku on the page with TypeSafe Jev: four toggles — spoilers, trolling, ads and fandom flame wars — plus one-sentence custom rules, no blocklists or prompts to maintain. Upstream of the listed Firefox port. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/jev-bili-filter.md).
- [Call Coach](https://github.com/ZeroGold/call-coach-ai) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Local live sales-call coach: TypeSafe Jev picks next actions and buying stage from transcript turns (mic or sample call). [Project guide](community/projects/apps/call-coach-ai.md).
- [Capture (sgaabdu4)](https://github.com/sgaabdu4/capture) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Private Mac voice diary: local Parakeet transcription, TypeSafe Jev sorting, Notion after approval. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/capture.md).
- [ChatJevPT](https://github.com/samkoosh/ChatJevPT) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Chat app that makes TypeSafe Jev 'generate' an answer one character at a time by asking the same choice question over and over (next character or END), shows each letter's top-five probabilities, lets Jev grade its own answer, and meters cost; several 'model levels' compare naive and fuller pipelines. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/chatjevpt.md).
- [ChatJump](https://github.com/saydus/chatjump) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Menu-bar launcher for your AI chats: press Control-Option-Space, type what you remember, and Jev ranks open Claude, ChatGPT, Codex and Chrome chat tabs by meaning, then opens the right one. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/chatjump.md).
- [Cheese Toast](https://github.com/jbum/cheesetoast) - `Open source` · `Free`. Classic teapot-in-space arcade game in the browser; TypeSafe Jev moderates score names and can play as a bot. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/cheese-toast.md).
- [Chess with Jev](https://github.com/vlaier/chessWithJev) - `Source available` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Browser chess where your opponent is TypeSafe Jev: every move is a short chain of choice questions (focus, piece, destination) whose options are the legal moves, so illegal moves cannot be expressed; you can read each answer chain and rewrite the questions in an in-app workflow editor. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/chess-with-jev.md).
- [Clasificador de noticias regionales con Jev](https://github.com/EdgarTapara/clasificador-noticias-jev) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Spanish-language Windows workflow that turns a monthly multi-portal news Excel into a reviewed list of Arequipa economic news, using Jev to classify headlines before scraping. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/clasificador-noticias-jev.md).
- [Clean Code Review](https://github.com/frostney/clean-code-review) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Hosted/source PR reviewer: TypeSafe Jev judges files on Clean Code questions; Luna writes evidence-first prose (MCP included). [Try app](https://clean-code-review.vercel.app) · [Project guide](community/projects/apps/clean-code-review.md).
- [Clef homelab decision engine](https://github.com/NikhileshThiru/clef) - `Open source` · `Free source build`. - [CrawlHive](https://github.com/h100envy/crawl-hive) - `Open source` · `Pricing unverified`. Memecoin signal crawler swarm: TypeSafe Jev rates every crawler find in ~0.5 s before safety scans and optional Grok review. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/crawl-hive.md).

[Project guide](community/projects/apps/xift.md).
- [Xtags](https://github.com/manifoldor/xtags) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Chrome extension/userscript that tags X posts with TypeSafe Jev intent and risk signals for personal local browsing. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/xtags.md).
- [YapSketch](https://github.com/farouqaldori/yapsketch) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. macOS app for explaining bugs by talking and pointing: record your screen and voice, and Jev splits what you said into points, matches each to what you showed, and picks the clearest screenshot, then puts numbered text plus images on your clipboard for a coding agent. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/yapsketch.md).
- [YouTube Sponsor Skip (Jev)](https://github.com/AnatoliyLikarchuk/youtube-sponsor-skip) - `Open source` · `Free` · `BYOK`. Chrome/Safari extension that finds spoken sponsor segments in Russian YouTube videos via TypeSafe Jev on subtitles and skips them. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/youtube-sponsor-skip.md).
- [Zearch](https://github.com/zearchcomputer/zearch) - `Open source` · `Free source build` · `BYOK`. Perplexity-style open-source UI for TypeSafe Jev: search to evidence state to typed decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/apps/zearch.md).
Applications with a user-facing workflow powered in part or entirely by Jev. The [full app directory](community/projects/apps/README.md) shows platforms, access requirements, and Jev's specific role. [Tags](community/APP_TAGS.md) distinguish source access from pricing; commercial and closed-source apps can qualify. Makers are welcome to [submit their own apps](CONTRIBUTING.md#list-a-jev-powered-app).

### Developer projects and integrations

- [2048 with Jev](https://github.com/abarslan04/2048-with-jev) - 2048 experiment where a bot chooses moves via the TypeSafe SDK (`typesafe-sdk`, pinned) using a fixed Jev model id so runs do not silently follow `jev-latest` alias moves. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/2048-with-jev.md).
- [2048: Jev vs Laya](https://github.com/Devikrishna545/2048GameDecision) - Experiment where two System One decision models — hosted TypeSafe Jev and open-weight Laya (plus a LoRA fine-tune) — play 2048 on the same seeds against random and greedy baselines, each move asking a `move` choice and a `danger` yes/no from a board description, with every move logged and reports comparing win likelihood, choices and latency. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/2048-jev-vs-laya.md).
- [8-K Filings and Decision Models](https://github.com/magilliam27/sec-8k-filings-and-decision-models) - Jev rated 26,962 S&P 500 8-K filings (2013 to 2026) from very bad to very good for $2.32; within item group and year the rating's rank correlation with the next two days' market-adjusted return is +0.025, positive in 13 of 14 years, and the move is mostly gone by the next open. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sec-8k-decision-models.md).
- [@amatelic/jev](https://github.com/amatelic/jev) - Node library and local server for one-line choice/noul/score decisions from small open models, with no API key or cloud call. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/amatelic-jev.md).
- [@contexera DSH jev plugin](https://github.com/Contexera/dsh-plugins) - DeepSeek Harness plugin monorepo whose `jev` package adds a TypeSafe Jev service for typed decisions alongside channel-gateway and context-continuity plugins. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/contexera-dsh-jev.md).
- [@rbxts/jev (Jev for roblox-ts)](https://github.com/Lypt1x/rbxts-jev) - Typed TypeSafe Jev client for roblox-ts games: declare choice, score and noul questions about one state, and the option names become TypeScript types your server code branches on, with a shared rate limiter, retries and request deduplication over Roblox `HttpService`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rbxts-jev.md).
- [@sideform/browser-agent](https://github.com/dan-rozhkov/browser-agent) - TypeScript goal-driven browser agent library whose step policy uses TypeSafe Jev via a System One client. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sideform-browser-agent.md).
- [A Decision Model as a World Model](https://github.com/thddydgnl/jev-world-model) - Code, data, run logs and manuscript for a KIIS 2026 Fall Conference paper testing whether a frozen TypeSafe Jev decision model can serve as an LLM agent's world model in TextWorld, against zero-shot and LoRA-tuned Qwen3-4B in decision-style and generative set-ups; the author reports Jev near the oracle on task success without task training. English and Korean docs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-world-model.md).
- [Abide](https://github.com/coldteadotai/abide) - Enforce AGENTS.md/CLAUDE.md rules on every coding-agent edit/turn with TypeSafe Jev (or Vercel AI Gateway) per-rule probabilities on diffs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/abide.md).
- [ACP Bridge](https://github.com/xiwan/acp-bridge) - Bridge that exposes local coding agents over the Agent Client Protocol, with a virtual `auto` agent where TypeSafe Jev picks the best-fit agent per task with a confidence gate. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/acp-bridge.md).
- [adecider](https://github.com/Agents365-ai/adecider) - Typed System One decisions for coding agents: one call, many Noul/Choice/Score questions, pluggable local Laya / TypeSafe Jev / OpenAI-compatible backends. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/adecider.md).
- [adk-go-typesafe](https://github.com/craigh33/adk-go-typesafe) - TypeSafe System One Go client and Google ADK-Go function tool (OpenAPI-generated types; Apache-2.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/adk-go-typesafe.md).
- [Advocaat](https://github.com/pithings/advocaat) - TypeScript `ask` client that batches typed Jev choice, score, and yes/no questions about structured data, with optional Vercel AI Gateway support. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/advocaat.md).
- [Agent Browser (caglarkc)](https://github.com/caglarkc/agent-browser) - Research browser runtime for AI agents: observes pages through Lightpanda DOM controls, lets Jev choose the next action, and verifies outcomes independently, with reproducible benchmarks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/caglarkc-agent-browser.md).
- [Agent checks (routing, brief lint, plan and review gates)](https://github.com/jacob-mcclelland/agent-checks) - Claude Code hooks and scripts that put Jev judgements around AI coding agents: route each turn on purpose, lint briefs before dispatch, block inline edits to files assigned to a worker, check cited sources in research reports, and ask whether returned work shows a check actually ran. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-checks-jev.md).
- [Agent Chess (Claude vs Jev)](https://github.com/bakirgdev/agent-chess) - Local chess board styled after chess.com where Claude models and Jev play each other, with per-side engine settings, Jev move probabilities in the live view, a per-run spend cap and server-side legality checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-chess.md).
- [Agent Router](https://github.com/nidhi-singh02/agent-router) - Quota-aware Herdr launcher that uses TypeSafe System One (Jev) to pick Cursor/Claude Code/Codex/OpenCode model and effort after local eligibility rules. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-router.md).
- [Agent Seek](https://github.com/Gitmaxd/agent-seek) - Cheap web recall for agents: You.com discover plus TypeSafe Jev cascade ranking (REST/MCP/UI); does not write answers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-seek.md).
- [Agent Stack](https://github.com/korallis/agent-stack) - Local multi-agent software team stack where TypeSafe Jev makes small typed merge/assignment decisions (no root LICENSE at tip). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-stack.md).
- [agent-chaperone](https://github.com/agent-chaperone/agent-chaperone) - Calibrated firewall for agent tool calls: MCP proxy plus hooks adapter; TypeSafe Jev screening with shadow mode and a local judgment log. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-chaperone.md).
- [agent-desktop](https://github.com/lahfir/agent-desktop) - Rust macOS accessibility CLI for desktop computer use; optional jev-desktop skill/scripts use TypeSafe Jev for target/command choice without putting the a11y tree in agent context (BYOK). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-desktop.md).
- [agent-doc-search](https://github.com/beqaux/agent-doc-search) - Docs routing for coding agents: BM25 and a TypeSafe Jev search propose, a Jev yes/no check confirms relevance. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-doc-search.md).
- [agent-evals](https://github.com/marianoberton/agent-evals) - Deterministic LLM-agent eval harness with optional calibrated TypeSafe Jev judge for CI deploy gates. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-evals.md).
- [agent-fastpath](https://github.com/abhishekswe/agent-fastpath) - MCP decision layer for coding agents: deterministic rules then TypeSafe Jev ship/risk/triage/browser tools. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-fastpath.md).
- [agent-on-agent-ids](https://github.com/stanibaj/agent-on-agent-ids) - Intrusion detection for AI agents over Opik traces, with TypeSafe Jev as a supported low-cost judge. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-on-agent-ids.md).
- [agent-reflex](https://github.com/psygreg/agent-reflex) - Coding-agent plugin that uses a System One decision model (Jev, or a local model served by llama.cpp, Ollama or LM Studio) for a shell guardrail, post-edit checks, task complexity classification and a confidence check across OpenCode, Claude Code, Codex, Hermes, Qwen Code, Antigravity and Pi. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-reflex.md).
- [agent-test-pruning](https://github.com/olegpustovit/agent-test-pruning) - Scripts and data for the paper "Which Tests to Keep When a Coding Agent Writes the Test Suite": replays of Claude Code writing tests, planted and real bugs, and pruning signals including four facet questions asked of Jev per test. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-test-pruning.md).
- [agent-tree](https://github.com/s112076649/agent-tree) - Claude Code plugin that only watches: optional bottom HUD lines merged into your status line plus an on-demand `/agent-tree` pane showing the main agent and subagents, and — when you opt in — TypeSafe Jev reading a small session summary to draw four advisory flags (looping, stuck, fan-out wide, near done) and a health score. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agent-tree.md).
- [AgentRun](https://github.com/Parcha-ai/agentrun) - Workflow language for the agents you already run: define repeatable steps, let TypeSafe Jev make focused typed checks (`@parcha/agentrun-jev`), and call an agent only when work needs investigation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agentrun.md).
- [agrouter](https://github.com/SvetlovA/agrouter) - Go CLI that asks Jev which coding-agent CLI, model and reasoning effort (Claude Code, Codex or any configured CLI) a prompt needs, then prints the choice as JSON or launches that CLI with it. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agrouter.md).
- [agy-cua (background computer use for Antigravity)](https://github.com/saariuslystoned/agy-cua) - Unofficial MCP plugin that gives Google Antigravity (app and `agy` CLI) background computer use on macOS through the open-source Cua Driver, with an optional Jev lane that picks each guarded step in about 0.2 to 0.3 s and refuses secure fields and send or commit actions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agy-cua.md).
- [agy-jevgate](https://github.com/catpotd/agy-jevgate) - Fail-closed Antigravity `PreToolUse` hook for shell commands using TypeSafe Jev risk scores. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agy-jevgate.md).
- [AI PM Job Radar](https://github.com/aakashg/ai-pm-job-radar) - Every open PM role at 36 AI and tech companies, sorted by Jev (via Vercel AI Gateway) into AI PM without ML, AI PM with ML, non-AI PM and not-a-PM-role. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ai-pm-job-radar.md).
- [aidiff-gate](https://github.com/JohnRiceML/aidiff-gate) - Claude Code plugin: optional TypeSafe Jev judge flags flaky AI-written tests and trims walls of text. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/aidiff-gate.md).
- [Ailuros](https://github.com/0x4272616e646f6e/ailuros) - Ingress decision service for proxies: Envoy (`ext_proc`, `ext_authz`), NGINX `auth_request`, Traefik `ForwardAuth` and Envoy Gateway on Kubernetes send each request to Ailuros, which asks TypeSafe Jev or an OpenAI-compatible model, including local models, for an allow or block decision. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ailuros.md).
- [aiscript](https://github.com/illescasDaniel/aiscript) - CLI that turns a plain-English intent into a cached Bash or PowerShell script written by an LLM, then checks every generated script with a static scan and optionally Jev (or a local Ollaya model) before it is saved or run; risky scripts need an explicit approval. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/aiscript.md).
- [AIWG](https://github.com/jmagly/aiwg) - Multi-agent workflow framework for existing AI tools whose opt-in decision runtime can bind the same validated decision definitions to Jev or to an LLM subagent, with pins, projection, bounded retries, fallback and receipts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/aiwg.md).
- [ajevt-browser](https://github.com/XYenon/ajevt-browser) - Bounded TypeSafe Jev browser tool for Pi, OpenCode V2, Amp, and MCP powered by agent-browser (AGPL-3.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ajevt-browser.md).
- [Albanian sentiment analysis with Jev](https://github.com/uran-lajci/albanian-sentiment-analysis-jev) - Reproducible study classifying Albanian COVID-19 Facebook comments (AlbAna, three-annotator labels) with TypeSafe Jev and no training, re-running the 20 classifiers of Kastrati et al. (2021) on the paper's own test split; maintainer reports Jev at 75.71 weighted F1 vs 72.08 for mBERT, with paired-bootstrap intervals. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/albanian-sentiment-jev.md).
- [AlphaOptimizer](https://github.com/alpha-tales/alphaoptimizer) - Compact large Codex/tool outputs locally; optional TypeSafe Jev ranks relevant chunks (BYOK). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/alphaoptimizer.md).
- [Amplifier Jev provider (Microsoft)](https://github.com/microsoft/amplifier-module-provider-jev) - Optional, experimental provider module for Microsoft's Amplifier agent framework that exposes Jev decisions (predicate, choice and score) to consumers such as browser and computer UI use, without ever joining the chat fallback chain. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/amplifier-provider-jev.md).
- [AnchorLint](https://github.com/prantikmedhi/anchorlint) - Audits internal links in built HTML sites with deterministic checks plus optional TypeSafe Jev promise/relevance judgments. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/anchorlint.md).
- [android-jev (FZ2000)](https://github.com/FZ2000/android-jev) - MCP server/skill: drive an Android phone over adb with TypeSafe Jev choosing each next action (distinct from listed jev-android / jevdevice). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fz2000-android-jev.md).
- [anofox-decide](https://github.com/DataZooDE/anofox-decide) - DuckDB extension: NL predicates and answer sets via TypeSafe Jev or local open System One–compatible models (remote opt-in). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/anofox-decide.md).
- [AnyJev (Nokia Applied Research)](https://github.com/nokia-applied-research/AnyJev) - Turn open LLMs into Jev-style typed decisions with probabilities (L0–L2); independent of official Jev / TypeSafe hosted API. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/nokia-anyjev.md).
- [Apple RAG MCP](https://github.com/BingoWon/apple-rag-mcp) - MCP server for Apple developer documentation and WWDC transcripts: hybrid retrieval, then TypeSafe Jev ranks the merged candidates against the query's API, platform and version needs, falling back to a Qwen3 reranker; hosted with a free tier and paid Pro plan, or self-host the MIT source. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/apple-rag-mcp.md).
- [argmax](https://github.com/WalterGropius/argmax) - Unity swarm-programming RTS where four commanders, including TypeSafe Jev answering typed battlefield questions, emit the same decision type. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/argmax.md).
- [arxiv-relevance-watch](https://github.com/johnlam1968/arxiv-relevance-watch) - Zero-dependency Node CLI that screens arXiv for one research topic and ranks papers with five typed questions to a System One decision model (Jev via OpenRouter, TypeSafe or a local `/v1/systemone`), with no generative model anywhere and a second stage for reading survivors against your open items. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/arxiv-relevance-watch.md).
- [ask-jev (Aether-254)](https://github.com/Aether-254/ask-jev) - MCP + Codex/Claude plugin for TypeSafe Jev evaluate/batch/ping (Choice/Score/Noul). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/aether-254-ask-jev.md).
- [ask-jev (Go CLI and MCP server)](https://github.com/hrubymar10/ask-jev) - Standard-library Go CLI and MCP stdio server for TypeSafe's System One API: ask choice, yes/no (noul) and score questions or a batch, get JSON back, and keep a local outcome log. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hrubymar10-ask-jev.md).
- [ask-jev-skill](https://github.com/shantanugoel/ask-jev-skill) - Hermes skill that asks TypeSafe Jev for typed Choice/Score/Noul tiebreaks when paths remain plausible. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ask-jev-skill.md).
- [ask-jev-skill (DiegoSalazar)](https://github.com/DiegoSalazar/ask-jev-skill) - Claude Code skill plus a dependency-free Bun `jev` CLI (`yes`, `pick`, `rate`, `rank`, `triage`) that lets Claude offload cheap, repetitive judgments to TypeSafe Jev — which files to read, whether command output means pass or fail, which skill fits, filtering and ranking — so raw content stays out of Claude's context. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/diegosalazar-ask-jev-skill.md).
- [askif](https://github.com/asakaxgit/askif) - TypeScript toolkit for typed branching on Jev answers — `ask.if(…).elseif()`, `ask.switch`, `ask.score` and `.unsure()` bands — with a provider-neutral `Backend` contract, a mock backend for tests, and `@askif/jev` as the TypeSafe Jev bundle. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/askif.md).
- [askjev](https://github.com/pZacca/askjev) - Unofficial MCP server for TypeSafe Jev (local stdio or hosted Worker): agents get calibrated Noul/Choice/Score answers over held context. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/askjev.md).
- [assay](https://github.com/patkusch/assay) - Local open take on System One/Jev: typed answers with calibrated confidence from a small laptop model (stdlib Python). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/assay.md).
- [Astra-Ares](https://github.com/miuuyy/Astra-Ares) - Adaptive GPT-6 Astra reasoning-effort selection during Codex tasks via TypeSafe Jev (experimental patched Codex CLI). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/astra-ares.md).
- [astrbot_plugin_jev_router](https://github.com/maolbsMd/astrbot_plugin_jev_router) - AstrBot plugin: TypeSafe Jev picks the sub-agent (model, persona, tools) for each chat message. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/astrbot-jev-router.md).
- [astrbot_plugin_smart_wake](https://github.com/Mikachiyo/astrbot_plugin_smart_wake) - Chinese AstrBot plugin that lets a group-chat bot join conversations without being @-mentioned: topic keywords trigger a weighted dice roll, then TypeSafe Jev judges reply value × naturalness × heat × energy before waking the bot; Jev also debounces when a conversation ends and confirms 'please be quiet' requests to enter a muted state, with the last 200 decisions in the dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/astrbot-smart-wake.md).
- [atoma](https://github.com/atoma-run/atoma) - Agent coordination platform (hosted atoma.run or self-hosted, AGPL-3.0) that turns a request into checked work; TypeSafe Jev takes bounded decisions such as which agent or recipe takes a task and whether a plan is approved. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/atoma.md).
- [auth-audit-jev](https://github.com/ianlintner/auth-audit-jev) - Observation-only Python EventPlugin: TypeSafe Jev audits allowlisted OAuth/IAM authn/authz events and emits advisory alerts (never enforces). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/auth-audit-jev.md).
- [Auto Mode for Paseo](https://github.com/obetomuniz/auto-mode-for-paseo) - Paseo plugin: TypeSafe Jev (default) or local Laya selects Codex model, effort, mode, and speed per turn. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/auto-mode-for-paseo.md).
- [Auto TLDR](https://github.com/gruckion/auto-tldr) - Claude Code mod that follows a detailed answer with a self-contained TLDR request in the same conversation (handy with per-reply Read aloud); optional Jev filtering asks TypeSafe for the probability that a summary would materially help and submits only at 0.7 or higher, sending the completed answer only. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/auto-tldr.md).
- [AutoJev](https://github.com/thinkany-ai/autojev) - Desktop local model-router gateway for Codex/Claude Code/Hermes; optional OpenRouter Jev decision routing with local fallback (AGPL-3.0-only). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/autojev.md).
- [autoloop](https://github.com/Sanctum-Origo-Systems/autoloop) - Config-driven GitHub issue → triage → implement → review → PR pipeline for coding agents, with an experimental, off-by-default Jev shadow mode (`typesafe/jev-1.13` via OpenRouter) that evaluates triage and auto-merge decision points alongside the pipeline and only logs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/autoloop.md).
- [automodel](https://github.com/moukrea/automodel) - Claude Code proxy where Jev (via OpenRouter) picks the model and effort for each prompt, subagent and workflow step. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/automodel.md).
- [AutoRouter (claude-autorouter)](https://github.com/frapposelli/claude-autorouter) - Independent local gateway that routes each Claude Code request across Haiku, Sonnet and Opus after an evaluator rates it — a local Ollama `/v1/systemone` model by default, or hosted TypeSafe Jev with `--evaluator jev` — checking model compatibility and context capacity first. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/claude-autorouter.md).
- [Axeon JEV Router (OpenClaw)](https://github.com/axeon-at/openclaw-jev-router) - OpenClaw plugin that asks Jev to pick the model for each interactive turn (Luna, Sol or Astra by default) with owner-controlled `/autoroute` on, off or shadow, keeping host locks, fallbacks and thinking settings untouched. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openclaw-jev-router.md).
- [azir-dispatch](https://github.com/JosssphZhou/azir-dispatch) - herdr dispatcher where Jev picks agent, model, and effort per task, with a JSONL log and live terminal board. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/azir-dispatch.md).
- [Backdrop AI Provider TypeSafe AI](https://github.com/backdrop-contrib/ai_provider_typesafeai) - Backdrop CMS AI module provider for TypeSafe System One decisions and moderation checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/backdrop-ai-provider-typesafeai.md).
- [banterarena-engine](https://github.com/muse254/banterarena-engine) - Open Go scoring engine behind Banter Arena: pure-function banter and country scoring, attribution checks, and two-tier reply sentiment where free rules settle obvious replies and an optional TypeSafe Jev adapter judges whether a reply laughs, concedes or calls the joke weak; labelled yardstick in tests. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/banterarena-engine.md).
- [barmkin-mod](https://github.com/samfrmr/barmkin-mod) - Claude Code mods security layer: secret redaction on every tool call, untrusted-content taint tracking with an injection screen and a Rule-of-Two egress gate, MCP server allowlists and post-write semgrep — with a Jev System One client that asks two noul questions per screened result (does it try to instruct the agent? does it contain credentials?) against an OpenRouter- or TypeSafe-style endpoint you configure, falling back to heuristics with a circuit breaker. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/barmkin-mod.md).
- [basal](https://github.com/rkinas/basal) - Independent open typed-decision models for Polish and English (basal-1.5 mini 1.5B, 4.5B, max 11B) with an inference engine serving a `/v1/systemone`-style API and a public leaderboard that includes hosted Jev 1.13; not affiliated with TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/basal.md).
- [Baton Agent Orchestrator](https://github.com/Gane2506/baton) - Idea-to-reviewed-code orchestrator (`jev` CLI) where TypeSafe Jev dispatches Claude Code, Codex and OpenRouter agents per step. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/baton-agent-orchestrator.md).
- [Bayes of Our Lives](https://github.com/jimador/bayes-of-our-lives) - One Embabel agent with three interchangeable decision services: TypeSafe Jev, OpenAI, and local code. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/bayes-of-our-lives.md).
- [beam-cli](https://github.com/whyashthakker/beam-cli) - AgentBeam local monitoring/safety CLI for coding agents, with optional TypeSafe Jev action judging (disabled by default; AGPL-3.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/beam-cli.md).
- [bekko-system-one](https://github.com/hotchpotch/bekko-system-one) - Small System One decision models (17M–400M) for Yes/No, Choice, and Score (independent open weights). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/bekko-system-one.md).
- [Better TypeScript](https://github.com/andrueandersoncs/better-typescript) - Go-based TypeScript linter whose `semantic` mode asks TypeSafe Noul questions over bounded source spans for policy checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/better-typescript.md).
- [Beyond CLIP: decision models for industrial image QC](https://github.com/fmegahed/decision-models-for-industrial-image-qc) - Ongoing research study (R code, data and rendered analysis) comparing Jev and the open-weight Clef-Flash with few-shot CLIP for calibrated defect detection on five industrial image datasets, with a five-stage audit, refine, calibrate and freeze framework. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decision-models-industrial-image-qc.md).
- [bitrate-advisor](https://github.com/affirmitv/bitrate-advisor) - Live-stream encoder settings from telemetry/history using TypeSafe Jev (OpenRouter) inside a deterministic safety envelope. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/bitrate-advisor.md).
- [Blackrose](https://github.com/Dino-Kupinic/blackrose) - TypeSafe System One decision layer that returns allow/review/block before/after LLM generation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/blackrose.md).
- [BLADE](https://github.com/GXG-CS/BLADE) - Research code that localizes the decisive error step in failed multi-agent runs using TypeSafe Jev multiple-choice judgments. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/blade-failure-attribution.md).
- [blink](https://github.com/ellipsis-dev/blink) - Bun CLI that searches a codebase with TypeSafe Jev walkers (Choice over directory entries via `@typesafe-ai/sdk`); upstream licensing unspecified. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/blink.md).
- [Blueprint Support Ticket Triage](https://github.com/tridaai/blueprint-support-ticket-triage) - Trida AI open blueprint: calibrated typed decisions (TypeSafe Jev / LLM / offline) for support routing and priority, stopping at a human gate. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/blueprint-support-ticket-triage.md).
- [Bongard](https://github.com/AgentBull/bongard) - Encoder-decoder System One model returning calibrated probabilities (not text); independent of TypeSafe-hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/bongard.md).
- [Bookmark Finder](https://github.com/ismailucrn/bookmarkfinder) - Local CLI and HTTP API that searches your GitHub stars and X bookmarks: SQLite + BM25 (and optional embeddings) retrieve candidates, then Jev optionally reranks them with a 0-3 relevance score and confidence. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/bookmark-finder.md).
- [bouncer (Faycall1l)](https://github.com/Faycall1l/bouncer) - Programmable live-chat moderation in one ~30ms System One pass using Laya (Jev-compatible) with per-server rules. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/faycall-bouncer.md).
- [BoundedCode](https://github.com/akynte/boundedcode) - 8 GB GPU coding agent: local generation, required TypeSafe Jev decision plane, and Go-supervisor verification before accept. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/boundedcode.md).
- [Brave Jev MCP](https://github.com/romantcig/brave-jev-mcp) - Fork of the Brave Search MCP Server whose `brave_llm_context` results are screened by TypeSafe Jev against the purpose of the search, removing pages and passages that match keywords without answering the question before they reach the AI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/brave-jev-mcp.md).
- [Brier](https://github.com/Mangaba-ai/brier) - Local Portuguese-focused typed choice/score/noul decisions in Jev API form (Qwen3-1.7B + LoRA on MLX); independent of hosted TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/brier.md).
- [BrighTO Router](https://github.com/thusinh1969/BrighTO_Router) - Self-hosted Rust LLM gateway with OpenAI/Anthropic-compatible routes plus System One / Jev / DJEV / Laya decision routing, load balancing, team keys, and usage budgets. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/brighto-router.md).
- [brojev](https://github.com/barockok/brojev) - Go browser agent for Claude Code with its own CDP engine, where Jev (via OpenRouter) picks each action and target during a delegated browser sub-task. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/brojev.md).
- [browser-laya](https://github.com/wexare-ai/browser-laya) - Runs the open Laya System One decision model entirely in a browser tab (ONNX on WebGPU or WASM): an npm library `@wexare/laya-web` and a hosted playground answer typed choice / score / noul questions in the Jev request shape with no server or API key once the ~900 MB weights are cached. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/browser-laya.md).
- [btx-skill-jev-judge](https://github.com/bitranox/btx-skill-jev-judge) - Claude Code plugin that hands repeated judgments to TypeSafe Jev from a tested script (redaction, rate limits). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/btx-skill-jev-judge.md).
- [Bud Decision Studio](https://github.com/BudEcosystem/Bud-Decision-Studio) - Cross-platform desktop app and localhost server for open Jev-like System One models (Laya, Kev, Lev, Jev-Omni, …) that speaks TypeSafe's `/v1/systemone` format so Jev SDK code can switch base URL; independent of hosted Jev; no LICENSE file. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/bud-decision-studio.md).
- [Building with Decision Models](https://github.com/aaddrick/building-with-decision-models) - Unofficial agent skill that teaches coding agents to design for decision models (TypeSafe Jev, Cloudflare Clef, pplx-decider, OpenAI Decisions, Databricks `ai_decide`, Ollama models and more): typed questions, calibrated confidence, per-provider reference files and a prior-art library of 450+ community projects sorted by pattern. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/building-with-decision-models.md).
- [Business Insights v2 (bi2)](https://github.com/haneeshraj/business-insights-v2) - CLI and MCP server that finds a business's name, logo, merchant category code, head-office address, phone, email and social links from its website, rules first and Jev and Gemini last, with a status, confidence and evidence for every answer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/business-insights-v2.md).
- [cai](https://github.com/ad-si/cai) - Rust CLI for AI tasks across many providers that adds TypeSafe Jev subcommands (`cai noul`, `choice`, `score`, `jev`) for typed questions about text piped on stdin. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cai.md).
- [Cairn Jev Lab](https://github.com/Cairn-ink/cairn-jev-lab) - Experimental memory-admission lab: TypeSafe Jev judges source-backed proposed memories; policy recommends save/skip/defer with recorded demos. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cairn-jev-lab.md).
- [Calendar Curator (Home Assistant)](https://github.com/poesterlin/calendar-curator) - Home Assistant custom integration that reads events from your calendars, asks Jev whether each one matches a criteria prompt, and mirrors the matches into a target calendar, with caching, reconciliation and a deletion cap. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/calendar-curator.md).
- [call-eval](https://github.com/henryzhangpku/call-eval) - Post-call customer-experience evaluation for contact-centre transcripts: Jev (or an offline rule extractor) pulls typed facts with evidence spans, deterministic code turns them into satisfaction and sentiment scores, and a calibration layer anchors scores to surveys — all on synthetic data with a sealed holdout. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/call-eval.md).
- [Callpoint](https://github.com/rcmeadow/callpoint) - Incident decision-support demo where TypeSafe Jev judges live facts and deterministic code makes the call. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/callpoint.md).
- [Camunda Jev AI Decision Connector](https://github.com/rajeshponna/camunda-jev-ai-decision-connector) - Camunda 8 connector for TypeSafe Jev: typed noul/choice/score decisions in BPMN over lists of any size. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/camunda-jev-ai-decision-connector.md).
- [Can you fool Jev?](https://github.com/huseyinbabal/can-you-fool-jev) - 1,000 English trick questions sent as identical `/v1/systemone` requests to Jev, Nimble 9B, tev1 4B and Strands Decider 2B, with the dataset, every request body, per-model answers, a runner for any `/v1/systemone` endpoint and a video walkthrough. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/can-you-fool-jev.md).
- [Candidate Experience Feedback Benchmark](https://github.com/adambkovacs/candidate-experience-benchmark) - Compare TypeSafe Jev vs LLMs on 60 synthetic candidate-experience reviews (four typed classifications). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/candidate-experience-benchmark.md).
- [Canny](https://github.com/qkal/Canny) - Claude Code/Codex warden: append-only ledger and deterministic done-gate; optional TypeSafe Jev advises but never alone blocks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/canny.md).
- [Canopy-Jev](https://github.com/Camellia86/Canopy-Jev) - Tree-based decision model on Qwen with shared context and isolated branches (independent System One–style research). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/canopy-jev.md).
- [CanvasTTY Assistant](https://github.com/BIackFIame/canvastty-plugin-assistant) - CanvasTTY plugin: System One (Jev/Laya/Eikos) command review, launch triage, and orchestrator advice (requires plugin API v2). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/canvastty-plugin-assistant.md).
- [cap-jev (SAP CAP plugin)](https://github.com/hendrik77/cap-jev) - Plugin for SAP CAP Node.js applications that adds an internal `system-one` service for choice, score and yes/no questions against one text or JSON state, defaulting to TypeSafe Jev and working with other Jev-compatible System One endpoints. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cap-jev.md).
- [Captain Code](https://github.com/lemma-ventures/captaincode) - One terminal that routes tasks across Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, and opencode models, with an optional Jev decision leg for triage, an action gate, and supervision. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/captaincode.md).
- [catherd](https://github.com/47vigen/catherd) - Herds coding agents from your Claude Code session: Claude plans and verifies, Codex/opencode write code, TypeSafe Jev picks the model. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/47vigen-catherd.md).
- [changelog-bot](https://github.com/nyaomaru/changelog-bot) - AI changelog CLI and GitHub Action with an experimental `--why-engine jev`: Jev checks each PR-description candidate for an explicit reason that applies to the change, and the strongest accepted text is quoted without paraphrase. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/changelog-bot.md).
- [chatwoot-workers](https://github.com/Phala-Network/chatwoot-workers) - Cloudflare Workers for Chatwoot: Discord relay plus ticket router that triages new tickets with TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/chatwoot-workers.md).
- [Chinese-Jev](https://github.com/gulucaptain/Chinese-Jev) - Chinese-language System One–style typed-decision training and eval pipeline (CJ-Bench/CJ-Dataset); pretrained weights planned, not bundled (independent of hosted TypeSafe Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gulucaptain-chinese-jev.md).
- [chinese-workflow-decision-bench](https://github.com/Adkid-Zephyr/chinese-workflow-decision-bench) - Feishu-style Chinese message classification bench: 64 frozen scenarios, Choice/four-Noul workflows, published TypeSafe Jev vs Laya results. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/chinese-workflow-decision-bench.md).
- [Chirp (Tern)](https://github.com/Noctivoro/tern-chirp) - Tiny robot voice for the Tern terminal: when a coding agent finishes or waits for you, Jev reads the end of its last message and answers six typed questions (mood, voice, pitch, pace, energy, and whether you're being asked something), and a small Luau synthesizer turns the probabilities into one short beep; without a key it falls back to a local reading. Inspired by Yohei Nakajima's Beep Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tern-chirp.md).
- [Choice Four](https://github.com/jevplays-games/jev-connect-four) - Four-in-a-row against Jev: the rules engine finds legal moves and tactical facts, and every Jev turn is a structured Choice over the remaining columns, with server-authoritative matches, Discord sign-in, leaderboards and a hash-linked move journal. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/choice-four.md).
- [ci-triage (samiulhuda360)](https://github.com/samiulhuda360/ci-triage) - GitHub Action that classifies failed CI with TypeSafe Jev: re-runs flaky/infra once and comments real failures on the PR (distinct from criguex/jev-ci-triage). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/samiulhuda360-ci-triage.md).
- [Cite](https://github.com/mdepolli/cite) - Elixir library that finds what you describe in a document — failures in a log, hallucinations in a transcript — by gathering candidate passages with fixed rules in code and having a decision model, via the bundled `Cite.Provider.TypeSafe` (Jev), judge each one; questions are written in a Spark DSL. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cite.md).
- [ck-browser (plain-language browser checks)](https://github.com/luv-agarwal/ck-browser) - Runs browser checks written as plain-language steps in a real Chrome, with Jev picking each element from a DOM snapshot and deciding whether the page has settled, and writes per-step probabilities, timings, cost and screenshots. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ck-browser.md).
- [Claims, Choices and Labels (jev-eval)](https://github.com/Clementtang/jev-eval) - Audit of TypeSafe Jev and five generative models on Taiwan-status claims, forced choices and labels; draft preprint, item explorer and replays. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-eval.md).
- [Classifier Arena](https://github.com/Malleable-ai/classifier-arena) - Three small browser games (a real-time fighter, a spam and smishing lane game, and a reverse-dictionary clue game) where Jev and OpenAI Decisions play head to head on one shared classifier core, with every match logged and replayable. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/classifier-arena.md).
- [classifier.dev](https://github.com/mrmps/classifier-dev) - Zero-shot text classification over plain HTTP, a CLI, and MCP: a Cloudflare Worker packs inputs into TypeSafe Jev requests and returns a label with calibrated confidence. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/classifier-dev.md).
- [Claude Subagent Router](https://github.com/robertanton81/claude-subagent-router) - Claude Code plugin with seven subagent workers, a review skill and a Codex CLI bridge; optional model routing sends Agent-call briefs to Jev and switches model when it is confident. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/claude-subagent-router.md).
- [Claude x Jev](https://github.com/charlesdove977/claude-x-jev) - Claude Code skill/commands that use TypeSafe Jev on OpenRouter for classify/route/gate so Claude only deep-reads unsure items. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/claude-x-jev.md).
- [claude-auto-effort](https://github.com/justmytwospence/claude-auto-effort) - Claude Code mod where TypeSafe Jev rates, in about 200 ms before each turn, how much careful reasoning a prompt needs (0–3) and sets the effort level with smoothing; Claude Code port of pi-auto-effort sharing its questions, policy and settings file. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/claude-auto-effort.md).
- [claude-code-jev](https://github.com/RahulBalakavi/claude-code-jev) - Claude Code PreToolUse permission gate: TypeSafe Jev via OpenRouter Decisions classifies allow/block/ask, with fixture latency/cost benchmarks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/claude-code-jev.md).
- [claude-jev (darwintechlab)](https://github.com/darwintechlab/claude-jev) - Claude Code plugin/MCP for live TypeSafe Jev Choice/Noul/Score tools with confidence auto/escalate labels. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/darwintechlab-claude-jev.md).
- [claude-jev-advisor](https://github.com/delt96/claude-jev-advisor) - Unofficial Claude Code command hooks using TypeSafe Jev: a `context` helper that suggests `/compact` or `/clear` at natural stopping points, and (on Windows) an `rm` helper that asks before real files are deleted but lets Jev clear deletes of session-made or git-ignored files; README lists exactly what is sent to Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/claude-jev-advisor.md).
- [claude-jev-funnel](https://github.com/Shakibuzzaman3104/claude-jev-funnel) - Claude Code plugin + zero-dep CLI: bulk TypeSafe Jev judgments; only the uncertain band goes to Claude/human. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/claude-jev-funnel.md).
- [claude-jev-router](https://github.com/sutan13/claude-jev-router) - Claude Code plugin (Indonesian README) that asks Jev three questions per prompt to pick the cheapest sufficient Claude tier and thinking effort, with a prompt-cache guard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/claude-jev-router.md).
- [claude-model-guard](https://github.com/mcowdery/claude-model-guard) - Claude Code hooks plus a CLI picker where TypeSafe Jev chooses the model tier (Haiku, Sonnet, Opus or a long-form tier) for every prompt and can block a prompt sent to the wrong model when it confidently disagrees; tier criteria are editable and there are no dependencies beyond Node's `fetch`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/claude-model-guard.md).
- [claude-referee](https://github.com/ismaildasci/claude-referee) - Claude Code referee/guard plugin that asks TypeSafe Jev before risky tool actions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/claude-referee.md).
- [claude-risk-router](https://github.com/riskybiz7/claude-risk-router) - Risk-aware Claude Code model routing: TypeSafe Jev answers typed risk questions; rules pick Opus/Sonnet/Haiku. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/claude-risk-router.md).
- [claude-router (alexei-led)](https://github.com/alexei-led/claude-router) - Claude Code Mod that sets model and effort per turn, advised by TypeSafe Jev (default) or another classifier; no local proxy. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/alexei-led-claude-router.md).
- [ClauJev (Jev checks for Claude Code)](https://github.com/Civalski/claujev) - Claude Code plugin that asks Jev short typed questions on six hooks to triage each request, pick the reasoning effort, guard destructive or credential-touching commands, review edits against plain-language rules and block an unfinished "done". [Project guide](community/projects/tools/claujev.md).
- [Clay JEV People Ranker](https://github.com/promptgtm-shared/clay-jev-people-ranker) - Clay CLI people search plus TypeSafe Jev Choice/Noul qualification for semantic role fit (bundled operating-founder example). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/clay-jev-people-ranker.md).
- [clear-head](https://github.com/VladyslavHontar/clear-head) - Claude Code Stop hook that judges answer claims against session tool evidence with TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/clear-head.md).
- [clef-mcp](https://github.com/kanedayo/clef-mcp) - MCP server (Japanese docs) that lets Claude Code ask Cloudflare Clef — a Jev / System One API-compatible decision model hosted on Ollama — for typed decisions: one `decide(state, questions, images?)` tool wraps Ollama's `POST /v1/systemone` with `noul` / `choice` / `score` questions (up to 64) and optional local images, plus a `health()` check. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/clef-mcp.md).
- [clef-webcam](https://github.com/lucataco/clef-webcam) - Runs Cloudflare's open-weight clef-flash decision model locally on an Apple Silicon Mac and turns live webcam frames into typed decisions (noul / choice / score) using the same question schema as Jev / System One, with Metal kernels that cut a webcam frame with three questions from ~840 ms to ~250 ms on the author's M5 Max. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/clef-webcam.md).
- [cleffa](https://github.com/zknpr/cleffa) - Native C+Metal Apple Silicon engine for Cloudflare Clef/Clef-Flash with Jev/System One request/response API (MIT; independent of Cloudflare). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cleffa.md).
- [clickhouse-jev](https://github.com/ahwlsqja/clickhouse-jev) - Ask ClickHouse rows plain-language questions through executable UDFs backed by TypeSafe Jev (inspired by pg-jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/clickhouse-jev.md).
- [CLM (Contrastive Language Models)](https://github.com/Contrastive-LM/CLM) - Independent open System One model (CLM-8B: Qwen3-8B embeddings plus a small contrastive state/action head) served behind a TypeSafe-compatible `POST /v1/systemone`, so Jev-style requests replay unchanged; not affiliated with TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/contrastive-lm.md).
- [CloakBrowser-Agent](https://github.com/CloakHQ/CloakBrowser-Agent) - Stealth browser agent where TypeSafe Jev decides each step and CloakBrowser executes (MCP/CLI/Python). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cloakbrowser-agent.md).
- [cmd-mod-jev-guard (Jev auto mode for Command Code)](https://github.com/felinae98/cmd-mod-jev-guard) - Command Code mod that risk-scores every tool call with Jev and answers deny, ask or allow, denies calls that follow instructions planted in content the agent read, scans tool results for prompt injection and sweeps instruction files. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cmd-mod-jev-guard.md).
- [cmd-mod-jev-nudge](https://github.com/CommandCodeAI/cmd-mod-jev-nudge) - Command Code mod: TypeSafe Jev nudges the agent to continue when stop leaves unfinished work. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cmd-mod-jev-nudge.md).
- [Cobalt Cockpit](https://github.com/echelong/cobalt-cockpit) - Mission control for Claude Code (HUD, verification gates, Run Ledger) with an optional per-session TypeSafe Jev advisory router. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cobalt-cockpit.md).
- [Code Quality (smixs)](https://github.com/smixs/code-quality) - Advisory code-quality report and red-flag hooks for Claude Code, Codex, pi, omp, OpenCode and Grok, with an optional TypeSafe Jev classifier that asks calibrated yes/no questions about added test and source hunks (never blocks). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/code-quality.md).
- [codec-swarm](https://github.com/malfarom29/codec-swarm) - Local single-user LangGraph orchestrator that takes a Jira ticket to a reviewed local PR with a swarm of Claude Code agents (specifier, architect, coder, reviewer, hardener, QA, judge) in per-repo worktrees; with Jev enabled, every approved Gherkin scenario and every uncovered ticket criterion is scored in the judge step, and risky actions (push, migrations, `terraform apply`) always ask you. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/codec-swarm.md).
- [codeforces-tag-prediction](https://github.com/msobroza/codeforces-tag-prediction) - Leak-free multi-label Codeforces tag benchmark comparing TypeSafe Jev to zero-shot LLMs and encoders. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/codeforces-tag-prediction.md).
- [codegraph-jev](https://github.com/snemesh/codegraph-jev) - Research log: can a code graph plus TypeSafe Jev match a coding agent at finding and ordering functions? [Project guide](community/projects/tools/codegraph-jev.md).
- [Codex Jev integration](https://github.com/monkey1sai/codex-jev-integration) - Standard-library Python MCP server for Codex CLI and App providing advisory role and resource selection with Jev: six tools (`jev_status`, `jev_route`, `jev_decide`, `jev_select_resources`, and two outcome reporters) send task goal, constraints and eligible candidates as `choice` questions to `jev-latest`, with deterministic review gates, local validation and confidence fallback; it never executes, launches, switches models or approves. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/codex-jev-integration.md).
- [Codex Jev Preflight](https://github.com/wellkilo/codex-jev-preflight) - Fail-open Codex UserPromptSubmit hook: TypeSafe Jev injects advisory task_type/complexity/risk/execution_mode. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/codex-jev-preflight.md).
- [Codex Jev Router (suenot)](https://github.com/suenot/codex-jev-router) - Uses Jev Choice and Noul to choose Codex subagent model and reasoning effort, with local confidence gates and a Sol fallback. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/codex-jev-router-suenot.md).
- [Codex-Jev](https://github.com/PhilippElhaus/Codex-Jev) - VS Code Codex plugin that shortens noisy tool results with Jev-gated evidence selection before Codex reads them. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/philippelhaus-codex-jev.md).
- [codex-jev-router](https://github.com/tiandee/codex-jev-router) - Local Codex CLI bridge: TypeSafe Jev selects model and reasoning effort per turn via a loopback Responses proxy (fail-open without a key). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/codex-jev-router.md).
- [codex-route-observer](https://github.com/jeancsil/codex-route-observer) - Local proxy that observes Codex/OpenAI-style coding-agent traffic and uses TypeSafe Jev to categorize current request usage for a dashboard of routes, models, and spend-ish signals. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/codex-route-observer.md).
- [codex-router](https://github.com/alexei-led/codex-router) - Codex CLI model router that picks the model and reasoning effort for each turn (optionally via a System One classifier such as Jev) and shows the choice in Codex's footer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/codex-router.md).
- [codex-triage](https://github.com/WesleySmits/codex-triage) - Local Codex task triage with human-reviewed archiving and optional TypeSafe Jev analysis. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/codex-triage.md).
- [Codify (cg)](https://github.com/Sidiora-Labs/codify) - Agent workflow CLI (`cg`) with a spec engine, code graph, and memory; `cg jev` asks TypeSafe Jev for typed judgments such as memory classification, flaky-failure triage, and finding ranking. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/codify.md).
- [Coding Router Jev](https://github.com/guanghuang/coding-router-jev) - TypeScript/Bun launchers `codex-jev` and `claude-jev` plus a Pi `jev/auto` virtual model that route each coding-agent user turn through TypeSafe Jev to pick a model tier (Fast, Balanced, Strong, Long) and reasoning effort, with confidence-capped local policy. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/coding-router-jev.md).
- [cog-typesafe](https://github.com/cogcloud-ai/cog-typesafe) - CogCloud provider Cog: TypeSafe Jev as an explicit versioned `system-one/decisions` binding. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cog-typesafe.md).
- [College Event Moderator](https://github.com/Saiguru2554/college-event-moderator) - Benchmark and local playground that screens college chapter event proposals with Jev or a local s1ced or Vega model: five shared questions per event, expected answers, and a report of accuracy, confidence, latency and cost. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/college-event-moderator.md).
- [ComfyUI-ScriptFlow](https://github.com/kantan-kanto/ComfyUI-ScriptFlow) - ComfyUI safe script node with TypeSafe Jev yes/no/choice/score decisions (local GGUF LLM fallback) for workflow branching. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/comfyui-scriptflow.md).
- [compact-adviser](https://github.com/kunchenguid/compact-adviser) - Agent plugin that asks TypeSafe Jev whether the session is at a safe `/compact` boundary (hint or optional auto-compact). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/compact-adviser.md).
- [Compass (ring29 labs)](https://github.com/ring29-labs/compass) - zsh helper that keeps your native completions and adds a plain-English request: type a CLI prefix plus what you want (e.g. `aws ec2` and 'list all instances in table format') and Compass gathers candidates from completions, shell history and local help, then asks TypeSafe Jev to rank them; the chosen command is inserted for review, never executed. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ring29-compass.md).
- [Competitor Monitor with Jev (Mastra template)](https://github.com/mastra-ai/template-competitor-monitor-jev) - Official Mastra template that watches competitor pricing, changelog, and docs pages, diffs them against saved history, has TypeSafe Jev classify each change, and applies a policy to ignore, record, request review, or alert, with an LLM explaining results. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/competitor-monitor-jev.md).
- [Conjevture](https://github.com/nibzard/conjevture) - Exact logic for uncertain answers: TypeScript library combining Boolean rules with Jev Noul/Choice probabilities (zero runtime deps; MIT). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/conjevture.md).
- [Conjure (decision model art-directs Three.js scenes)](https://github.com/captnocap/conjure) - Type an idea or mood and a decision model picks worlds, things, looks and directions from a catalogue of procedural Three.js components, then builds and prints the scene in seconds; works with Jev directly or other System One hosts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/conjure.md).
- [Connector for TypeSafe Jev (WordPress)](https://github.com/juanlentino/connector-for-typesafe-jev) - WordPress plugin that gives themes and plugins a typed way to ask Jev about content and branch on a probability, a named choice or a score with confidence, with the key managed by WordPress core's Connectors API; a companion plugin backs comment moderation with it. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/connector-for-typesafe-jev.md).
- [content-evals (agent skill)](https://github.com/Automatify-Pty-Ltd/content-skills) - Agent skill that breaks a content requirement into atomic yes/no questions, builds a YAML scoring profile, scores a document with Jev and turns low scores into concrete edits. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/content-evals-skill.md).
- [context-budget](https://github.com/samiulhuda360/context-budget) - Context-compaction toolkit that can rank relevance with TypeSafe Jev (`/v1/systemone` on `api.typesafe.ai`) to cut most of what agents send upstream while measuring answer quality on a benchmark. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/context-budget.md).
- [contextleleo](https://github.com/Adityakk9031/contextleleo) - Move coding-agent sessions between tools with TypeSafe Jev scoring which context the next task needs (built on txcript). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/contextleleo.md).
- [Copilot Studio × Jev](https://github.com/Zakariakhchiche/copilot-studio-jev) - Copilot Studio/Power Platform: MCP gates Azure AI Search passages with TypeSafe Jev yes/no questions; plus a TypeSafe custom connector (MIT). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/copilot-studio-jev.md).
- [corp-jev-skill](https://github.com/bandersong/corp-jev-skill) - Claude Code `/corp-jev` skill that runs a corporation of review departments with TypeSafe Jev second opinions via OpenRouter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/corp-jev-skill.md).
- [could-jev](https://github.com/rlfordon/could-jev) - Claude skill that gives a ten-line gut-check on whether TypeSafe Jev could help with what you are building: verdict, matching pattern from 31 tagged Jev patterns, a state/questions sketch, closest prior, pitfalls and the cheapest test; it does not call Jev itself. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/could-jev.md).
- [CourtVision (basketball computer vision with a Jev decision layer)](https://github.com/aiden-guan/courtvision) - Real-time basketball pipeline that turns single-camera video into verified stats, then uses Jev to classify play type and moment stakes, rank coach cards and guard Claude's narration lines, with an operator dashboard over WebSockets. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/courtvision.md).
- [cpa-plugin-typesafe](https://github.com/EcoRayDev/cpa-plugin-typesafe) - CLIProxyAPI plugin exposing TypeSafe Jev typed API with OpenAI compatibility and private settings. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cpa-plugin-typesafe.md).
- [craft-cli](https://github.com/pa1ar/craft-cli) - Unofficial local-first CLI and TypeScript library for Craft Docs, with an agent skills library stored in Craft; `craft lib pick --jev` can use TypeSafe Jev to choose which skills fit a request. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/craft-cli.md).
- [Creative Decision Making (cdm)](https://github.com/chrisnkuno/creative-decision) - Paper and Python library that combine calibrated decision-model judgments (Jev and peers) with decision theory: calibrate, pool, gate unknowns to verify, and pick options plus bounded bets on new ideas, with a benchmark on 237 real Jev judgments. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/creative-decision.md).
- [creator-teardown](https://github.com/ai-orchestrators/creator-teardown) - Claude Code/Cowork skill that tears down a creator account, labels posts/sentences with TypeSafe Jev, and drafts rebuilt variants for your brand. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/creator-teardown.md).
- [Crosstalk](https://github.com/Chaddacus/crosstalk-lab) - Lab for finding and fixing look-alike option confusion in typed-decision routers; live mode runs against TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/crosstalk-lab.md).
- [Cua jev-use](https://github.com/trycua/cua/tree/main/libs/cua-driver/examples/jev-use) - Composes a bounded Jev chooser with Cua Driver and an independently verified browser fixture. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cua-jev-use.md).
- [CUA-JEV (ZJU-REAL)](https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/CUA-JEV) - Jev-powered computer-use framework: adapters propose legal actions; Jev chooses action and channel; code guards and verifies. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cua-jev-zju.md).
- [Cygnet recipe](https://github.com/blockbrain-ai/cygnet-recipe) - Independent recipe that answers Jev-format typed decisions (`choice`, `noul`, `score`) with frozen Gemma-4-12B-it on unmodified vLLM via a one-token option-letter readout and a `/v1/systemone` shim; not affiliated with TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cygnet-recipe.md).
- [daf-jev](https://github.com/docxology/daf-jev) - Composable Python toolkit for TypeSafe Jev: question builders, confidence gates, evaluator, calibration, CLI, and optional MCP server. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/daf-jev.md).
- [dailypaper-skills](https://github.com/huangkiki/dailypaper-skills) - Agent-skills pipeline (Chinese-language docs) that finds new papers from HuggingFace Daily/Trending and arXiv, uses TypeSafe Jev to score topic relevance against your research interests, then has your agent write reviews and Obsidian notes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dailypaper-skills.md).
- [Damocles](https://github.com/AizenvoltPrime/damocles) - VS Code extension and desktop app embedding a multi-provider AI coding agent; with a TypeSafe (or OpenRouter) key, Jev runs its persistent-memory contradiction judge and memory reranking instead of a sub-call LLM. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/damocles.md).
- [Data Agent MNIST (ClickHouse)](https://github.com/ClickHouse/data-agent-mnist) - ClickHouse's harness for building an agentic-analytics benchmark from your own data warehouse, with two optional Jev tools: schema-retrieval ranking (one yes/no per table) and failure-mode labeling of graded runs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/data-agent-mnist.md).
- [datafusion-jev](https://github.com/hotdata-dev/datafusion-jev) - DataFusion SQL `prompt_jev` UDF: typed TypeSafe Jev Noul/Choice/Score over row text (bring your HTTP client). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/datafusion-jev.md).
- [Dataiku TypeSafe AI plugin](https://github.com/dataiku/dss-plugin-typesafe-ai) - Dataiku's DSS plugin that brings TypeSafe Jev into the LLM Mesh: a `Jev Question Set` block for Structured Visual Agents, a yes/no Jev guardrail, a Jev reranker and a Jev chat-completion model usable in prompt and Classify text recipes, with usage, cost and traces recorded by the Mesh. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dataiku-typesafe-plugin.md).
- [dbt_jev](https://github.com/smithclay/dbt_jev) - Classify warehouse SQL values with TypeSafe Jev (or OpenRouter→Jev) from dbt macros on DuckDB and ClickHouse. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dbt-jev.md).
- [Deadline Tetris](https://github.com/jagadeeswara-reddy-p/jev-demos) - Live benchmark where Jev, Clef, Laya, Strands Decider and LLMs play the same Tetris game while pieces keep falling: over four games Jev landed about 119 pieces per game with a 289 ms median answer and won all four, while slower models placed better moves but too late. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/deadline-tetris.md).
- [decide (jeffcampbell)](https://github.com/jeffcampbell/Decide) - Dependency-free Python 3.11+ CLI and library that asks System One models — hosted TypeSafe Jev by default, or `clef-flash` locally in Ollama — typed yes/no, choice and score questions about files and text; `decide triage` tells a coding agent which files are worth reading before they enter its context. Distinct from the listed *decide (vsekhar)*. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jeffcampbell-decide.md).
- [decide (vsekhar)](https://github.com/vsekhar/decide) - CLI to ask TypeSafe Jev typed decisions from the shell, scripts, and agent skills — no application code required (Show HN). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/vsekhar-decide.md).
- [DecideBench](https://github.com/choyiny/decidebench) - Benchmark of decision models (Jev and open alternatives) and LLMs measuring accuracy, cost per task and latency together on 400 contrastive decisions, with Pareto frontiers instead of a single ranking. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decidebench.md).
- [DecideKit](https://github.com/sameerkhan24/decidekit) - Typed decision policies for TypeScript/Python with Jev via OpenRouter or TypeSafe, offline fixtures, and explicit confidence fallbacks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decidekit.md).
- [Decider.jl](https://github.com/farrellm/Decider.jl) - Julia front end for decision models: ask Choice, YesNo and Score questions about some state and get calibrated probabilities keyed by your own `@enum` or `Symbol`s, with backends for TypeSafe Jev, OpenRouter decision models and the OpenAI Decisions API. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decider-jl.md).
