- [DecideX](https://github.com/dengyie/decidex) - Industrial System One decision foundation with TypeSafe Jev provider, confidence gating, and deterministic fallbacks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decidex.md).
- [DeciLabel](https://github.com/EvanProgramming/decilabel) - GitHub Action that classifies Issues and PRs with TypeSafe Jev (or OpenRouter / OpenAI Decisions) and adds only evidence-backed labels. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decilabel.md).
- [Decis](https://github.com/chaitin/Decis) - Self-hosted System One–compatible `/v1/systemone` server with open Laya/kev Docker images (independent of hosted TypeSafe Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/chaitin-decis.md).
- [Decision Engine for PHP (swisnl)](https://github.com/swisnl/DecisionEngine) - PHP library for typed, probabilistic Choice / Score / Noul decisions in application code, with TypeSafe Jev as the default engine and OpenAI/Anthropic as alternatives. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/swis-decision-engine.md).
- [Decision Index (apolinario)](https://github.com/apolinario/decision-index) - Reproduction kit for the Decision Index benchmark suite for typed decision engines (choice/noul): local or one HF Job; not affiliated with TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/apolinario-decision-index.md).
- [Decision Layer Benchmark](https://github.com/GOhaike/decision-layer-benchmark) - Rerunnable cost comparison of agent decision layers on OpenAI, Anthropic, Google and TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decision-layer-benchmark.md).
- [Decision Model Arena](https://github.com/dante01yoon/decision-model-arena) - Arena comparing TypeSafe Jev, OpenAI Decisions, Decision-1 and Laya on the same game states and questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decision-model-arena.md).
- [Decision Model Rust SDK](https://github.com/zchee/decision-model-sdk-rust) - Async Rust SDK for decision models served through the System One API (TypeSafe Jev and compatible vendors): typed question builders or `#[derive(QuestionSet)]`, one HTTP/2 connection per client, and an adapter crate for OpenAI/Anthropic/Gemini. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decision-model-sdk-rust.md).
- [Decision Model Workbench](https://github.com/BatuhanYilmaz26/decision-model-workbench) - Local-first workbench and guides for bounded decisions: call hosted decision models (TypeSafe Jev, OpenRouter, Perplexity, Cloudflare) with typed validation and fallbacks, or review data and train a CPU baseline domain model. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decision-model-workbench.md).
- [Decision Models Lab](https://github.com/shashikantdev3/decision-models-lab) - Hands-on lab for decision models: send the same support ticket and typed questions to TypeSafe Jev and Microsoft Decision-1 through OpenRouter, compare answers and confidences, and learn when to hand a case to Claude for careful reasoning. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decision-models-lab.md).
- [Decision models on Polish historical gazetteer entries (IHPAN)](https://github.com/IHPAN/modele-decyzyjne-test) - Evaluation by the Institute of History, Polish Academy of Sciences: 250 human-labelled fragments of a 19th-century Polish geographical dictionary scored by TypeSafe Jev (via OpenRouter), TEV1 and Basal against human relevance judgments, with data, scripts and raw outputs (Polish docs). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ihpan-modele-decyzyjne-test.md).
- [Decision Tagger (Obsidian)](https://github.com/xinye1017/obsidian-decision-tagger) - Obsidian plugin for System-1 note tagging via TypeSafe Jev with multi-key parallel batch classification. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/obsidian-decision-tagger.md).
- [decision-eval](https://github.com/NISH1001/decision-eval) - Benchmark harness for zero-shot decision models (choice / noul / score): accuracy, calibration, latency, and cost across suites, with a hosted Jev backend on OpenRouter (`typesafe/jev-1.14`) alongside local Laya/GLiNER and OpenAI Decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decision-eval.md).
- [decision-first](https://github.com/harrymunro/decision-first) - Agent skill that tries TypeSafe Jev on bounded-judgment steps first and documents every attempt in a reusable decision lab. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decision-first.md).
- [Decision-model referral on medical exams](https://github.com/mahlernim/decision-model-referral) - Code, recorded runs and manuscript for a preprint (Sangzin Ahn, 2026) comparing TypeSafe Jev, OpenAI Decisions and Cloudflare Clef on 435 KorMedMCQA and 1,273 MedQA questions, using each model's selected-option probability to refer its least confident 20% of answers to a stronger model versus random and oracle referral. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decision-model-referral.md).
- [decision-models-eval](https://github.com/sammy143/decision-models-eval) - Independent, pre-registered evaluation of SubQ Snap against TypeSafe Jev 1.13 and 1.14 on Decision Index benchmarks, including a long-context fairness test (whole documents vs chunks), with figures and confidence intervals. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decision-models-eval.md).
- [decision-server](https://github.com/PrashamTrivedi/decision-server) - Cloudflare Worker that answers Jev-format decision calls on free Workers AI Clef models first and falls back to Jev, with REST, a remote MCP server, budgets and a stats dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decision-server.md).
- [decision-shadow](https://github.com/jatingargiitk/decision-shadow) - Zero-dependency Python wrapper to shadow-test Jev against an existing LLM classifier, report agreement, tune thresholds, then switch over with fallback. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decision-shadow.md).
- [decision-toolkit](https://github.com/gia-uh/decision-toolkit) - Local-first toolkit from GIA-UH and Syalia for open-weights System One decision models: typed choice, yes/no, and score questions with calibrated probabilities on CPU, as a library, CLI, and REST server that speaks TypeSafe's System One contract. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decision-toolkit.md).
- [DecisionBench](https://github.com/KizitoNaanma/decisionbench) - Evaluation harness and dashboard comparing TypeSafe Jev (via OpenRouter) with Claude Sonnet 5 on the same structured decision tasks — 100 real `invoice_processing` cases with five sub-questions each — reporting accuracy vs gold, cost per 1k decisions, p50/p95 latency and a disagreement view; Jev led overall but Claude won duplicate detection (author-reported). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decisionbench.md).
- [DecisionKit](https://github.com/iamjonatha/decisionkit-dotnet) - Provider-independent .NET decision model: typed Choice/Score/Noul questions in the domain, TypeSafe Jev protocol in a separate package, DI and offline test doubles (pre-release). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decisionkit.md).
- [decisionkit (adityajha2005)](https://github.com/adityajha2005/decisionkit) - Provider-neutral decision CLI, MCP server and Claude Code plugin with a Jev backend for agents, scripts and CI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/adityajha-decisionkit.md).
- [decisions-mcp](https://github.com/GorOhanyan/decisions-mcp) - MCP server that lets coding agents scan large codebases with OpenRouter decision models (Jev by default) to locate bugs from symptoms and classify or filter files cheaply. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decisions-mcp.md).
- [decisionsmith](https://github.com/izam-mohammed/decisionsmith) - Use and fine-tune System One models (Jev, Laya) on your own data with an LLM as teacher: declare decisions as Pydantic fields and get a harness that calls Jev or a trained local student. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/decisionsmith.md).
- [Deeds](https://github.com/danielmiessler/Deeds) - CLI and Claude Code plugin that reads every commit diff and counts what actually got built (new, deepened, regressed, removed capabilities); TypeSafe Jev answers typed questions per commit and a fixed policy turns them into counts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/deeds.md).
- [Deep Recall](https://github.com/turlockmike/deep-recall) - Answer-aware recall over a folder of Markdown notes (Claude Code plugin + Python CLI/MCP) whose pluggable reranker can be TypeSafe Jev, scoring each passage window by the probability that it states the answer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/deep-recall.md).
- [DeepSeek Harness for VS Code](https://github.com/HarcoChen/dsh-vsc-integration) - VS Code extension for DeepSeek Harness with a built-in, optional Laya/Jev decision layer: loop guard, large-output shaping, done-gate, tool pruning, and skill routing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-vsc-integration.md).
- [deepseek-harness-jev (luobosibing2)](https://github.com/luobosibing2/deepseek-harness-jev) - DeepSeek Harness (DSH) plugin: TypeSafe Jev for skill/file ranking, supervision, corrections, and workspace approval (features off by default) — distinct from wjw66/deepseek-harness-jev-pre-compaction. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/luobosibing2-deepseek-harness-jev.md).
- [defrag](https://github.com/jaylamping/defrag) - Research tool for telling a coding agent's user when compacting is safe — at the quiet point between tasks, not when the context is nearly full: it extracts checkpoints from real sessions, lets you label them, and replays them against judges with TypeSafe Jev as the first production judge (plus OpenAI Decisions, rules, a threshold proxy or a local model) to measure which one to ship. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/defrag.md).
- [demo-expanso-jev](https://github.com/expanso-io/demo-expanso-jev) - Expanso Edge × TypeSafe Jev demos: fingerprint routine logs locally, ask Jev only on the remainder, route with hold-on-failure and live boards. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/demo-expanso-jev.md).
- [DepthJev](https://github.com/ZJUCQR/DepthJev) - Embodied navigation: RGB→depth/text facts; TypeSafe Jev chooses EB-Navigation actions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/depthjev.md).
- [Deqio](https://github.com/ILuce/deqio) - Local typed decision API (noul/choice/shared) with swappable engines/backends (Kev, Laya, Open-Jev, and others) and a lightweight UI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/deqio.md).
- [Dernier Abri (Last Shelter)](https://github.com/Pablositoo/dernier-abri) - French student 3D browser defence game where Jev estimates from a text description whether a night silhouette is a zombie or a survivor, and deterministic, tested code decides whether to fire, using Bayesian evidence fusion and a sequential test. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dernier-abri.md).
- [DESIGN:OS Generative UI](https://github.com/jangtrinh/design-os-generative-ui) - Streaming generative-UI runtime for agent webviews whose engine includes a TypeSafe Jev adapter (with a Laya-MLX path) for its decision cascade. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/design-os-generative-ui.md).
- [deslop](https://github.com/yoichiojima-2/deslop) - Agent skill/CLI that scores page bodies with TypeSafe Jev probabilities for ads, slop, SEO shape, and derivative content. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/deslop.md).
- [dev-decisions](https://github.com/adelvillar1/dev-decisions) - Verification and calibration layer for AI-aided development (v0.3.0): git hooks block secrets and classify diffs, an optional global ZCode hook blocks agent-run commits/pushes that fail the checks, and a CLI runs plan-contract gates and outcome grading — routing classification through a roster of decision models where TypeSafe Jev (`jev-1.13.0`) handles contract gates and fan-out next to GLiDE, local GLiNER2 and others. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dev-decisions.md).
- [Discern](https://github.com/doeixd/discern) - Effect Decision/DecisionModel patterns, policies, and procedures with an optional TypeSafe Jev provider (`@doeixd/discern`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/discern.md).
- [discoprint](https://github.com/lirantal/discoprint) - Classify an artist discography for theme, mood, and lyrical complexity with TypeSafe Jev and an Ink terminal dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/discoprint.md).
- [Discord Policy Checker](https://github.com/kennethwolters/discord-policy-checker) - Experimental Chrome extension that checks outgoing Discord messages against your policies with Jev, with optional Gemini rewriting. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/discord-policy-checker.md).
- [discourse-chatbot](https://github.com/merefield/discourse-chatbot) - The original Discourse AI Chatbot plugin; optional System One settings (default model `jev-latest`) let Jev decide whether a question belongs to the community and which tools the bot should use. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/discourse-chatbot.md).
- [Distill](https://github.com/samuelfaj/distill) - Lightweight coding-agent TUI with TypeSafe Jev (or OpenRouter decisions) for model/effort routing, utility tasks, and retention. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/distill.md).
- [DMLab TypeSafe Core (Jev for Magento 2)](https://github.com/dmlab-work/module-typesafe-core) - Magento 2 module that connects a store to TypeSafe's Jev with one encrypted key, a pinned model version, a monthly token budget, a response cache, retries and a circuit breaker, shared by DMLab AI modules such as Catalog Enrich, which fills empty product filter attributes from descriptions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dmlab-typesafe-magento.md).
- [doc-router](https://github.com/misbahsy/doc-router) - Routes PDF pages between local text extraction and OCR using optional Jev judgments, with a Rust CLI and Python bindings. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/doc-router.md).
- [DocJev](https://github.com/jerryjliu/docjev) - Document classification and splitting with TypeSafe Jev over LiteParse (optional LlamaParse) page text. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/docjev.md).
- [Docket (pg-jev Phase 0 benchmark)](https://github.com/ElyasEbrahim/docket) - Design and reproducible Phase 0 benchmark for durable, versioned, calibrated AI decisions in Postgres: measures jev-1.13 through pg-jev on 2,000 real complaint narratives for calibration, determinism and context effects. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/docket-pg-jev-bench.md).
- [docseek](https://github.com/mohojojo/docseek) - Goal-driven document crawler where a relevance judge (Jev is one built-in option) scores links, ranks pages, flags pages that hide documents, and picks filter values. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/docseek.md).
- [Document classifier (financial document types)](https://github.com/LandoCole/document-classifier) - Node app and benchmark that names a financial document's type (10-K, proxy statement, fund fact sheet, trade confirmation and 17 more) with one Jev Choice call, compared on 43 synthetic documents against frontier and small LLMs, a bi-encoder and keyword rules. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-document-classifier.md).
- [Doom in Claude Code (Jev pilots)](https://github.com/abelardodiaz/doom-in-claude-code) - Claude Code plugin that runs ViZDoom with Freedoom in a terminal pane: Jev looks at the game screen and picks the strategy about 2.5 times a second, Claude Haiku narrates in Spanish or English, and Python reflexes and an A* navigator handle aiming and the map. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/doom-in-claude-code.md).
- [dopp](https://github.com/doppsh/dopp) - Point Jev-shaped decision calls at one URL, keep every request, train a small model you own, run hosted/offline/browser—independent path. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dopp.md).
- [doublestrain](https://github.com/seanturner83/doublestrain) - Grades dependency advisories into SSVC Act, Attend or Track using whether the vulnerable code is reachable in your app, with a fixed decision tree and model judges (including an optional Jev classifier) held to measured acceptance floors. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/doublestrain.md).
- [Dowse (semantic find for Safari)](https://github.com/tinoosan/dowse) - macOS app and Safari extension that opens from the MacBook notch: describe a passage you remember, and Jev finds and highlights the matching text on the page so you can step through matches. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dowse.md).
- [DriveJev](https://github.com/benmagnifico/DriveJev) - Open System I autonomous-driving decision model (DriveJev-4B) that scores behaviours with probabilities in one forward pass, with JevPilot closed-loop harness. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/drivejev.md).
- [Drone Autonomy: AI vs Rules](https://github.com/subho004/drone-autonomy-ai-vs-rules) - Local benchmark that flies a simulated Crazyflie drone through adversarial events and measures collision rate against decision latency for Jev, other decision models, chat LLMs, rule-based pilots and a perfect oracle. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/drone-autonomy-ai-vs-rules.md).
- [drouter](https://github.com/timur-3c/drouter) - Experimental Rust decision API router that sends requests to Jev or an OpenAI-compatible teacher, caches verified answers and promotes audited shadow students. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/drouter.md).
- [DSH Decision Router](https://github.com/xinyang20/dsh-decision-router) - DeepSeek Harness plugin (Chinese docs) for automatic model selection: when "Auto" is chosen, a separate decision model reads each turn and picks one of 2–6 user-defined routes (provider, model, effort); decision models are local Laya, d1-3B / d1-omni-600M, or — after explicit consent — the System One cloud API (Jev), with an explicit fallback route on timeout or low confidence. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-decision-router.md).
- [dsh-auto-review-jev (sperictao)](https://github.com/sperictao/dsh-auto-review-jev) - DeepSeek Harness Auto-permission plugin: TypeSafe Jev reviews each tool call (distinct from xlennart/dsh-auto-review-jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sperictao-dsh-auto-review-jev.md).
- [dsh-backdoor-guard](https://github.com/miketako3/dsh-backdoor-guard) - DeepSeek Harness guard where TypeSafe Jev (or self-hosted d1) judges every model-requested tool call. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-backdoor-guard.md).
- [dsh-jev](https://github.com/noetion/dsh-jev) - DeepSeek Harness plugin registering `jev_ask` for TypeSafe Jev noul/choice/score (pin GitHub commit; npm name `dsh-jev` collides with another package). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-jev.md).
- [dsh-jev (iptton-ai)](https://github.com/iptton-ai/dsh-jev) - DeepSeek Harness plugin registering `jev_evaluate` for mixed TypeSafe Jev questions (distinct from noetion/dsh-jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/iptton-ai-dsh-jev.md).
- [dsh-jev-context-gate](https://github.com/vb2250158/dsh-jev-context-gate) - DeepSeek Harness plugin: data-driven preflight and evidence-aware review/skill gates with TypeSafe Jev (distinct from other dsh-jev* plugins). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-jev-context-gate.md).
- [dsh-jev-decide](https://github.com/nanami-0713/dsh-jev-decide) - DeepSeek Harness plugin registering `jev_decide` for TypeSafe Jev noul/choice/score (distinct from dsh-jev / dsh-jev-verify / dsh-jev-prune). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-jev-decide.md).
- [dsh-jev-gate](https://github.com/hemppp/dsh-jev-gate) - DeepSeek Harness (DSH) plugin for AgentTeams (Chinese docs) that checks each round's promised deliverables against a frozen baseline and evidence ledger before the team advances, with an optional `native-jev` decider (TypeSafe Jev) as an advisory or enforcing second opinion. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-jev-gate.md).
- [dsh-jev-goal-judge](https://github.com/kittimzhe/dsh-jev-goal-judge) - DeepSeek Harness add-on: TypeSafe Jev judges goal progress between rounds with budgeted nudges (shadow mode default). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-jev-goal-judge.md).
- [dsh-jev-interceptor](https://github.com/AskTheWay/dsh-jev-interceptor) - DeepSeek Harness plugin: TypeSafe Jev risk-gates tool calls (shadow→enforce) and optional session-reference scoring. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-jev-interceptor.md).
- [dsh-jev-kit](https://github.com/jackchen13755/dsh-jev-kit) - DeepSeek Harness plugin with ~23 named TypeSafe Jev advisory judgments (privacy/scope/memory/triage) on budgeted transport. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-jev-kit.md).
- [dsh-jev-loop](https://github.com/JWE24-code/dsh-jev-loop) - TypeSafe Jev judgments at DeepSeek Harness agent-loop gates (no UI dependency). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-jev-loop.md).
- [dsh-jev-plugin (jackie-cqz)](https://github.com/jackie-cqz/dsh-jev-plugin) - DeepSeek Harness plugin for TypeSafe Jev noul/choice/score with configurable guardrails and Web UI result cards (distinct from other dsh-jev* plugins). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jackie-cqz-dsh-jev-plugin.md).
- [dsh-jev-plugin (luobosibing2)](https://github.com/luobosibing2/dsh-jev-plugin) - DeepSeek Harness plugin wiring TypeSafe Jev into skill/file selection, supervision, corrections, and workspace approvals (distinct from other dsh-jev* plugins). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/luobosibing2-dsh-jev-plugin.md).
- [dsh-jev-prune](https://github.com/yangyu666/dsh-jev-prune) - DeepSeek Harness plugin: TypeSafe Jev keep/drop pruning plus deterministic receipt compaction (distinct from dsh-jev / dsh-jev-verify). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-jev-prune.md).
- [dsh-jev-thinking](https://github.com/kyan001/DSH-Jev-Thinking) - DeepSeek Harness plugin: TypeSafe Jev Choice sets reasoningEffort from the route's offered levels (one question per prompt; MIT). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-jev-thinking.md).
- [dsh-jev-verify](https://github.com/xienda/dsh-jev-verify) - DeepSeek Harness plugin with TypeSafe Jev `jev_decision` plus live `jev_verify` benchmark (no mock fallback; distinct from dsh-jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-jev-verify.md).
- [dsh-jev-zxh](https://github.com/TiChuXiXi/dsh-jev-zxh) - DeepSeek Harness plugin that exposes Jev as a model-callable `jev_decide` tool for typed decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-jev-zxh.md).
- [dsh-judgment](https://github.com/j62268781-alt/dsh-judgment) - DeepSeek Harness plugin that exposes one structured-judgment service: one state plus a batch of named choice, noul or score questions in, a batch of calibrated probability answers out, with validation, credentials, audit and metering. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-judgment.md).
- [dsh-opencli](https://github.com/IKEASven69/dsh-opencli) - DeepSeek Harness (dsh) plugin that drives your own logged-in Chrome or Edge through OpenCLI: 180+ site commands, generic browser primitives, approval gates on writes, watch jobs, and a System One decision layer (Jev or local Laya) for fast checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-opencli.md).
- [dsh-plugin-jev](https://github.com/cyberofficial/dsh-plugin-jev) - DeepSeek Harness (DSH) Web GUI plugin that gives the model a `jev_ask` tool for calibrated TypeSafe Jev judgments, exposes the same call to sibling plugins as a host service, adds an optional Jev command guard before tool execution, and shows per-chat usage in the browser. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-plugin-jev.md).
- [dsh-plugin-jev-compaction](https://github.com/YYTbit/dsh-plugin-jev-compaction) - DeepSeek Harness plugin: TypeSafe Jev scores messages for relevance-based compaction (pins constraints/tracebacks; falls back to age-based on failure; MIT). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-plugin-jev-compaction.md).
- [dsh-set-model](https://github.com/john-walks-slow/dsh-set-model) - DeepSeek Harness plugin where TypeSafe Jev auto-switches model presets and thinking depth each turn. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-set-model.md).
- [dsh-subagent-custom](https://github.com/xingzhen199186/dsh-subagent-custom) - DeepSeek Harness plugin for subagent model control with Jev-based intelligent dispatch. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-subagent-custom.md).
- [dsh-system1](https://github.com/exoticknight/dsh-system1) - DeepSeek Harness (dsh) package that gives agents a `system1_decide` tool and plugins a `ctx.system1.decide()` API for yes/no, choice, score and image-box judgments, with TypeSafe Jev, Laya and Cloudflare Clef providers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dsh-system1.md).
- [duckdb-jev](https://github.com/prasanthj/duckdb-jev) - Native DuckDB C++ extension for TypeSafe Jev semantic predicates, Choice classification, and Score rubrics in SQL. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/duckdb-jev.md).
- [DuoMind](https://github.com/j1s4nn/duomind) - OpenAI-compatible local LLM server that pairs a small local model (System 2) with TypeSafe Jev (System 1) for faster, more accurate coding-assistant answers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/duomind.md).
- [Edward](https://github.com/VeridicalTech/Edward) - Supervisor for unattended coding agents (CI, overnight runs) that wraps any subprocess with deterministic rules and an optional advisory scorer; `EDWARD_SCORER_BACKEND=jev` batches all probes into one TypeSafe Jev call per check. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/edward.md).
- [effortless](https://github.com/HeyCubit/effortless) - Claude Code mod that picks the reasoning effort for every prompt with a judge — Jev (TypeSafe key, about 0.25 s), Haiku on your Claude login, or your own OpenAI-compatible model — and shows a prompt-cache countdown with one-click Compact. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/effortless.md).
- [effortless-hermes](https://github.com/ivcained/effortless-hermes) - Hermes Agent port of effortless: a fast judge (Jev/TypeSafe, local model or custom) picks reasoning effort per turn by rewriting existing effort fields. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/effortless-hermes.md).
- [ego-decision-layer](https://github.com/jiangkoumo/ego-decision-layer) - Pluggable ego lite browser decision layer: one TypeSafe Jev System One call per step with fail-closed guards. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ego-decision-layer.md).
- [ego-jev](https://github.com/kimjisub/ego-jev) - macOS CLI that lets TypeSafe Jev pick each click and field in ego lite browser TaskSpaces from caller-supplied values, with requirement checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ego-jev.md).
- [ElBruno.AI.Decisions](https://github.com/elbruno/ElBruno.AI.Decisions) - .NET 10 decision SDK with one API for Choose, Score and Assess across Microsoft-Decision-1 on Foundry, local Ollama models and a native Jev package, plus Microsoft.Extensions.AI routing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/elbruno-ai-decisions.md).
- [English Net (prompt-injection detector of yes/no questions)](https://github.com/isaacbitran/english-net) - Prompt-injection detector whose seven neurons are English yes/no questions answered by Jev in one call; on a locked confirmation set it ties a single good Jev question on ranking, while every verdict names the questions behind it and a blind spot can be patched by adding one sentence. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/english-net.md).
- [Engraphis](https://github.com/Coding-Dev-Tools/engraphis) - Local-first, inspectable memory for coding agents (MCP, code-aware recall, WebUI) with optional advisory Jev decisions via BYOK or managed hosted plans. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/engraphis.md).
- [enowx](https://github.com/enowdev/enowxcli) - Early-release terminal coding agent (one Rust binary, specialist agents, any model provider) with an off-by-default decision model that can use TypeSafe Jev to decide brainstorm-vs-build, which specialist to delegate to, and when to ask the user. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/enowx.md).
- [estate-guardian](https://github.com/m-check1B/estate-guardian) - Overnight AI-agent fleet watchdog where TypeSafe Jev classifies the free-text failures rules miss. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/estate-guardian.md).
- [EuLLM](https://github.com/eullm/eullm) - Sovereign, local-first LLM engine (AGPL-3.0, Ollama/OpenAI-compatible APIs) whose v0.7.20 adds a `/v1/systemone` decisions endpoint that reads option probabilities straight from local models — e.g. its Jev-Style 2B — with an audit trail; no hosted Jev involved. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/eullm.md).
- [Eutrya](https://github.com/hellozenstrategist-lab/eutrya) - CLI-first agent (public alpha) with TypeSafe Jev in the decision loop for attention, candidate rubrics, and research micro-steps; offline `eutrya demo`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/eutrya.md).
- [Evaluating and Benchmarking the System One Model Jev](https://github.com/AppliedMachineLearning-Lab/jev-benchmarking) - Code for the arXiv paper by Deußer, Sparrenberg and Sifa (arXiv:2609.37647, 2026) benchmarking TypeSafe Jev (pinned `jev-1.13.0`) zero-shot on 37 public datasets against two open-weight LLMs scored on identical requests; raw responses are on Zenodo so every number can be recomputed offline. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-benchmarking.md).
- [evidence-referee](https://github.com/ismaildasci/evidence-referee) - Claude Code plugin that checks "done" claims and small judgment calls with TypeSafe Jev and keeps receipts (distinct from claude-referee). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/evidence-referee.md).
- [EvolveRoute](https://github.com/holny/evolve-router) - Local-first Rust LLM routing gateway that picks a model per request with Jev plus a heuristic fallback and logs every decision. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/evolve-router.md).
- [ex_typesafe_ai](https://github.com/iamtalha-arshad/ex_typesafe_ai) - Unofficial Elixir client for the TypeSafe AI API — typed structs, Req-based HTTP with retries, and ergonomic noul/choice/score questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/iamtalha-arshad-ex-typesafe-ai.md).
- [ExcelPilot](https://github.com/vikramlingam/excelpilot) - Live Excel agent with Qwen planning and TypeSafe Jev for intent routing, tool gating, and claim checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/excelpilot.md).
- [fast-browser](https://github.com/hamza-sahin/fast-browser) - Playwright browser automation for Codex/MCP with TypeSafe Jev (or Laya) decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fast-browser.md).
- [fast-compact](https://github.com/NodarDavituri/fast-compact) - `/fc` for Claude Code: TypeSafe Jev decides which old tool outputs stay whole; the rest are trimmed with cuts saved to disk. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fast-compact.md).
- [fast-jev-compaction](https://github.com/tamaratran/fast-jev-compaction) - Selects tool-call/result pairs to keep, truncate, or remove while preserving retained conversation content verbatim. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fast-jev-compaction.md).
- [fast-jev-compaction (Ayush)](https://github.com/AyushChauhan9389/fast-jev-compaction) - Claude Code plugin that replaces compaction summaries with Jev decisions over tool calls and results. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ayush-fast-jev-compaction.md).
- [fast-jev-compaction-opencode](https://github.com/dex-community/fast-jev-compaction-opencode) - opencode verbatim session compaction via probabilistic keep/drop judgments (local LM Studio judge by default; based on fast-jev-compaction). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fast-jev-compaction-opencode.md).
- [fast-jev-opencode](https://github.com/nrdz-labs/fast-jev-opencode) - OpenCode V2 plugin: TypeSafe Jev prunes stale tool calls/results on the outgoing request (fail-open; leaves history/`/compact` alone). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fast-jev-opencode.md).
- [fast-jev-opencode (roshan-shaik-ml)](https://github.com/roshan-shaik-ml/fast-jev-opencode) - OpenCode v1/v2 plugin: TypeSafe Jev prunes stale/bulky tool calls on the outgoing request (verbatim; distinct from nrdz-labs/fast-jev-opencode). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/roshan-shaik-ml-fast-jev-opencode.md).
- [fast-resume-scoring](https://github.com/dphbfs/fast-resume-scoring) - Score resume-to-job fit and requirement coverage with one TypeSafe Jev request in under a second. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fast-resume-scoring.md).
- [fastcampus-jev](https://github.com/teddylee777/fastcampus-jev) - Korean Fastcampus Jev tutorial: 13 notebooks + React/LangGraph demo (tool Choice, guardrails, risk gates, RAG) via OpenRouter (MIT). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fastcampus-jev.md).
- [FastGate](https://github.com/rajantripathi/fastgate-jev) - Multilingual EN/UZ/RU RAG helpdesk where TypeSafe Jev is the System One decision layer before the LLM writes, with an independent benchmark. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fastgate-jev.md).
- [Fastpunkt](https://github.com/Ritesist/fastpunkt) - Evidence-first research agent for Norwegian companies (built for the Builderr Signalpost challenge): given organisation numbers it returns one profile per company where every published fact links to its source with retrieval time; official registers anchor identity, and Jev (`jev-1.13.0`) is used only to select among candidates the code found (links, sentences, job titles) or to give a calibrated same-entity probability — no model-written facts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fastpunkt.md).
- [FAVA Trails](https://github.com/MachineWisdomAI/fava-trails) - Git-native, versioned memory for AI agents via MCP (Machine Wisdom AI): drafts are isolated, and since 0.8 the default Trust Gate asks TypeSafe Jev through OpenRouter one typed Noul question with an explicit threshold before a memory is promoted to shared truth. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fava-trails.md).
- [feelings](https://github.com/BoundaryML/feelings) - BAML `.feels()` / `.how()` / `.matches<T>()` typed AI-if methods powered by TypeSafe Jev; upstream licensing unspecified. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/feelings.md).
- [Figma Jev Review](https://github.com/gbesse/figma-jev-review) - Figma plugin that reviews selected interface copy against declared criteria and cites the exact weak TextNodes, so clicking a finding selects and zooms to the original layer; it never writes copy. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/figma-jev-review.md).
- [finance-decision-evals](https://github.com/vicwlau/finance-decision-evals) - Independent evaluation of Jev on ASC 606 revenue-recognition review decisions using synthetic contract clauses. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/finance-decision-evals.md).
- [firefox-jev-mcp](https://github.com/kitoutou999/firefox-jev-mcp) - Claude plans; TypeSafe Jev picks Firefox clicks via MCP + WebExtension bridge. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/firefox-jev-mcp.md).
- [Flick (flick-computer-use)](https://github.com/bgivenb/flick-computer-use) - Local MCP computer-use: agents hand Flick goals; TypeSafe Jev runs browser/macOS observe→decide→act. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/flick-computer-use.md).
- [flowtape](https://github.com/vvedantb/flowtape) - Records a user journey in a running Vite + React or Next.js app and exports it as JSON and a Claude Code prompt to replay and explore; Jev (TypeSafe) finds replay targets visually from screenshots. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/flowtape.md).
- [fmev (System One API on Apple Foundation Models)](https://github.com/mrubens/fmev) - Local server that implements TypeSafe's System One API (`POST /v1/systemone`, `GET /v1/models`) on the Apple Foundation Models model built into macOS, so existing Jev clients and SDKs work offline once you change the base URL. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fmev.md).
- [fndds-matcher-jev](https://github.com/braydenabo/fndds-matcher-jev) - Match food descriptions to USDA FNDDS codes via hybrid retrieval plus TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fndds-matcher-jev.md).
- [Footwork](https://github.com/Tom-R-Main/Footwork) - Dual-process browser agent: TypeSafe Jev as System 1 in front of browser-use System 2, with a code-owned arbiter and evidence verification (`jevdual`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/footwork.md).
- [Foreman](https://github.com/thruwire/foreman) - Supervises Codex workers with Jev assessments and deterministic steering, retry, and verification policy, with a synthetic demo. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/foreman.md).
- [forge (continuous engineering loop)](https://github.com/sudikama/forge) - Claude Code plugin that applies the autoresearch ratchet (one change, one commit, one measurement, keep or reset) inside a team-style process — spec, mapped scope, clarifying questions, tests beyond the happy path and human sign-off — with small tasks run through a test-driven harness and large ones driven to a measurable goal by an orchestrator, parallel subagents and a deterministic judge; Claude does the work while Jev makes cheap judgments such as triage, model routing and failure labelling. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/forge-claude-loop.md).
- [forged](https://github.com/colesmcintosh/forged) - Terminal tool that screens a PDF or image for signs of document fraud: it reads the file's metadata, renders pages to images, and asks Jev through Vercel AI Gateway for a fraud probability and the likelihood of each fixed fraud signal. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/forged.md).
- [Formanator](https://github.com/timrogers/formanator) - CLI/MCP for Forma benefit claims; optional TypeSafe Jev chooses benefit/category when you supply claim fields (receipt inference stays on LLM). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/formanator.md).
- [Founder CRM](https://github.com/shlok97/founder-crm) - Local SQLite CRM for an early startup's first customers, conversations and promises, with a dashboard, Claude over MCP to propose updates, and optional Jev checks on whether the evidence supports each proposed change before you approve it. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/founder-crm.md).
- [fr (Arindam200)](https://github.com/Arindam200/fr) - Ask a codebase question and get relevant files via live parallel TypeSafe Jev relevance. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/arindam200-fr.md).
- [Frame Engineering for Jev](https://github.com/nanagb/jev-frame-engineering) - Agent skill and scripts for designing and evaluating Jev decision frames: evidence in `state`, atomic questions, answer boundaries and application policy, with ablation, sweep and token-probe scripts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-frame-engineering.md).
- [Frost (model router built on Jev)](https://github.com/marcus/frost) - Go CLI from Haplab that takes a prompt or markdown task, has Jev assess it, then picks a model, harness and effort level locally from your own catalog, with explanations, alternatives and JSON output; decisions can be recorded and replayed offline. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/frost.md).
- [fusion-jev](https://github.com/Tlkh201313/fusion-jev) - Local coding evidence plus optional guarded TypeSafe Jev choices for MCP hosts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fusion-jev.md).
- [FuZev](https://github.com/PeisongXiao/fuzev) - Jev-compatible Choice, yes/no, and Score decisions alongside text generation on vLLM/ExLlamaV3 from one loaded model. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fuzev.md).
- [FYIsoft.Extensions.AI.Jev](https://github.com/FYIsoft/FYIsoft.Extensions.AI.Providers) - .NET `Microsoft.Extensions.AI` provider SDKs from FYIsoft, including a typed `JevClient` port of `@typesafe-ai/sdk` v0.6.0 and an `IChatClient` adapter for structured judgments, with a support-desk sample where Jev triages tickets and reviews Claude drafts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fyisoft-extensions-ai-jev.md).
- [game-rsi](https://github.com/gaohuan2020/game-rsi) - Real-game teacher correction and gated model evolution experiments with PlayJev configuration packs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/game-rsi.md).
- [Gatekeeper](https://github.com/AgriciDaniel/gatekeeper) - YAML rules plus TypeSafe Jev Choice route Claude Code prompts to the right skill/agent (allow/route/block bands). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gatekeeper.md).
- [Gatekeeper Editorial 30X](https://github.com/Michael-Liendo/gatekeeper-30x) - Hackathon web app (in Spanish) where Jev answers typed questions about each content piece and code routes it to auto-approve, LLM rewrite, human review or reject; a second flow sorts Luma event sign-ups into programs and decides whether to call them now. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gatekeeper-30x.md).
- [Gavel](https://github.com/shivanathd/gavel) - Salesforce packages for governed Choice/Score/Noul judgments with TypeSafe Jev, org policy, and an immutable ledger (v1 pre-release). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gavel.md).
- [gbrain-evals](https://github.com/garrytan/gbrain-evals) - Benchmark suite for the gbrain agent-memory system, including a matched-pair System One report on where TypeSafe Jev (`jev-1.13.0`) helps or hurts nine memory decision slots. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gbrain-evals.md).
- [genai (maruel)](https://github.com/maruel/genai) - High-performance multi-provider Go AI package whose `Provider.SystemOne()` asks typed decision questions across TypeSafe Jev, Cloudflare CLEF/CLEF-Flash, Ollama and local llama.cpp decision models with shared `genai.Questions` / `genai.Answers` maps. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/maruel-genai.md).
- [genigrep](https://github.com/Cloudgeni-ai/genigrep) - Ask a codebase question and get only answering source; TypeSafe Jev ranks candidates for coding agents. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/genigrep.md).
- [Genkit flows on Kubernetes routed by Jev](https://github.com/xavidop/genkit-k8s-flows-jev) - Go example where each step of a support-ticket pipeline is a Genkit flow running as a Kubernetes pod through senro; Jev decides department, urgency and escalation through Genkit's System One plugin and then reviews Gemini's draft reply. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/genkit-k8s-flows-jev.md).
- [Genkit software factory gated by Jev](https://github.com/xavidop/genkit-k8s-flows-jev-software-factory) - Go example of a software factory on Kubernetes: Gemini designs, plans and writes a Go module as Genkit flows run through senro, and Jev holds every gate, deciding whether the design is ready and whether to ship, rework or hand a human a brief. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/genkit-jev-software-factory.md).
- [ghtriage](https://github.com/KalyanM45/GitHub-Issue-Classification-Using-Jev) - Classifies GitHub issues with TypeSafe Jev typed labels and calibrated confidence; writes are opt-in (`ghtriage`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ghtriage.md).
- [ghwatch](https://github.com/koshian/ghwatch) - Ruby orchestrator that runs coding, review and finalizer agents off GitHub issues and pull requests, with optional TypeSafe Jev issue screening before work starts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ghwatch.md).
- [git-jev-stage](https://github.com/ibrahemid/git-jev-stage) - Classifies Git hunks against a one-sentence staging intent with TypeSafe Jev, then stages confirmed blocks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/git-jev-stage.md).
- [gitpp-gate](https://github.com/SuperInstance/gitpp-gate) - Confidence-gated nudging for the git.pp fold: asks TypeSafe Jev before nudging, fails closed, and logs the decision so automation cannot push without a typed yes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gitpp-gate.md).
- [glimpse](https://github.com/Viverun/glimpse-jev) - Computer-use harness for the Linux GNOME desktop: a planner model writes the plan, and Jev finds each control, checks each result and decides when the task is done, across LibreOffice, Files, the browser and other apps. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/glimpse-jev.md).
- [gm-autoplayer](https://github.com/sugeesh/gm-autoplayer) - Agent that plays browser games such as slither.io from screenshots through the Playwright MCP server; pick an LLM brain, a Jev brain that answers quick typed questions about each frame (danger, nearest food) for game rules to turn into moves, or a hybrid with an LLM strategist. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gm-autoplayer.md).
- [gmail-auto-cleanup](https://github.com/nani-stack/gmail-auto-cleanup) - Python tool that sorts a Gmail inbox with a System One model — TypeSafe Jev (tested on ~27,000 emails) or experimental local Laya — answering typed yes/no questions per email while plain Python rules act; actions are archive + label with a 7-day hold before Trash, and the OAuth scope cannot permanently delete mail. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gmail-auto-cleanup.md).
- [go-decide](https://github.com/rshade/go-decide) - Go CLI that puts a choice or ordered rubric to a System One model — TypeSafe Jev by default, Cloudflare Clef opt-in — and prints a JSON outcome whose exit code tells your script to act, ask a person or escalate; it gates and labels decisions but never approves them. Early development, no tagged release yet. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/go-decide.md).
- [go-discern](https://github.com/mrz1836/go-discern) - Small typed Go decision layer for the TypeSafe Jev and Cloudflare Clef System One APIs, with validated observations and zero third-party runtime dependencies. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/go-discern.md).
- [go-jev](https://github.com/mattn/go-jev) - Go SDK and jev-cli for TypeSafe Jev typed Noul/Choice/Score (explicit key; distinct from jevgo and TypeSafe Go). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/go-jev.md).
- [GoEventBus](https://github.com/Protocol-Lattice/GoEventBus) - High-performance Go event bus with deterministic rules, a decision cache, and an optional `JevSelector` that asks Jev (via OpenRouter's Decisions API) to choose an event type only on a cache miss. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/goeventbus.md).
- [goinfer](https://github.com/townsendmerino/goinfer) - Pure-Go, no-cgo local LLM inference (library and server) whose `POST /v1/systemone` answers TypeSafe's decisions wire shape so clients such as jevx work against it; it scores option labels with the served model or loads a trained decision head — TypeSafe's hosted model is not included. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/goinfer.md).
- [gojev](https://github.com/taigrr/gojev) - Go harness for System One decision models — TypeSafe's hosted Jev, Jev via Vercel AI Gateway, Cloudflare Clef and in-process open-weight Kev — with typed Choice/Noul/Score question builders, calibrated answers, gating helpers and a CLI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gojev.md).
- [Gomoku × Jev](https://github.com/fanweiya/gomoku-jev) - Five-in-a-row against Jev on a Three.js 3D board (README in Chinese): local heuristics propose up to ten tactically labelled moves and Jev picks one with a Choice question, showing the pick and its confidence in the status bar. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gomoku-jev.md).
- [gpui-agent](https://github.com/stolinski/gpui-agent) - Jev-driven native GPUI testing over accessibility trees (experimental, macOS-first, instrumented apps). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gpui-agent.md).
- [granite-switch-systemone (vLLM System One endpoint)](https://github.com/generative-computing/granite-switch-systemone) - vLLM endpoint plugin that serves a Granite Switch checkpoint's decision adapter through both OpenAI's Decisions API and Jev's System One API on the same server and GPU cache as chat completions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/granite-switch-systemone.md).
- [graphify-jev](https://github.com/54Lynnn/graphify-jev) - Codebase knowledge graph with Tree-sitter AST topology plus TypeSafe Jev semantic navigation for coding agents (no vector DB). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/54lynnn-graphify-jev.md).
- [Graphlin](https://github.com/royosherove/graphlin) - Live architecture and activity diagrams for Claude Code or Codex while they explore code, with optional TypeSafe Jev classification. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/graphlin.md).
- [grep2jev](https://github.com/mars7100/grep2jev) - CLI that lets a coding agent hand file triage to Jev: pipe in candidate files, Jev scores each against the request in parallel (Python sent as AST skeletons), and the agent reads only files that pass. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/grep2jev.md).
- [grev](https://github.com/aurorainfra/grev) - "Thinking coreutils": Unix filters (`grev`, `isv`, `tagv`, `rank`, `sortv`, `pickv`) that ask TypeSafe Jev typed questions, so output is always your input with labels or scores only as explicit columns. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/grev.md).
- [Grok Bot Jev](https://github.com/Bodila51/grok-bot-jev) - Reference router and skill that use TypeSafe Jev to choose reuse/stop/cap/ask-human actions before expensive Grok Bot work. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/grok-bot-jev.md).
- [grok-jev-guard](https://github.com/0xwhrari/grok-jev-guard) - Typed preflight/approval layer for Grok Bot: local policy plus TypeSafe Jev for ambiguity (shadow-first; distinct from Grok Bot Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/grok-jev-guard.md).
- [grokbot-jev-skills](https://github.com/fal3/grokbot-jev-skills) - Ten Grok Bot agent skills, adapted from hermes-jev-skills, that hand small decisions — which search results to read, passage filtering and injection screening, mailbox sort, support triage, routine wake-ups, transcript cutting — to TypeSafe Jev through a `jev` CLI; fail-open, with documented redaction. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/grokbot-jev-skills.md).
- [guardrails-md](https://github.com/berget-ai/guardrails-md) - Berget AI plugin for opencode, pi and Claude Code (plus a git pre-commit hook) that scores every bash command with a System One decision model against your repo's `guardrails.md` before it runs; defaults to Berget's `berget/bev` model, and any `/v1/systemone`-compatible endpoint (TypeSafe settings as fallback) can be used. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/guardrails-md.md).
- [guesswork](https://github.com/FindMalek/guesswork) - zsh history autosuggestions ranked by TypeSafe Jev instead of prefix match. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/guesswork.md).
- [Gut](https://github.com/vinibrsl/gut) - Elixir DSL for typed decisions in ordinary control flow (`Gut.feel/3` returns an Elixir value) with a `Gut.ReqLLM.Jev` adapter for TypeSafe Jev, plus other LLM, Ollama, and custom adapters. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gut.md).
- [Gut Feel Benchmark](https://github.com/pragmaticfrontier/gut-feel-benchmark) - Benchmark of snap probabilities at deadlines from a quarter second to 16 seconds: 703 boolean items in four packs (poker equity, base-rate puzzles, heart disease, Hacker News outcomes), sent to Jev, other decision models and chat models through AI SDK's `experimental_decide`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gut-feel-benchmark.md).
- [gutfeel](https://github.com/huayaney-exe/gutfeel) - Usability testing for MCP servers: a participant that doesn't deliberate — TypeSafe Jev by default — walks your tools on realistic, hint-free missions, and gutfeel draws the tree of every path (right tool, wrong turn, dead end, error, loop) with the description sentence behind each failure; pre-alpha, with `gutfeel live` runnable today. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gutfeel.md).
- [gutsy](https://github.com/kouhxp/gutsy) - Local calibrated gutsy-0.8b decision model with Jev-compatible /v1/systemone and Decisions APIs on CPU (independent of hosted TypeSafe Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gutsy.md).
- [HA Jev Autopilot](https://github.com/davidzha712/ha-jev-autopilot) - Home Assistant: TypeSafe Jev decides per-room actions; code acts with ask-first for risky devices (distinct from HA-Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ha-jev-autopilot.md).
- [HA_SystemOne](https://github.com/jarekmor/HA_SystemOne) - Polish-language Home Assistant command layer: local embedding and reranker models narrow about 100 possible actions to five, Jev picks one or none with a confidence, and the action runs only at 0.8 or above; otherwise it asks back. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ha-systemone.md).
- [hak-jev-plugin](https://github.com/jmgomezl/hak-jev-plugin) - Hedera Agent Kit gate: TypeSafe Jev judges sensitive tool calls (v0 SaucerSwap) before sign; HCS receipts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hak-jev-plugin.md).
- [Hammertime](https://github.com/KishoreXO/Hammertime) - Local live Formula 1 strategy desk: a strategy engine owns the numbers (tyre model, pit loss, Monte Carlo), while System One models such as TypeSafe Jev read race-control messages, gauge rain risk, and weigh evidence into box-now or stay-out calls. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hammertime.md).
- [Handoff Graph](https://github.com/aashish-g03/handoff-graph) - Issue-routing experiment that stores handoffs in FalkorDB and asks TypeSafe Jev one narrow question per candidate owner. Graph memory cut human escalations from ~two-thirds to under 10% on held-out VS Code issues in the author's study. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/handoff-graph.md).
- [harness-router](https://github.com/Protocol-Lattice/harness-router) - Fast decision routing for agent harnesses — native MCP with Jev for tool selection and MCTS for multi-step decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/protocol-lattice-harness-router.md).
- [Harness² (recursive agent harnessing)](https://github.com/SASUKE40/harness2) - Python framework that improves an agent's harness (prompts, guardrails, memory, tools, skills, scripts) task by task while the model weights stay fixed, with OpenCode and Codex executors, three professional benchmarks, and optional Jev selection of the best final output when it runs k > 1 candidates. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/harness2-recursive-harnessing.md).
- [HearMemory](https://github.com/ssd1051/hearmemory) - Shared multi-agent coding memory: TypeSafe Jev checks claims against recorded evidence before the next agent trusts them. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hearmemory.md).
- [HekaJev](https://github.com/zurk/hekajev) - Reproducible Git-history analytics powered by TypeSafe Jev classifications. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hekajev.md).
- [Herdr macOS plugins](https://github.com/klinter007/herdr-macos-plugins) - Four macOS plugins for the Herdr agent terminal: open files from agent output, keep the Mac awake, show CPU, disk and folder size, and switch Jev on or off per Claude Code or Codex session through jev-gateway. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/herdr-macos-plugins.md).
- [Herdr-Jev](https://github.com/flaviomartil/herdr-jev) - Plugin for the Herdr terminal workspace and AI-Harness that uses TypeSafe Jev System One (~260–340 ms) for multi-model triage — architectural complexity, research need and reasoning effort — then routes turns to Codex, Claude, Kiro and other peers with calibrated latency deadlines, Triad plan/implement/review orchestration, quota awareness and resumable runs. Separate from the listed alexzfe jev-herdr. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/herdr-jev.md).
- [here-we-go-jev](https://github.com/creativoma/here-we-go-jev) - Local playground/test bench for TypeSafe Jev: typed questions, calibrated probabilities, side-by-side LLM baseline, and mock backend (Bun). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/creativoma-here-we-go-jev.md).
- [Hermes Adaptive Effort](https://github.com/atostivint/hermes-adaptive-effort) - Hermes Agent plugin that asks a separate scorer (Jev by default) to judge each task and sets the outgoing request's reasoning effort. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hermes-adaptive-effort.md).
- [Hermes Bots plugin](https://github.com/clearspace-to/hermes-bots) - Hermes agent plugin behind the Hermes Bots mobile app: native cards and forms, phone alerts, voice, model routing, and a card guard where Jev decides in about 0.2 s whether a prose reply should become a card. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hermes-bots.md).
- [Hermes GPT (Jev decision bridge)](https://github.com/asimons81/hermes-gpt) - MCP connector that gives ChatGPT controlled access to a Hermes Agent machine, now with an optional Jev bridge: three typed tools for yes/no, choose and score judgments, plus an advisory Jev review score for dispatched work. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hermes-gpt.md).
- [Hermes Jev Skills](https://github.com/kerpopule/hermes-jev-skills) - Agent skills and `jev` CLI using TypeSafe Jev for model routing, memory filtering, compaction, skill selection, triage, and computer/browser action choice on Hermes, Claude Code, and Codex. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hermes-jev-skills.md).
- [hermes-adaptive-model-router](https://github.com/INEEDBUG/hermes-adaptive-model-router) - Shadow-first adaptive model router plugin for Hermes Agent: a redacted, current-turn routing dossier goes to TypeSafe Jev, which chooses between a fast model and a high-capability model; decisions are logged content-free while the gateway's configured model still runs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hermes-adaptive-model-router.md).
- [hermes-jev (ourines)](https://github.com/ourines/hermes-jev) - Hermes Agent decision sidekick: explicit Jev tools and a bundled skill via TypeSafe, Cloudflare, or OpenRouter backends. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ourines-hermes-jev.md).
- [hermes-jev-curator](https://github.com/anpicasso/hermes-jev-curator) - Hermes plugin: TypeSafe Jev typed skill-relationship judgments and safe curator archive/guard plans (experimental 0.1.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hermes-jev-curator.md).
- [hermes-jev-helper](https://github.com/maurorosero/hermes-jev-helper) - Hermes Agent plugin: TypeSafe Jev intent classifier picks a source-first route (`skill`/`research`/…) before the LLM turn. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hermes-jev-helper.md).
- [hermes-jev-lesson-gate](https://github.com/VBS2004/hermes-jev-lesson-gate) - Hermes plugin: TypeSafe Jev judges when memory/skill background review should run instead of a turn counter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hermes-jev-lesson-gate.md).
- [hermes-loopguard](https://github.com/DECRUX9812/hermes-loopguard) - Hermes Agent plugin with cheap typed semantic gates (TypeSafe Jev via typesafe-sdk) plus zero-token change gates and computer-use gates to stop automations from burning tokens in loops. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hermes-loopguard.md).
- [Hev](https://github.com/luisrudge/hev) - Working Jev-compatible Decisions API answered by human visitors on a live page; swap the Jev base URL to test an integration against human answers (not for production). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hev.md).
- [himalaya-jev-mail-classify](https://github.com/initrd/himalaya-jev-mail-classify) - CLI that classifies Gmail threads via himalaya + TypeSafe Jev (OpenRouter) and applies labels (dry-run default). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/himalaya-jev-mail-classify.md).
- [HiRoute](https://github.com/higress-group/HiRoute) - Local-first agent routing engine with TypeSafe Jev decision extensions for branch selection. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hiroute.md).
- [HN Jev Sorter](https://github.com/ritza-co/hn-jev-sorter) - Chrome extension that sorts Hacker News comments into custom buckets in real time with Jev probabilities. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hn-jev-sorter.md).
- [Hollow Knight + Jev](https://github.com/mbrajk/hollow-knight-jev) - Source-only research prototype that patches Hollow Knight with a C# bridge exposing bounded numeric observations, lets Jev choose movement and nail strikes as separate questions against Gruz Mother in Godhome, and applies short input leases, with a spending ledger and a local dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hollow-knight-jev.md).
- [Honeytongue](https://github.com/tbrought/honeytongue) - NPC persuasion engine: TypeSafe Jev judges whether player dialogue convinced a character. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/honeytongue.md).
- [hono-jev-router](https://github.com/yusukebe/hono-jev-router) - Experimental Hono router that matches HTTP requests to plain-English descriptions with TypeSafe Jev Noul judgments. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hono-jev-router.md).
- [hookgate](https://github.com/Allan-Nava/hookgate) - Claude Code/Codex hooks: TypeSafe Jev gates risky Bash and unverified completions (audit/fail-open). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hookgate.md).
- [Hopwise (knowledge-graph traversal benchmark)](https://github.com/Aohkne/hopwise) - Benchmark (README in Vietnamese) where Jev picks which edge to follow at each node of a Neo4j knowledge graph built from Wikidata and SEC EDGAR: 26 of 60 questions right (43%) in a 4.2 s median for $0.07, against 25% for Gemma 4 12B and 27% for gpt-oss-20b. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hopwise.md).
- [hsom (jevdo)](https://github.com/gi-dellav/jevdo) - Strict-allowlist harness that lets Jev pick bash commands, flags and paths from a TOML decision tree. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hsom.md).
- [hunch](https://github.com/steven-shoemaker/hunch) - Python verbs (`classify`/`score`/`check`/`pick`/`rank`/`where`) over scalars, lists, and pandas columns backed by TypeSafe Jev (`hunch-jev`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hunch.md).
- [hunch (Unix filter)](https://github.com/yarolegovich/hunch) - Go command-line filter that sorts, filters and routes text by meaning: pipe in logs, tickets, reviews or diffs, ask a plain-English question, and Jev answers it for every line as yes/no, a category or a score with a confidence your next command can use. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hunch-unix-filter.md).
- [Huncho](https://github.com/grafuls/huncho) - Rust server that implements the Jev `/v1/systemone` API for self-hosted System One models (ONNX, Laya, Kev, Clef runtimes, plus a mock), so the TypeSafe SDK can point at your own server. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/huncho.md).
- [hyalo](https://github.com/ractive/hyalo) - Rust CLI for Markdown knowledgebases (LLM Wiki pattern, frontmatter queries, links) whose `hyalo-tidy` agent skill can, when you explicitly ask, have TypeSafe Jev suggest missing types and existing folders for selected documents — audit-only suggestions the agent checks against local schemas. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hyalo.md).
- [ICDLens](https://github.com/Advaidv121/icdlens) - Python SDK for ICD-10 lookup where TypeSafe Jev scores catalogue candidates for human-reviewable coding suggestions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/icdlens.md).
- [imajev](https://github.com/mohit67890/imajev) - Open Jev-style multimodal typed-decision models (photos+records+text → options with can't-tell). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/imajev.md).
- [InspireJev](https://github.com/q1325833367/inspire-jev) - Browser-automation execution toolkit (Chinese and English docs) where the main agent plans and verifies while TypeSafe Jev picks Playwright actions from candidates actually observed on the page; GPT, Pi and MCP entries, with optional local `/v1/systemone` deciders in the 1.1 RC. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/inspire-jev.md).
- [IntelligentDocumentProcessing (Jev + Haiku)](https://github.com/DanielHuette/IntelligentDocumentProcessing) - Hybrid document processing where TypeSafe Jev decides type and completeness and Claude Haiku extracts, benchmarked on 250 documents. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/intelligent-document-processing.md).
- [Intent-Router](https://github.com/angel291592/Intent-Router) - Agent skill that compiles vague requests into typed IntentSpec contracts (probe/ask/halt) as input for Jev/Laya routers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/intent-router.md).
- [IntentLock](https://github.com/BrienAustinClayton/intentlock) - Service that uses TypeSafe Jev (`TypeSafeClient`, default `https://api.typesafe.ai`) as the “smart” decision path for intent locking / approval-style checks alongside other LLM lanes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/intentlock.md).
- [invalidate](https://github.com/chopratejas/invalidate) - Semantic TTL for agent memory: TypeSafe Jev checks every stored fact against new evidence; code marks superseded memories without rewriting text. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/invalidate.md).
- [IronBee Express](https://github.com/ironbee-ai/ironbee-express) - Goal-driven browser agent: each step is one TypeSafe Jev decision over the page's offered controls plus one IronBee DevTools call that acts and returns the next snapshot, so model output never becomes a selector, coordinate or script; after the run it reviews the final page and API responses (and backend traces with IronBee), and runs can be recorded and replayed without the engine. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ironbee-express.md).
- [IronBee Gamer](https://github.com/ironbee-ai/ironbee-gamer) - Plays browser games live (2D canvas, Phaser, PixiJS, Cocos, HTML): a perception adapter turns what the page draws into a small JSON state and a decision engine picks every input — hosted TypeSafe Jev with your key, or Laya fine-tuned per game and run locally — with a web UI showing each state, action and probability, a coding-agent trainer for new games, and a shared Hugging Face library. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ironbee-gamer.md).
- [is-lesion](https://github.com/hemanth/is-lesion) - npm package that evaluates MRI and other medical scan images against Hugging Face dataset labels with Jev 1.14 (Noul, Choice, Score and Box), or local OpenJev vision models, returning a label, probability and bounding box; can score dataset rows directly. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/is-lesion.md).
- [is-malicious](https://github.com/luantak/is-malicious) - Scans source/config/CI files with TypeSafe Jev for deceptive or data-stealing behavior and points to suspicious lines. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/is-malicious.md).
- [J++](https://github.com/Towow-ai/jpp) - Experimental language and Rust runtime for composing semantic Jev questions with exact methods as values; offline fixtures included. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jpp.md).
- [J3v](https://github.com/JGalego/J3v) - Edge-compiled System One decision runtime ('J3v is to Jev as k3s is to k8s') with Rust compiler/firmware demos. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/j3v.md).
- [japanese-jev-lint](https://github.com/pankona/japanese-jev-lint) - Go CLI (`jjl`) that flags Japanese sentences with TypeSafe Jev Noul probabilities (plus local です/ます regex); no rewrite generation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/japanese-jev-lint.md).
- [Jarvis (Amphion)](https://github.com/AmphionTeam/Jarvis) - Research system and paper ("Coordination Deserves a Brain of Its Own") for full-duplex voice agents: JEV decides each turn (answer, memory, worker, clarify, control, ignore or wait) so you can keep talking while background tasks run. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jarvis-amphion.md).
- [JarvisCore](https://github.com/Prescott-Data/jarviscore-framework) - Python multi-agent runtime (peer-to-peer mesh, durable state, integration atoms) with a `typesafe` extra that injects a shared Jev client as `self.decisions` and can route subagents, task complexity and RAG passages with Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jarviscore.md).
- [JCR](https://github.com/NiazMorshed2007/jcr) - Jev Capability Resolver: TypeSafe Jev searches a nested capability tree and returns deterministic command documentation for agents (MCP + harnesses). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jcr.md).
- [jear](https://github.com/iJ03l/jear) - Rust Jev-routed client for NEAR AI Cloud inference and IronClaw agents using budget/quality/sensitivity decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jear.md).
- [Jeeves (PostHog)](https://github.com/PostHog/jeeves) - Open 9B Jev-like reasoning classifier (noul/choice/score) with a Jev-compatible API and training code; independent of TypeSafe-hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/posthog-jeeves.md).
- [jeff (Claude Code router)](https://github.com/saembit/jeff) - Claude Code plugin that uses Jev to route every subtask: model tier, Claude or Codex, which skill, write access, and parallelism, then asks Jev to verify each result and escalates misses. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jeff-claude-code.md).
- [Jeff (firelex)](https://github.com/firelex/jeff) - Jeff: an open 0.8B "System 1" decision model (MIT code, Apache-2.0 weights on Hugging Face) that uses the same request format as Jev, with swappable LoRA adapters (guard, triage, tools, grounding, spam…), GGUF files for llama.cpp, and Jeff-Code; independent and not affiliated with TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/firelex-jeff.md).
- [jeff (Viperwow)](https://github.com/Viperwow/jeff) - Single-binary Rust classifier server with an admin page: one `/v1/systemone`-shaped API in front of Jev-compatible models — TypeSafe Jev in the cloud, a local Contrastive-LM (CLM) it installs, or Laya/PipeLLM/any compatible server — with access keys and a playground. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/viperwow-jeff.md).
- [Jeffort](https://github.com/andrewstephenson-v1/Jeffort) - Claude Code plugin: TypeSafe Jev scores each prompt and sets Claude effort without touching the model (MIT). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jeffort.md).
- [jegrep](https://github.com/can1357/jegrep) - Semantic grep CLI: describe code intent and get Jev-ranked matches via TypeSafe or OpenRouter, without an embedding index. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jegrep.md).
- [jes](https://github.com/everafterlabs/jes) - Open-source guardrails for AI agents that ask Jev to judge prompts, retrieved content, tool calls and responses for prompt injection, jailbreaks and secret/PII leaks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jes.md).
- [jev (drpaneas)](https://github.com/drpaneas/jev) - Go package for calling TypeSafe Jev System One typed questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/drpaneas-jev.md).
- [jev (okooo5km)](https://github.com/okooo5km/jev) - Stdlib Python CLI + Agent Skill for TypeSafe Jev `yes`/`pick`/`score` via TypeSafe API or OpenRouter (distinct from typesafe-cli / typesafeai-cli). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/okooo5km-jev.md).
- [jev (polidog)](https://github.com/polidog/jev) - Unofficial Rust CLI for TypeSafe Jev across TypeSafe, Cloudflare, and Vercel providers (CLI shape follows stefafafan/jev)—distinct from feder-cr/jev (jevos). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/polidog-jev.md).
- [jev (stefafafan)](https://github.com/stefafafan/jev) - Unofficial provider-neutral Go/Unix CLI for TypeSafe Jev (Choice/Noul/Score) via TypeSafe, Cloudflare, or Vercel. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/stefafafan-jev.md).
- [jev (taifoon-io)](https://github.com/taifoon-io/jev) - Grade an AI agent job with TypeSafe Jev: fact checks in code, four closed questions, receipt with probabilities, optional on-chain records (`@taifoon/jev`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/taifoon-io-jev.md).
- [Jev + Claude Code case study](https://github.com/gsaini/jev-claude-code-case-study) - Independent learning project testing what "Jev + Claude Code" means with two working programs: Triage Desk, a support-ticket app asking Jev six typed questions per ticket in one request with low-confidence cases going to human review, and Jev Guard, a Claude Code PreToolUse hook asking five redacted `noul` questions about ambiguous shell commands that never answers `allow`; both have offline evals and keyword baselines. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-claude-code-case-study.md).
- [Jev 1.14 comparison benchmark (etsabary)](https://github.com/etsabary/jev-1.14-comparison-benchmark) - 1,136 deterministic text decisions across 25 families run on eleven services (Jev 1.14 and 1.13, Luna Decisions, Luna, Mercury, Gemini Flash-Lite, Qwen, Granite and others) with matched diagnostics, latency percentiles and versioned results. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-1-14-comparison-benchmark.md).
- [Jev Account Filter](https://github.com/caiweihan/jev-account-filter) - Screens video-platform accounts for dataset curation with Jev title ranking followed by multimodal account-category decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-account-filter.md).
- [Jev Ad Block](https://github.com/lingengyuan/jev-adblock) - Chrome MV3 ad blocker that combines network rules with Jev judgements of candidate page elements. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-adblock.md).
- [JEV ADK](https://github.com/abyakod/JEV_ADK) - Agent Development Kit patterns for TypeSafe Jev: guardrails, dual-brain routing, and PR triage blueprints (System One decisions in code). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-adk.md).
- [Jev Agent Builder](https://github.com/enzotripoli/jev-agent-builder) - Single-file visual builder for Jev requests: compose Choice, Score and Noul questions with `{{parameters}}`, validate against documented limits, and export the exact `/v1/systemone` JSON, cURL, a TypeScript function or a reusable agent definition. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-agent-builder.md).
- [Jev Agent Control](https://github.com/Krzysztof-Cieslak/jev-agent-control) - OpenCode V2 plugin that uses Jev to route requests between Plan, Build, and custom primary agents, with automatic handoff at execution boundaries. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-agent-control.md).
- [Jev Agent Kit (cloud-f1)](https://github.com/cloud-f1/jev-agent-kit) - Claude Code plugin that prunes long Bash output with local rules plus an optional Jev relevance pass, keeping originals recoverable. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cloud-f1-jev-agent-kit.md).
- [JEV AgentNetwork](https://github.com/Kelly-Leeee/AgentNetwork-jev) - Event-driven multi-agent network where TypeSafe Jev reassigns pending work whenever any agent finishes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agentnetwork-jev.md).
- [Jev Behavior for Unity](https://github.com/gbesse/unity-jev-behavior) - Guarded asynchronous decision nodes for Unity Behavior using a server-side DecisionPacks gateway for Jev-style decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/unity-jev-behavior.md).
- [Jev Bench (cocodedk)](https://github.com/cocodedk/jev-bench) - Configurable workbench for repeatable TypeSafe Jev latency and accuracy experiments, with published results at jev-bench.cocode.dk. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cocodedk-jev-bench.md).
- [JEV Book Tags](https://github.com/iamjonatha/jev-book-tags) - Calibre plugin for AI-assisted book tagging with TypeSafe Jev (single-book and batch; GPL-3.0-or-later). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-book-tags.md).
- [Jev Browser (openqa-cn)](https://github.com/openqa-cn/jev-browser) - Indexed Playwright automation: TypeSafe Jev chooses control/op; CodexQA skill with replay and HTML reports. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openqa-jev-browser.md).
- [Jev Browser (tontoko)](https://github.com/tontoko/jev-browser) - Integrates Jev field selection and source-backed extraction with a Playwright SDK, CLI, MCP server, and explicit assertions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-browser-tontoko.md).
- [Jev Browser (Ying-Kai-Liao)](https://github.com/Ying-Kai-Liao/jev-browser) - Turns caller-defined browser goals into Jev-selected Playwright actions with a library, CLI, and MCP server. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-browser-ying-kai-liao.md).
- [Jev Browser Agent](https://github.com/ali-master/jev-browser-agent) - Bun and TypeScript browser agent with an indexed action space: a typed decision model (Jev, local or cloud) picks the operation and element each step, a chat helper writes a checklist, and a live inspector streams Chromium as it works. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-browser-agent.md).
- [Jev Browser MCP (saurabhgangurde)](https://github.com/saurabhgangurde/jev-browser-mcp) - Local MCP server that lets a coding agent run bounded browser tasks in a dedicated Chrome window: `browser_task` follows a goal through Jev-selected page actions drawn only from the request and live page evidence, with the full Chrome DevTools MCP operation set also exposed. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-browser-mcp-saurabh.md).
- [Jev Browser Skill](https://github.com/zurfyx/jev-browser-skill) - Claude Code/Codex skill where TypeSafe Jev chooses browser operations and targets from a code-built element table (reference implementation; explainer site included). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-browser-skill.md).
- [Jev Bug Hunter](https://github.com/BillNDD/jev-bug-hunter) - Bounded first-pass bug hunting for source files powered by TypeSafe Jev — a quick sanity check, not a replacement for tests or review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-bug-hunter.md).
- [Jev by Example](https://github.com/ReallyArtificial/jev-by-example) - Ten runnable JavaScript lessons on agent decisions between steps; Jev supplies typed judgments and code owns policy (offline fixtures by default). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-by-example.md).
- [Jev Chromium](https://github.com/kolawong/jev-chromium) - Electron browser agent that collects data across pages: Jev picks each click, focus, navigation or done step from the page's actionable elements, an OpenAI-compatible text model plans, fills and verifies, and results export as a cited table or CSV. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-chromium.md).
- [Jev Classification for n8n](https://github.com/khmuhtadin/n8n-nodes-jev-classification) - Adds typed classification, scoring, yes/no checks, and batched questions to self-hosted n8n, with configurable review routing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-classification-n8n.md).
- [Jev Classifier for Obsidian](https://github.com/ItsBen321/obsidian-jev-classifier) - Obsidian plugin: classify note properties with TypeSafe Jev using an editable guide (one note or whole vault). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/obsidian-jev-classifier.md).
- [jev Claude Code plugin (yinjs)](https://github.com/yinjs/claude-jev) - Claude Code plugin that hands small frequent decisions to Jev at six points — permission requests, prompt-injection warnings on fetched content, a 'done' re-check, prompt nudges, and jev-route effort/model routing — each one ~1 s call that changes nothing when Jev is unsure; Jev via OpenRouter (default), TypeSafe or Vercel AI Gateway. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/yinjs-claude-jev.md).
- [jev CLI (vilinski)](https://github.com/vilinski/jev) - Rust CLI that sends typed questions to Jev and other System One providers and prints JSON answers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/vilinski-jev-cli.md).
- [Jev Code Reviewer (egma-ai)](https://github.com/egma-ai/jev-code-reviewer) - Local PR triage: TypeSafe Jev prioritizes changes; OpenAI explains; Chrome overlay posts nothing to GitHub. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/egma-ai-jev-code-reviewer.md).
