- [JEV Codex Pilot](https://github.com/Charlyhno-eng/jev-codex-pilot) - Local Codex Kanban workspace with Jev-model routing, contextual compaction, and ticket lifecycle control. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-codex-pilot.md).
- [Jev Consensus Gate](https://github.com/kallurayaankit/jev-consensus-gate) - Agent action gate that asks Jev about each proposed action three independent ways (safety, legitimacy, adversarial) and only allows it when all three agree; any block wins and any escalation goes to a human. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-consensus-gate.md).
- [Jev Content Moderation](https://github.com/kallurayaankit/jev-content-mod) - Content moderation demo with asymmetric approve / shadow / remove thresholds on Jev probabilities, so irreversible removal needs more certainty than approval. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-content-mod.md).
- [Jev Cookbook (Datawhale)](https://github.com/datawhalechina/jev-cookbook) - Chinese System One/Jev cookbook: notebooks, 18 recipes, docs translation (CC BY-NC-SA 4.0 — free educational / non-commercial; not OSI open source). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-cookbook.md).
- [Jev Decision](https://github.com/Coding-Dev-Tools/jev-decision) - Portable, budgeted Jev client for coding-agent harnesses (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Gemini CLI, OpenCode, any MCP or shell client) with a shared daily budget ledger, explicit `unavailable` results and harness recipes; advice, never authority. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-decision.md).
- [Jev Decision Gateway (kartikanand73)](https://github.com/kartikanand73/jev-decision-gateway) - Governed withdrawal PoC: TypeSafe Jev scores, deterministic policy signs, executor only accepts signed decisions; bench vs GPT-6 Sol and rules — distinct from kuldeepsinh19/jev-decision-gateway. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kartikanand73-jev-decision-gateway.md).
- [JEV Decision Hub](https://github.com/gbonnet65923/jev-decision-hub) - Zero-dependency Python TypeSafe Jev client with key-pool rotation, a small web dashboard, and ready examples (moderation, routing, trades). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-decision-hub.md).
- [Jev decision-model benchmarks (vilinski)](https://github.com/vilinski/jev-benchmarks) - Reproducible decision-model benchmark: a hand-labelled own set (60 tasks x3) and a 1,100-item public sample, run against Jev and other System One routes, with raw results, pricing snapshots and generators for a published report and interactive model explorer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/vilinski-jev-benchmarks.md).
- [Jev Decisions Plugin for Hermes](https://github.com/bojansandhaus/jev-decisions-hermes) - Hermes (and other agent) plugin adding Jev-backed tool-risk reviews, approval recommendations, source checks, and task-completion checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-decisions-hermes.md).
- [Jev Deep Research](https://github.com/sunyasheng/JevDeepResearch) - Research agent pattern: GPT plans searches; TypeSafe Jev judges document regions in parallel and returns original evidence passages. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevdeepresearch.md).
- [Jev Engineering Cookbook](https://github.com/Jev-Engineering/cookbook) - Curriculum of Jupyter notebook recipes for building with Jev one bounded decision at a time — state in, typed `choice` / `noul` / `score` questions out — with offline fixture runs that need no API key, a live mode, and explicit provenance rules separating synthetic pipeline checks from measured Jev results. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-engineering-cookbook.md).
- [Jev Filter (Apixly)](https://github.com/apixly-ai/jev-filter) - Semantic filtering for AI agent tools: npm package `@apixly/jev-filter` (CLI, Python library, MCP server) turns large tool outputs, retrieved records, code and logs into compact typed TypeSafe Jev decisions with source IDs, local originals and receipts before they enter an agent's context; batched requests and maintainer-run A/B benchmarks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/apixly-jev-filter.md).
- [JEV Flaky Detective](https://github.com/JevForge/jev-flaky-detective) - GitHub Action: TypeSafe Jev classifies failing tests as regression/flaky/environment/unknown (never auto-reruns). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-flaky-detective.md).
- [Jev Flow](https://github.com/daltonrpj/jev-flow) - Standalone Node.js studio for typed Jev workflows (Studio, Compendium, Battle Arena; distinct from jev-flow-harness). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-flow.md).
- [Jev for Claude](https://github.com/VoidLight00/jev-for-claude) - Dependency-free Claude Code plugin with nine user-invoked engineering skills (safety preflight, rule rubric, browser QA, evidence completion and more), conservative local hooks, and an explicit opt-in typed TypeSafe Jev judge adapter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/voidlight-jev-for-claude.md).
- [Jev for Claude Code (brookcs3)](https://github.com/brookcs3/jev--system-one-for-claude) - Unofficial Claude Code plugin exposing TypeSafe Jev typed judgments (noul/choice/score) plus corpus→eval loops. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/brookcs3-jev-system-one-for-claude.md).
- [Jev for Claude Code (pachelbel23)](https://github.com/pachelbel23/claude-code-jev) - Unofficial one-file installer that adds Jev hooks to Claude Code: skill suggestion and task-type classification per prompt, optional subagent model tier choice and shell risk confirmation, plus a jev-vision CLI for image yes/no, choice and Box questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pachelbel23-claude-code-jev.md).
- [jev for DuckDB (judoaseeta)](https://github.com/judoaseeta/duckdb-jev) - DuckDB port of pg-jev: ask TypeSafe Jev yes/no, probability, choice and score questions about table rows from SQL (distinct from prasanthj/duckdb-jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/judoaseeta-duckdb-jev.md).
- [Jev for Excel](https://github.com/paulobrien/jev-excel) - Unofficial Excel add-in that calls TypeSafe Jev from worksheet formulas — `=JEV.ASK("choice"|"score"|"noul", A2, …)`, shortcut functions and `JEV.STATE` for multi-field rows — as an async Office add-in for Microsoft 365 or a Windows VBA edition. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-excel.md).
- [Jev for Home Assistant](https://github.com/AboveColin/HA-Jev) - Turns judgments over selected Home Assistant state into sensors and automation responses, with usage accounting. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ha-jev.md).
- [Jev for Splunk](https://github.com/sispehar/jev-for-splunk) - Splunk app adding a `jev` search command for noul/choice/score over events via TypeSafe Jev with KV-store cache (Apache-2.0 LICENSE; GitHub SPDX unset). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-for-splunk.md).
- [Jev Foundry Judge](https://github.com/nguyennhianhtri/jev-foundry-judge) - Drop-in Azure AI Foundry agent evaluators powered by TypeSafe Jev, plus a BYOK demo app and model-router bench. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-foundry-judge.md).
- [Jev Game Engine](https://github.com/King4s/jev-game-engine) - Early Rust desktop prototype for controlling games through bounded goals: TypeSafe Jev selects a goal, a local executor handles movement, validation and stopping, and every decision is traced, timed and replayable; Minecraft Java 26.2 is the first integration, with an offline fixture preview. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-game-engine.md).
- [Jev Gate (lymagics)](https://github.com/lymagics/jev-gate) - GitHub Action/CLI that asks TypeSafe Jev yes/no quality questions over selected scopes and fails the pipeline on threshold breaches (distinct from other listed jev-gate projects). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/lymagics-jev-gate.md).
- [Jev Gate (royalskynet)](https://github.com/royalskynet/jev-gate-mcp) - MCP server and Claude Code/Codex plugin that prunes tool output, compacts conversations and gives task/review advice with TypeSafe Jev; optional local Laya pilot (distinct from other jev-gate projects). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/royalskynet-jev-gate.md).
- [Jev Gatehouse (Kinde)](https://github.com/kinde-starter-kits/jev-agent-authorization) - Kinde identity + permissions with TypeSafe Jev typed gates before every MCP tool call. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-gatehouse.md).
- [Jev GitHub Action](https://github.com/cachix/jev-action) - GitHub Action: install pinned Jev CLI, run typed judgments on an event/JSON request, expose answers (no issue mutation). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-action.md).
- [Jev Graph Search](https://github.com/Emlembow/jev-graph-search) - CLI and agent skill that retrieves evidence from local Obsidian vaults, Logseq folders, or JSON memory snapshots: a local shortlist is reranked by Jev (TypeSafe or OpenRouter). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-graph-search.md).
- [Jev Harness (Apixly)](https://github.com/apixly-ai/jev-harness) - Python runtime for open-ended goals: a primary AI defines tools, parameter providers and completion checks, then TypeSafe Jev chooses the next tool and arguments from the goal, history and fresh state while code executes, verifies and enforces bounds. Install from Git; the PyPI name jev-harness is an unrelated project. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/apixly-jev-harness.md).
- [Jev Highlight Cutter](https://github.com/gnipbao/jev-highlight-cutter) - Codex agent skill and stdlib Python CLI (Chinese docs) that cuts long interviews, podcasts and streams into an evidence-backed highlight reel of a target length: transcript windows are scored by Jev against your preferences, selections are traceable, and FFmpeg makes a local rough cut. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-highlight-cutter.md).
- [Jev IDS](https://github.com/jev-sec/jev-ids) - Few-shot network intrusion detection with Jev, benchmarked on NSL-KDD against an LLM and classic ML. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-ids.md).
- [Jev in Large Language Gibbs](https://github.com/eumemic/jev-gibbs) - Replication of the TruthfulQA consistent-reasoning experiment from Large Language Gibbs (arXiv 2606.19264) with Jev supplying per-claim conditionals, plus controls, GSM8K, knights-and-knaves, entity resolution and kinship tests of where Gibbs helps. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-gibbs.md).
- [Jev Internal Links](https://github.com/NicoSKOOL/jev-internal-links) - Claude Code skill: crawl a site, ask TypeSafe Jev which internal links to add, apply local rules, export a report. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-internal-links.md).
- [Jev Java SDK (dmishra2022)](https://github.com/dmishra2022/jev-java-sdk) - Community Java 26 client for the System One API with typed noul, choice and score questions, retries, timeouts and virtual threads, plus an optional structured-concurrency module and Spring Boot 4 sample. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-java-sdk.md).
- [Jev Lab](https://github.com/jammaru/jev-lab) - Local Hundred NPC-town and Jev Shogi labs where TypeSafe Jev chooses the next legal action (Rules mode without a key). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-lab.md).
- [Jev LLM Quality Tester](https://github.com/aydinozcekic-maker/jev-llm-quality-tester) - Prototype dashboard and FastAPI service that scores LLM-generated answers against explicit criteria with a Jev/TypeSafe structured-scoring adapter, SQLite history and a no-key deterministic demo mode. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-llm-quality-tester.md).
- [Jev LLM router](https://github.com/dbokatov/jev-llm-router) - Python lab that asks TypeSafe Jev to route a query to an OpenAI model, then has a local Ollama judge review the same choice. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-llm-router.md).
- [Jev Logs](https://github.com/reachjalil/jevlogs) - Scores OpenTelemetry logs for a separate analysis branch, with mock mode and conservative error handling. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevlogs.md).
- [Jev Lucid Memory](https://github.com/JayYun98/jev-lucid-memory) - Research prototype of wake–sleep agent memory: an LLM drafts lessons, a decision model (TypeSafe Jev via the official SDK or OpenRouter, or local Clef-Flash) decides what to keep and when a lesson applies, and code enforces evidence and scope. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-lucid-memory.md).
- [JEV Mail Filtering](https://github.com/albertcas/jev-mail-filtering) - Local read-only IMAP inbox triage with TypeSafe Jev (needs reply / worth reading / scam / unsure). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-mail-filtering.md).
- [Jev MCP (asimons81)](https://github.com/asimons81/jev-mcp) - Small community MCP server that exposes three bounded Jev decision tools (yes/no, choose and score) over stdio or authenticated loopback HTTP, with text-only inputs, no search or action execution, and credentials read only from the environment. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/asimons81-jev-mcp.md).
- [Jev MCP (Freepik)](https://github.com/freepik-company/jev-mcp) - Go MCP server for typed decide/classify/verify/rerank with Jev via OpenRouter or TypeSafe (binary/container releases). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/freepik-jev-mcp.md).
- [Jev MCP (tmbmartell)](https://github.com/tmbmartell/jev-mcp) - Portuguese-language MCP server that exposes Jev (TypeSafe System One) to agents such as Hermes, Claude Code, and Cursor as a typed judgment tool for noul, choice, and score questions, routed via OpenRouter or TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-mcp-tmbmartell.md).
- [Jev MCP Server (keysersoft)](https://github.com/keysersoft/jev-mcp-server) - AnythingMCP connector exposing TypeSafe Jev yes/no, classification, and scoring tools to Claude, ChatGPT, and other MCP hosts (hosted or self-hosted). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/keysersoft-jev-mcp-server.md).
- [JEV MLX](https://github.com/itsodeleo/jev-mlx) - Typed decisions from existing MLX-LM and MLX-VLM models on Apple Silicon without fine-tuning or extra decision weights. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-mlx.md).
- [Jev Mode](https://github.com/tiffygk/jev-mode) - Claude Code skills and study course for building with TypeSafe Jev (PolyForm Noncommercial; not all skills call Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tiffygk-jev-mode.md).
- [Jev Model Router](https://github.com/davila7/claude-code-templates/tree/main/cli-tool/components/mods/productivity/jev-model-router) - Early-access Claude Code mod that uses Jev task assessments and configurable confidence thresholds to route subagent models and main-conversation reasoning effort. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-model-router.md).
- [Jev Model Router (Codex skill)](https://github.com/mrhsiung2015/jev-model-router) - Codex skill (Chinese and English docs) where the parent session condenses a non-trivial task and asks TypeSafe Jev to recommend the model tier and reasoning effort for an execution subagent; a standard-library Python script validates the answer against local policy and outputs one JSON decision, keeping the current model on timeout or invalid responses. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mrhsiung-jev-model-router.md).
- [Jev Model Routing Lab](https://github.com/goldytech/jev-model-routing) - TypeScript lab: TypeSafe Jev typed signals drive confidence-aware Claude/Kimi model routing with simulated offline demos. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-model-routing.md).
- [Jev Moderation](https://github.com/abishakkodi/jev-moderation) - TypeScript and Python SDKs for text moderation with TypeSafe Jev: a bundled, versioned community policy or your own filters, each rule compiled to a Noul question, with review/block thresholds and a policy test CLI (validate, eval, compare with quality gates). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-moderation.md).
- [Jev Moderation API](https://github.com/Noah-Haf/jev-moderation-api) - Small FastAPI proof of concept that moderates text against configurable policies with Jev Noul questions via the official TypeSafe Python SDK. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-moderation-api.md).
- [Jev NetHack](https://github.com/statico/jev-nethack) - TypeSafe Jev plays NetHack 5.0: code lists legal moves, Jev picks one (dashboard + optional Hardfought). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-nethack.md).
- [Jev Observer](https://github.com/LimePencil/jev-observer) - Local proxy and dashboard for TypeSafe Jev decisions (history, versions, latency/cost) with an embedded web UI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-observer.md).
- [JEV OpenFast Browser](https://github.com/samuelyossef/jev-openfast) - Local chat-driven browser agent built on Jev Ultrafast: Jev (via OpenRouter) picks each operation and target from a numbered element table in a real Chrome tab, checks results before reporting success, and hands over for logins or CAPTCHAs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-openfast-browser.md).
- [Jev Orchestrator](https://github.com/petercr/jev-orchestrator) - Small Node/TypeScript experiment testing whether Jev can make useful next-step decisions around a coding agent: decision-only by default (inspect the repo, send compact state to Jev via TypeSafe, Vercel or OpenRouter, apply deterministic thresholds, record a JSONL trace), with an explicit `--orchestrate` mode for a bounded, manually approved loop of safe searches, reads, read-only git, validation scripts and delegation to Codex CLI or Claude Code. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-orchestrator.md).
- [Jev Outlook Cleaner](https://github.com/jimmyLC/jev-outlook-cleaner) - Local web app that triages older Outlook Inbox messages into keep, review and delete candidates with Jev plus local mail signals, auto-moves only high-confidence bulk mail to Deleted Items, and never deletes permanently. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-outlook-cleaner.md).
- [Jev Paired-Replay Study](https://github.com/poisson-labs/jev-replay) - Pre-registered study of whether identical replays flip Jev 1.13's action at shipped thresholds: 12,705 calls on 847 near-threshold items from Banking77, CLINC150 and HelpSteer2 found about 18.5% flips at 0.5 and 0.6 and 5.3% at 0.9, with flips nearly cancelling out. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-paired-replay.md).
- [Jev Playground (xingqiux)](https://github.com/xingqiux/jev-playground) - Local, standard-library Python test bench for Jev: enter text, JSON or images plus Noul, Choice, Score or Box questions, run one request, and see probabilities, raw request/response, cURL and a choice-order sensitivity check, with 60 built-in examples. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/xingqiux-jev-playground.md).
- [jev plays snake](https://github.com/hoanganh25991/typesafe-jev) - Browser Snake where code computes legal moves and exact facts (food distance, flood-fill reachable cells, dead ends) and TypeSafe Jev picks one move per tick with a single `choice` question through a small Hono proxy; misses the deadline → the snake goes straight. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-plays-snake.md).
- [Jev RAG Chinese Benchmark](https://github.com/vicfei/jev-rag-zh-benchmark) - Reproducible Chinese RAG reranking benchmark for TypeSafe Jev (T2Reranking, pinned model, cost caps, per-call logs). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-rag-zh-benchmark.md).
- [JEV Reasoning Navigator](https://github.com/AndreuVM/jev-reasoning-navigator) - Cognitive supervision runtime: TypeSafe Jev semantic judgment plus policy engine, capability receipts, and sandboxed tool execution (license unspecified). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-reasoning-navigator.md).
- [JEV Research MCP](https://github.com/Merth1470/jev-research-mcp) - Local-first MCP research tool: TypeSafe Jev filters search hits and page blocks before dumping them into an agent context. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-research-mcp.md).
- [Jev Review](https://github.com/NiazMorshed2007/jev-review) - MCP server for experimental software-quality rubric scores and comparisons; requires `JEV_API_KEY` and sends supplied code context to TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-review.md).
- [Jev Review (Dev Agrawal)](https://github.com/devagrawal09/jev-review) - Screens JavaScript and TypeScript changes or source files with staged Jev judgments and displays evidence-linked findings in a local dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-review-devagrawal.md).
- [Jev Review Action](https://github.com/fatwang2/jev-review-action) - Configurable GitHub Action: catalog or PR classification with TypeSafe Jev only (no text-gen), one template comment. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-review-action.md).
- [Jev review gate](https://github.com/RomanXSad/jev-review-gate) - GitHub Actions gate: local rules + TypeSafe Jev questions on the diff; exits 0/1 (does not merge). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-review-gate.md).
- [Jev Review Markers for Premiere](https://github.com/gbesse/jev-premiere-markers) - Premiere UXP panel that turns typed Jev review findings on an SRT caption export into timeline markers: Jev flags issues and picks the exact caption, and every marker is added in one undoable transaction with the caption text copied byte for byte. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-premiere-markers.md).
- [Jev Router for Windows](https://github.com/pouramin/jev-router-windows) - One-click Windows GUI for TypeSafe Jev routing with Codex (jev-codex-bridge) and assisted Claude Code plugin setup. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-router-windows.md).
- [Jev Rules for WordPress](https://github.com/gbesse/wordpress-jev-rules) - Experimental WordPress plugin that queues posts for Jev classification against versioned rule packs and keeps an administrator review history; posts are never changed, and other plugins can enqueue posts and react to decisions through hooks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/wordpress-jev-rules.md).
- [Jev Runway](https://github.com/IPECTER/jev-runway) - Local Codex proxy that uses TypeSafe Jev to trim stale tool output and save tokens. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-runway.md).
- [Jev Score](https://github.com/a-Fig/jev-score) - Local CLI/web scoreboard that grades document revisions against your criteria with TypeSafe Jev via OpenRouter Decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-score.md).
- [Jev Second Brain](https://github.com/fellowship-dev/jev-second-brain) - Local-first Markdown vault CLI: FTS index, source-linked related-note suggestions, optional TypeSafe Jev judgments via Vercel AI Gateway. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-second-brain.md).
- [JEV Sees](https://github.com/CharlesFeng0314/JEV_sees) - Give TypeSafe Jev eyes: one call, many object judgments from camera/video frames. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-sees.md).
- [Jev Sift](https://github.com/kbhuw/jev-sift) - Screens files, public webpages, and tool descriptions with Jev before an agent reads selected content; requires a TypeSafe key, and upstream licensing is unspecified. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-sift.md).
- [Jev Skill Router (kelasdev)](https://github.com/kelasdev/jev-skill-router) - OpenCode plugin that uses TypeSafe Jev (two gated `/v1/systemone` calls) to inject at most one relevant `SKILL.md` into the system prompt. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kelasdev-jev-skill-router.md).
- [Jev Skills (n23eos)](https://github.com/n23eos/jev-skills) - Fourteen installable Claude Code and Codex skills plus a CLI where TypeSafe Jev makes one bounded choice (skill, model, context file, first test, bug component or plan) and the coding agent does the work; distinct from the listed jev-skills plugin. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/n23eos-jev-skills.md).
- [Jev Skills Retrieval](https://github.com/aimmetal-tech/jev-skills-retrieval) - Pi coding agent extension that retrieves skills per request: Jev scores skill metadata once per turn, and Pi then reads only the selected skills' bodies, with pinned skills and explicit `/skill:name` taking priority. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-skills-retrieval.md).
- [Jev Starter](https://github.com/simplybychris/jev-starter) - Express + React starter for small web apps that make Jev decisions via OpenRouter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-starter.md).
- [Jev Starter](https://github.com/YanfLIZi56/jev-starter) - Browser console to configure TypeSafe Jev Noul/Choice/Score questions, test live, and export Python/JS/cURL (or example JSON). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/yanflizi56-jev-starter.md).
- [Jev Support Ticket Router](https://github.com/Zafer-Liu/jev-demo-router) - Support-ticket routing demo (English/Chinese) that asks Jev department, urgency and tone in one call and sends low-confidence tickets to a human queue. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-demo-router.md).
- [Jev Symfony Bundle](https://github.com/vbcherepanov/jev-symfony-bundle) - Unofficial Symfony bundle for TypeSafe Jev: typed client, validator constraints, Messenger, Workflow guards, and profiler. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-symfony-bundle.md).
- [Jev Task Router](https://github.com/hainalstefan-coder/Jev_Router) - npm CLI that asks Jev, through the AI SDK and Vercel AI Gateway, which eligible coding model should handle a task, then hands the task to your local Codex or GitHub Copilot CLI session after you confirm. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-task-router.md).
- [Jev the Janitor](https://github.com/kylehovance-ai/jev-the-janitor) - Markdown vault janitor: TypeSafe Jev votes on each note; code adds frontmatter or quarantines secrets (dry-run default; offline mode). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-the-janitor.md).
- [Jev Trader](https://github.com/jarrodwatts/jev-trader) - Studies Jev market-direction choices, simulated fills, and on-chain order execution through a Bun trading experiment. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-trader.md).
- [JEV Trading Command Center](https://github.com/mars-tw/jev-trading-command-center) - Paper-trading research app (README in Traditional Chinese) where Jev decisions pass validation and risk checks before a simulated broker acts, shown in an animated 3D command center with model 'pets' and an offline demo that uses only synthetic data. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-trading-command-center.md).
- [Jev Ultrafast](https://github.com/browser-use/jev-ultrafast) - Selects browser operations and observed targets with Jev, using a separate text model for text entry. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-ultrafast.md).
- [Jev Unpack](https://github.com/lucaminudel/jev-unpack-hook) - Codex/Claude Code Stop hook where Jev detects multi-topic, multi-ask agent messages and makes the agent pace them one ask at a time. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-unpack-hook.md).
- [Jev Vampire Survivors](https://github.com/oldmoldycake/jev_vampire_survivors) - TypeSafe Jev picks character, stage, level-ups, and walk direction in real Steam Vampire Survivors. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-vampire-survivors.md).
- [Jev vehicle classifier (Korean 12-class traffic survey)](https://github.com/kh20134/jev-vehicle-classifier) - Episode 1 of a Korean ITS series: vision-style features (axles, units, trailer type, load tons) become a JSON state and Jev picks one of the 12 official Korean road-traffic vehicle classes with probabilities; low confidence goes to an unclassified bucket for review (README in Korean). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-vehicle-classifier.md).
- [Jev Vision Benchmarks](https://github.com/MaceTenth/jev-vision-benchmarks) - Benchmarks of Jev 1.14 image input on marketplace tasks (product-photo moderation, photo-to-listing matching, classification, package-label reading) against GPT vision models, AWS Rekognition and OpenAI moderation, with checked-in labels and results. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-vision-benchmarks.md).
- [Jev Voice Browser](https://github.com/moritzkremb/jev-voice-browser) - Experiments with typed Jev decisions over partial voice transcripts and page elements to control Playwright, with a local decision inspector. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-voice-browser.md).
- [Jev vs Basal insurance benchmark](https://github.com/marcinnowak79/jev-basal-insurance-benchmark) - Polish motor-insurance (autocasco) decision benchmark: 325 synthetic typed decisions built from public policy terms, with code-computed labels, run against Jev, Basal 2.0 and Gemini models with accuracy, calibration (ECE) and latency. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-basal-insurance-benchmark.md).
- [Jev vs. LLM (Banking77)](https://github.com/0xGondarxyz/jev-vs-llm) - Small, fair test of the "up to 200x faster" claim: 100 real Banking77 customer messages sorted into 77 categories by Jev, Claude Haiku 5.5 and Claude Fable 5.1, measuring accuracy, off-list answers, median and p90 latency, and cost. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-vs-llm-banking77.md).
- [Jev WCAG Auditor](https://github.com/ctrimm/jev-wcag-auditor) - axe-core WCAG checks plus TypeSafe Jev adjudication of judgement calls on public/.gov pages. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-wcag-auditor.md).
- [JEV WHERE?](https://github.com/cesarroger/jev-where) - Claude Code plugin that finds where Jev could make decisions in your app and writes the typed Choice/Score/Noul question for each (analysis only). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-where.md).
- [Jev Workflows for Codex and Claude Code](https://github.com/integrate-your-mind/jev-codex-plugin) - Open-source Codex and Claude Code plugin that consults Jev about tools, models, tasks, skills, context and custom decisions, diagnoses failed commands and checks completion claims against evidence; eight MCP tools, lifecycle hooks and three standalone skills. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-workflows-codex-claude.md).
- [Jev Workshop (notebooks and deck)](https://github.com/fnusatvik07/jev-workshop) - Teaching kit for Jev: five notebooks executed live against the API, a 42-slide deck with speaker notes, and a cited reference covering the primitives, confidence and common patterns. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/fnusatvik07-jev-workshop.md).
- [Jev x AI SDK Form Router](https://github.com/ashiruus/jev-and-ai-sdk) - Next.js form router that uses Jev to route submissions by context, with an LLM fallback for uncertain cases. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-and-ai-sdk.md).
- [jev-a2a (Jev router for Paseo agents)](https://github.com/skhlo/jev-a2a) - Personal-prototype router that sends work between coding agents running in Paseo sessions on several machines and keeps one record of what was asked, who took it and what came back; when a request names no recipient, TypeSafe Jev chooses the participant. Includes a web board and design docs; published as a worked example, not a product. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-a2a.md).
- [jev-adb](https://github.com/srsergi0/jev-adb) - MCP server for Android control over ADB where TypeSafe Jev makes typed UI decisions behind consent and safety gates. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-adb.md).
- [jev-advisor (gjc-plugin)](https://github.com/jjongguet/gjc-plugin) - gajae-code (gjc) plugin set whose jev-advisor asks TypeSafe Jev for fast/balanced/strong tier advice on subagent delegations, in shadow mode by default, with offline reports. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gjc-jev-advisor.md).
- [jev-advisor-gateway](https://github.com/1arley/jev-advisor-gateway) - Local gateway that routes coding-agent tool decisions through Jev and can attach a read-only advisor (GPT or Claude) without giving the advisor write authority. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-advisor-gateway.md).
- [jev-agent-browser](https://github.com/mhingston/jev-agent-browser) - Confidence-gated browser actions for `agent-browser` with TypeSafe Jev (or Gateway/Cloudflare/custom providers). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-agent-browser.md).
- [jev-agent-failure-benchmark](https://github.com/TokenTrim/jev-agent-failure-benchmark) - Benchmarks TypeSafe Jev on Who&When Pro agent-failure attribution (who/when/what) against published LLM baselines. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-agent-failure-benchmark.md).
- [jev-agent-kit](https://github.com/walidboulanouar/jev-agent-kit) - Zero-dependency CLI + MCP tools (route/triage/guard/grep/rank/compact/judge/…) on TypeSafe Jev (`@walidboulanouar/jevkit`; distinct from Rust jevkit). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-agent-kit.md).
- [jev-agent-kit (nanoDBA)](https://github.com/nanoDBA/jev-agent-kit) - Evidence-layer hooks for Claude Code/Codex/Hermes: TypeSafe Jev scores risky tool calls in shadow mode by default — distinct from walidboulanouar/jev-agent-kit. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/nanodba-jev-agent-kit.md).
- [jev-agent-router](https://github.com/CiroVilmer/jev-agent-router) - Claude Code mod that picks the model and effort of every subagent from its task type, with Jev as an optional classifier. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-agent-router.md).
- [jev-agent-skill (RosarioDiBartolo)](https://github.com/RosarioDiBartolo/jev-agent-skill) - Agent skill connecting Jev and Kev System One models to Codex and AI agents for fast tool routing, classification, and bounded decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rosariodibartolo-jev-agent-skill.md).
- [jev-agent-tools](https://github.com/jkudish/jev-agent-tools) - Jev transport/provider layer with fail-closed validation; shared by jkudish/jev-browser and jkudish/jev-mcp. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jkudish-jev-agent-tools.md).
- [jev-ai-demo (Jev primitives walkthrough)](https://github.com/georgeykalangi/jev-ai-demo) - Small Python walkthrough of Jev's Choice, Score and Noul primitives: a quickstart, atomic scoring and confidence routing examples that replay the sample responses from TypeSafe's docs without a key, then call the live API when one is set. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-ai-demo-georgeykalangi.md).
- [Jev-AI-Skill](https://github.com/King4s/Jev-AI-Skill) - Claude Code/Codex/Hermes skill + MCP: TypeSafe Jev gates for agent decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-ai-skill.md).
- [jev-ai-use-cases (atliq)](https://github.com/atliq/jev-ai-use-cases) - LangChain notebook: TypeSafe Jev for triage, routing, reply guards, tool selection, finance-inbox checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/atliq-jev-ai-use-cases.md).
- [jev-align](https://github.com/sutro-sh/jev-align) - CLI to build calibrated AI Functions from human labels using TypeSafe Jev evaluation and GEPA optimization. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-align.md).
- [jev-android](https://github.com/dougsong/jev-android) - Kotlin Android UI-agent SDK: TypeSafe Jev or DeepSeek selects accessibility actions; local Maven sample (not on Maven Central). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-android.md).
- [jev-answers](https://github.com/AmooEbrahim/jev-answers) - MCP server + CLI: send files and typed questions to TypeSafe Jev and get the raw answer back (one session folder per request). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-answers.md).
- [jev-apply](https://github.com/Noface07/SystemOne-Apply) - Job-application form filler: rules plus a decision backend (local Laya default, TypeSafe Jev selectable) choose facts/options from your profile; stops before Submit. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-apply.md).
- [jev-audit (agent skill)](https://github.com/idogoldd/skills) - Agent skill that audits a codebase for brittle fact-guessing rules and writes a ranked `JEV_OPPORTUNITIES.md` with evidence, fit scores, a replacement sketch that keeps reliable checks in code, and a measurement plan; makes no Jev calls itself. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-audit-skill.md).
- [jev-auto-approve](https://github.com/BasmaAbouzied0/jev-auto-approve) - Claude Code hook: TypeSafe Jev auto-approves read-only shell commands in milliseconds; everything else still prompts you (fail-open). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/basmaabouzied0-jev-auto-approve.md).
- [jev-autopilot](https://github.com/hmcdaniel03/jev-autopilot) - Claude Code plugin that keeps unattended sessions moving: TypeSafe Jev answers Claude's routine questions, screens every non-read-only tool call, and holds risky ones until you tap Proceed or Block on your phone via Telegram; falls back to the terminal when Jev is unreachable. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-autopilot.md).
- [jev-backed-function-calling](https://github.com/gogoymh/jev-backed-function-calling) - Thin Python layer that uses TypeSafe Jev as a verifier behind function-calling flows (`TYPESAFE_API_KEY` / `JEV_API_KEY`), so tool arguments can be checked with typed System One questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-backed-function-calling.md).
- [jev-backend-qa](https://github.com/diamitani/jev-backend-qa) - Backend QA agent with PAL/Jev adjudication to BLOCK/WARN/PASS (CLI + GitHub Action). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-backend-qa.md).
- [jev-bench (stern9)](https://github.com/stern9/jev-bench) - Benchmark harness that asks Jev and general LLMs (via system-one-adapter) the same routing, yes/no and rating questions and reports accuracy, p50/p95 latency, calibration and cost. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/stern9-jev-bench.md).
- [jev-blindspot](https://github.com/jsk4581/jev-blindspot) - Claude Code / Codex side panel: TypeSafe Jev gates whether a prompt needs a second look, then optional blind-spot cards (issue #400). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-blindspot.md).
- [jev-box-tracking](https://github.com/BenCaunt/jev-box-tracking) - Python library and webcam demo for real-time object tracking: Jev Box answers give the absolute position, optical flow tracks motion between answers, and a Kalman filter rewinds and fuses late answers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-box-tracking.md).
- [jev-broker](https://github.com/AlekseiUL/jev-broker) - Local HTTP MCP broker for Hermes agents: validate questions, call TypeSafe Jev via OpenRouter, return structured judgments (agent still decides). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/alekseiul-jev-broker.md).
- [jev-browse (cooper667)](https://github.com/cooper667/jev-browse) - Claude Code plugin: plain-English Playwright checklist QA judged by TypeSafe Jev on Cloudflare Workers AI (distinct from other jev-browse listings). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cooper667-jev-browse.md).
- [jev-browse (danielnc)](https://github.com/danielnc/jev-browse) - browser-harness helpers: TypeSafe Jev decides each click for agent-delegated website sub-tasks (distinct from cooper667). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/danielnc-jev-browse.md).
- [jev-browsecomp](https://github.com/jjd-lab/jev-browsecomp) - BrowseComp-Plus research harness: Jev Choice/Noul screening vs full-LLM/RLM pipelines with mechanical citation checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-browsecomp.md).
- [jev-browser-bridge](https://github.com/lexmount/jev-browser-bridge) - Plug any CDP browser into Jev—cloud, local, or self-hosted, including no-render engines. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-browser-bridge.md).
- [jev-browser-mcp (bothuany)](https://github.com/bothuany/jev-browser-mcp) - MCP browser: host states intent; TypeSafe Jev clicks; cheap reader sees the page; DOM stays out of context. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/bothuany-jev-browser-mcp.md).
- [jev-browser-sidekick-mcp](https://github.com/TechyAditya/jev-browser-sidekick-mcp) - MCP server: plain-language browser steps; TypeSafe/OpenRouter Jev picks page controls while Playwright MCP shares the Chrome session. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-browser-sidekick-mcp.md).
- [jev-browser-skill (ChenYCL)](https://github.com/ChenYCL/jev-browser-skill) - Browser/computer-use CLI+MCP for coding agents: TypeSafe Jev chooses actions in ego lite/Chrome/Safari (distinct from zurfyx/hqman). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/chenycl-jev-browser-skill.md).
- [jev-browser-skill (hqman)](https://github.com/hqman/jev-browser-skill) - Playwright Chromium skill/`jb` CLI driven by Jev (Vercel AI Gateway or TypeSafe); distinct from zurfyx/jev-browser-skill. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hqman-jev-browser-skill.md).
- [jev-browser-use (AuroraPixel)](https://github.com/AuroraPixel/jev-browser-use) - Jev browser loops for Codex/Claude: warm Chrome daemon, CLI/MCP/extension, host handoffs for reading/text. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/aurorapixel-jev-browser-use.md).
- [jev-calibrate](https://github.com/smkrv/jev-calibrate) - Calibrate TypeSafe Jev questions against labelled examples and get per-question gate/ranker/unusable verdicts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-calibrate.md).
- [jev-calibration](https://github.com/themsquared/jev-calibration) - Reliability diagram and ECE for TypeSafe Jev on 240 hand-labelled agent tool calls (labels + scripts published). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-calibration.md).
- [jev-cascade](https://github.com/CapCurious/jev-cascade) - Python cascade that uses Jev as a cheap, recall-tuned first stage over many questions per item, then only escalates the uncertain band to a stronger second-stage command and reports whether stage two actually improves accuracy. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-cascade.md).
- [jev-cc-codex-router](https://github.com/peterwanghot/jev-cc-codex-router) - Per-turn Codex model-routing proxy: TypeSafe Jev picks tier, rewrites the model, retries flaky upstream errors (distinct from 0xNatoshi/jev-codex-router). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-cc-codex-router.md).
- [jev-certify](https://github.com/nikkoxgonzales/jev-certify) - Conformal risk certificates and prediction-powered audits for TypeSafe Jev routing thresholds (offline tests; live via OpenRouter Decisions). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-certify.md).
- [jev-chess (byrencheema)](https://github.com/byrencheema/jev-chess) - Experiment where Jev plays chess by choosing one move from all legal moves as a single Choice per turn, with no search or filtering: 350 games vs Stockfish and Maia, 50 vs random, 1,000 Lichess puzzles, Stockfish analysis and all results committed. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/byrencheema-jev-chess.md).
- [jev-chrome-mcp](https://github.com/Pioneer113/jev-chrome-mcp) - MCP adapter running the jev-browser-use click loop in Chrome for Cursor/Codex. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-chrome-mcp.md).
- [jev-ci-pathfinder](https://github.com/JevForge/jev-ci-pathfinder) - GitHub Action: TypeSafe Jev selects which allowlisted CI jobs to run after a change; workflow YAML stays in control (distinct from jev-ci-selector). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-ci-pathfinder.md).
- [jev-ci-selector](https://github.com/guilhem/jev-ci-selector) - GitHub Action that asks TypeSafe Jev which described CI tasks apply to the current PR diff and exports boolean job outputs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-ci-selector.md).
- [jev-ci-triage](https://github.com/criguex/jev-ci-triage) - Classify failing CI tests (Playwright/JUnit) as regression, flaky, environment, test-data, or unknown—rules first, TypeSafe Jev for ambiguous cases; never reruns or masks failures. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/criguex-jev-ci-triage.md).
- [jev-claude](https://github.com/thiago-lage/jev-claude) - Python library, CLI and MCP server that sends code-facing decisions (decide/when/switch/rank) to TypeSafe Jev and human-facing text to an LLM you choose, with low-confidence answers routed to a person. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-claude.md).
- [jev-claude-code (Axiumine)](https://github.com/Axiumine/jev-claude-code) - Research catalog plus reference MCP: TypeSafe Jev yes/no triage for Claude Code via OpenRouter (distinct from DarioFontanel/weiping). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/axiumine-jev-claude-code.md).
- [jev-claude-code (DarioFontanel)](https://github.com/DarioFontanel/jev-claude-code) - Claude Code prompt pack: TypeSafe Jev model/effort routing, tool-call compaction, and thresholded diff review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dariofontanel-jev-claude-code.md).
- [jev-claude-code (weiping)](https://github.com/weiping/jev-claude-code) - Claude Code plugin: TypeSafe Jev in the agent loop for permission gate, output ladder, conditional context, and subagent routing (shadow mode default; distinct from DarioFontanel/jev-claude-code). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/weiping-jev-claude-code.md).
- [jev-claude-plugin (Jev around Claude Code tool calls)](https://github.com/zacharytgray/jev-claude-plugin) - Claude Code plugin that puts Jev at four points around tool calls: it windows long tool output and withholds irrelevant or injected spans, can replace auto mode with a hazard-based allow/deny/ask check, suggests effort and model, and can check final claims against tool results. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-claude-plugin.md).
- [jev-claude-router (Flam1ngFir3ball)](https://github.com/Flam1ngFir3ball/jev-claude-router) - Claude Code plugin fork: TypeSafe Jev picks model tier and effort per turn with cost-aware switch checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-claude-router.md).
- [jev-claude-router (sergeiboikov)](https://github.com/sergeiboikov/jev-claude-router) - MCP server for Claude Code and Claude Desktop where TypeSafe Jev recommends which Claude model and effort level suit a task, comparing candidates by live OpenRouter prices, context sizes and output limits; it recommends only and never switches the model itself. Distinct from the separately listed jev-claude-router. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sergeiboikov-jev-claude-router.md).
- [jev-claw](https://github.com/trietphan/jev-claw) - OpenClaw typed model routing with TypeSafe Jev (`jev_route`) and optional automatic enforcement. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-claw.md).
- [jev-cli (portlandhodl)](https://github.com/portlandhodl/jev-cli) - Agent-facing Rust CLI over the unofficial jev-sdk crate: predict, choose and score subcommands return JSON probabilities an agent can gate on. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/portlandhodl-jev-cli.md).
- [jev-cli (shaharia-lab)](https://github.com/shaharia-lab/jev-cli) - Rust CLI/MCP for TypeSafe Jev: noul/choice/score with exit codes for shells/CI and JSON for scripts (distinct from tumf/jev-cli). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/shaharia-lab-jev-cli.md).
- [jev-cli (shetautnetjer)](https://github.com/shetautnetjer/jev-cli) - Clean-room CLI for TypeSafe System One Decision Contracts, search, and benchmarks (distinct from tumf/shaharia-lab jev-cli). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/shetautnetjer-jev-cli.md).
- [jev-cli (tumf)](https://github.com/tumf/jev-cli) - Unofficial PyPI CLI + stdio MCP for TypeSafe Jev noul/choice/score (distinct from okooo5km/jev, typesafe-cli, typesafeai-cli). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tumf-jev-cli.md).
- [jev-click-bench](https://github.com/arthurkatcher/jev-click-bench) - Benchmark of Jev 1.14's `box` tool as a GUI click model on ScreenSpot-v2 and ScreenSpot-Pro with the official scoring: 84% on ordinary screens, 19% on 4K pro apps, and 43% to 60% on Pro after one or two zoomed second looks, with every request logged. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-click-bench.md).
- [jev-cloud-cost-guardian](https://github.com/JevForge/jev-cloud-cost-guardian) - GitHub Action FinOps gate: TypeSafe Jev evaluates cloud spend vs budget (`approve`/`warn`/`block`/`manual-review`) without mutating infra. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-cloud-cost-guardian.md).
- [jev-cmdline-classifier](https://github.com/liodali/jev-cmdline-classifier) - Agent skill/CLI that classifies shell commands with TypeSafe Jev Choice into allow/prompt/forbidden (advisory; sandbox stays authoritative). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-cmdline-classifier.md).
- [jev-code-mode](https://github.com/acoyfellow/jev-code-mode) - Typed Jev judgments behind two MCP tools: search and execute. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-code-mode.md).
- [jev-codex-router](https://github.com/0xNatoshi/jev-codex-router) - Routes each Codex turn through Jev tier and thinking-depth selection via a local Codex Router generic provider, with fail-open fallbacks and an optional Codex-dry tandem. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-codex-router.md).
- [jev-codex-token-saver](https://github.com/jcressler/jev-codex-token-saver) - Codex plugin/MCP that scores bounded local evidence with TypeSafe Jev and returns selected exact excerpts (local fallback when Jev is unavailable). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-codex-token-saver.md).
- [jev-commit](https://github.com/valentynkit/jev-commit) - pre-commit hook that uses one Jev call to check the commit message against the staged diff and flag leftovers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-commit.md).
- [jev-community-ops](https://github.com/FirasB9/jev-community-ops) - Community triage for a developer community on TypeSafe Jev: typed questions, confidence-gated routing, weekly digest (simulate-friendly until early-access key). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/firasb9-jev-community-ops.md).
- [jev-compact](https://github.com/fatelei/jev-compact) - Codex CLI plugin: TypeSafe Jev scores tool calls before compaction and restores critical outputs verbatim afterward (distinct from fast-jev-compaction for Claude Code). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-compact.md).
- [jev-compaction (Waxmell114514)](https://github.com/Waxmell114514/jev-compaction) - Context compactor that can only score, never write—so agent memory cannot invent transcript facts (offline demo; distinct from fast-jev-compaction). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/waxmell114514-jev-compaction.md).
- [jev-compaction-plus](https://github.com/cth9191/jev-compaction-plus) - Claude Code compaction via TypeSafe Jev keep/drop (~0.5s): retained text stays verbatim; dropped tool outputs move to a drawer file (fork of fast-jev-compaction). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-compaction-plus.md).
- [jev-computer-use-skill (Mrchen116)](https://github.com/Mrchen116/jev-computer-use-skill) - Codex/agent skill that delegates repetitive computer-use judgments to TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mrchen116-jev-computer-use-skill.md).
- [jev-context-filter-harness](https://github.com/zacharytgray/jev-context-filter-harness) - Paper code and run records for screening LLM agents' tool output with Jev: a relevance filter keeps chunks that answer the agent's question and an injection filter withholds planted instructions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-context-filter-harness.md).
- [jev-controller](https://github.com/ac-kurniawan/jev-controller) - Fail-open advisory post-tool controller for OMP (oh-my-pi): asks TypeSafe Jev whether to inspect, edit, test, or ask the user—leaves the tool result unchanged if Jev is unavailable. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ac-kurniawan-jev-controller.md).
- [jev-cops](https://github.com/FrancoisChastel/jev-cops) - Context-aware tool-call policing for coding agents with graduated Jev-backed verdicts (allow→kill). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-cops.md).
- [jev-corrective-rag](https://github.com/sudeshkar/jev-corrective-rag) - Corrective RAG demo: TypeSafe Jev typed gates for triage/grading/verify; generative LLM only writes answers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-corrective-rag.md).
- [jev-crowdsim](https://github.com/gbesse/jev-crowdsim) - Test a message across an explicit grid of audience traits: Jev returns finite reactions (engage, trust, convert, main objection) for each declared combination, and code computes main effects and Wilson intervals instead of one average. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-crowdsim.md).
- [Jev-cu](https://github.com/Sac-Y/Jev-cu) - Experimental Codex skill and JavaScript runtime for selecting macOS Accessibility targets with Jev, with action previews and optional result verification; execution-policy limitations require review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-cu.md).
- [jev-cu-win](https://github.com/WorkFrameHub/jev-cu-win) - Windows port of Jev-cu (docs in Japanese and English): a Codex skill where Jev reads the UI Automation text of a window and picks the next element to click or type into, with dry runs by default. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-cu-win.md).
- [jev-datafusion](https://github.com/parable-work/jev-datafusion) - Rust crate that runs Jev decisions (noul, choice, score, ask) as Apache DataFusion SQL functions, so you can score tickets, route email or question meeting notes at warehouse scale. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-datafusion.md).
- [jev-debtgate](https://github.com/smlayero/jev-debtgate) - Technical-debt gate for coding agents and CI: local metrics plus TypeSafe Jev typed questions and confidence policy (BYOK). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-debtgate.md).
- [jev-decide (abdullahaamuda)](https://github.com/abdullahaamuda-code/jev-decide) - Agent skill + stdlib Python CLI: offload page-state Choice/Noul/Score judgments to TypeSafe Jev for browser/desktop automation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/abdullahaamuda-jev-decide.md).
- [jev-decision-harness](https://github.com/chhetri123/jev-decision-harness) - Claude Code plugin adding typed Jev decisions for command-risk gating, triage and review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-decision-harness.md).
- [jev-decision-kit (kdcadmin)](https://github.com/kdcadmin/jev-decision-kit) - Local skill/plugin cabinet: an on-device Jev head selects which skills to preface before the chat model speaks (distinct from DecisionKit .NET). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kdcadmin-jev-decision-kit.md).
- [jev-decisions (wonghanz)](https://github.com/wonghanz/jev-decisions) - Typed decision layer for backend engineering (log triage, incident routing, PR triage, deploy risk) with a control-group eval harness via OpenRouter Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/wonghanz-jev-decisions.md).
- [jev-dev-demo (LIDR)](https://github.com/LIDR-academy/jev-dev-demo) - Spanish-language teaching repo with three runnable demos of 'Jev decides, the LLM reasons': Jira triage that classifies, prioritises and assigns each ticket with a confidence and escalates ambiguous ones, a pre-flight scan that flags existing bugs in the functions a ticket touches, and an LLM router that sends each PR to a large, small or no model (upstream reports 50–75% lower cost); every demo has a `--mock` mode. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-dev-demo.md).
- [jev-doc-search](https://github.com/VectifyAI/jev-doc-search) - Find answering pages in long PDFs with TypeSafe Jev Choice over a PageIndex tree (no vector DB). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-doc-search.md).
- [jev-docs](https://github.com/asuma312/jev-docs) - Claude Code hook that asks Jev which pages in your project's docs folder matter for each prompt and adds a short reading list to the context; routine prompts get nothing. Includes a skill that writes docs in the format the hook reads best. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-docs-hook.md).
- [jev-dom](https://github.com/eralabs-ai/jev-dom) - TypeSafe Jev operates any web page through its DOM action space (no WebMCP required); Playwright peer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-dom.md).
- [jev-dotnet](https://github.com/ukashanoor/jev-dotnet) - Unofficial typed C# client for the Jev System One API (Choice/Score/Noul). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-dotnet.md).
- [jev-dotnet (CMaintz)](https://github.com/CMaintz/jev-dotnet) - Unofficial zero-dependency .NET client for TypeSafe Jev Choice/Score/Noul (distinct from ukashanoor/jev-dotnet). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cmaintz-jev-dotnet.md).
- [jev-drone](https://github.com/RomanSlack/jev-drone) - Explores Jev tactical judgments in a MuJoCo quadrotor simulation, with local flight control and a separate experimental tunnel policy. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-drone.md).
- [jev-effort](https://github.com/ifoster01/jev-effort) - Unofficial Claude Code helper: TypeSafe Jev picks per-step reasoning effort and lease (OpenRouter/TypeSafe/Vercel). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-effort.md).
- [jev-effort-router](https://github.com/AlphaPerseii3000/jev-effort-router) - Hermes plugin for Ollama:Cloud: TypeSafe Jev routes both model and reasoning effort per turn via `llm_request` rewrite. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-effort-router.md).
- [jev-effort-router (hasanzaman22)](https://github.com/hasanzaman22/jev-effort-router) - Claude Code plugin that asks Jev how much thinking each prompt needs and how risky it is, then runs the turn at the lowest effort level likely to get it right, leaning toward more effort when Jev is unsure. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hasanzaman22-jev-effort-router.md).
- [jev-emoji](https://github.com/DCPRevere/jev-emoji) - Dependency-free npm package that picks or ranks an emoji for a piece of text with a Jev Choice call, from a curated list of about 250, the full Unicode set or your own list, with a confidence fallback. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-emoji.md).
- [jev-enforce](https://github.com/erkamyaman/jev-enforce) - Claude Code plugin: TypeSafe Jev checks every reply and file edit against CLAUDE.md / AGENTS.md rules and blocks broken rules in-turn. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-enforce.md).
- [jev-eval-harness](https://github.com/Joshua7792/jev-eval-harness) - Pre-registered research harness testing whether Jev's typed market judgments beat trivial baselines on hourly BTC/USD, with sealed holdout, purge, deflated Sharpe and bootstrap CIs; the measured answer was no. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-eval-harness.md).
- [jev-eyes](https://github.com/LeddoEngano/jev-eyes) - Local OCR/layout `see()` (and optional `ask()`) so TypeSafe Jev decides over inspectable image-derived text state. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-eyes.md).
- [jev-fabric](https://github.com/fabric-runtime/jev-fabric) - Single native executable (written in Bend) that owns an agent's long-running processes with deadlines and bounded logs, and asks Jev a typed choice/noul/score question only when explicitly told to. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-fabric.md).
- [jev-factcheck](https://github.com/maksym771/jev-factcheck) - Agent skill (English and Ukrainian) that asks Jev whether a verbatim source excerpt supports, contradicts or says nothing about a specific claim, keeping provenance. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-factcheck.md).
- [jev-fanout-bench](https://github.com/blowxian/jev-fanout-bench) - Reproducible measurement that N TypeSafe Jev questions in one call bill shared state once (2,976 requests, raw JSONL). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-fanout-bench.md).
- [jev-feels](https://github.com/Qew7/jev-feels) - Ruby gem for TypeSafe Jev as `feels?` / `decide` / `score` and Rails validations (distinct from BAML feelings and ruby_decision_model). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-feels.md).
- [jev-filter (ByteBell)](https://github.com/ByteBell/jev-filter) - Claude Code plugin: after grep, TypeSafe Jev noul-filters which files matter (~1% of LLM file-triage cost per upstream bench). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/bytebell-jev-filter.md).
- [jev-flight-agent](https://github.com/kallurayaankit/jev-flight-agent) - Browser-use flight search demo: TypeSafe Jev picks DOM actions; small LLM types text only (Playwright/CDP). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-flight-agent.md).
- [jev-for-all](https://github.com/emirbartu/jev-for-all) - Shared TypeSafe Jev decision contract for OpenCode, Claude Code, and Hermes (skill routing, tool subset, browser). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-for-all.md).
- [jev-foundation-models](https://github.com/peterfriese/jev-foundation-models) - Swift 6 bridge: TypeSafe Jev as an Apple Foundation Models `LanguageModel` for `@Generable` decisions (distinct from TypeSafe Swift client). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-foundation-models.md).
- [Jev-Frame](https://github.com/chayan-bit/Jev-Frame) - Python framework for typed, auditable Jev decisions in agents: describe judgments, candidate sets, completion contracts and budgets, and it compiles the Jev questions, tracks evidence with provenance and returns a supported typed result or an explicit unresolved outcome. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/chayan-jev-frame.md).
- [jev-frontier-bench](https://github.com/manjunathshiva/jev-frontier-bench) - Reproducible typed-decision benchmark: TypeSafe Jev 1.13 vs Claude/GPT/Kimi/MiniMax/DeepSeek on 200 items (accuracy, calibration, cost). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-frontier-bench.md).
- [jev-fuse](https://github.com/0xshikhar/jev-fuse) - Governed execution/safety-gate/audit proxy for TypeSafe Jev and local Laya engines. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-fuse.md).
- [jev-gate (MongLong0214)](https://github.com/MongLong0214/jev-gate) - Claude Code plugin: TypeSafe Jev Gate/Router/Evidence plus Compact/Output (no LICENSE file; ≠ Neoo-Blue jev-gate). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/monglong0214-jev-gate.md).
- [jev-gate (Neoo-Blue)](https://github.com/Neoo-Blue/jev-gate) - Claude Code plugin: TypeSafe Jev checks the plan vs your request before edits, then reviews the final summary until gaps are closed. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/neoo-blue-jev-gate.md).
- [jev-gates](https://github.com/carlchou0dailyfresh/jev-gates) - Composable three-valued semantic logic circuits from TypeSafe Jev judgments and exact rules, with mock/offline demos (distinct from typesafe-agent-gates). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-gates.md).
- [jev-gateway](https://github.com/vinilana/jev-gateway) - Local LLM gateway that asks TypeSafe Jev which tool to call for Codex, Claude Code, OpenCode, or Gemini while other traffic uses your usual LLM. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-gateway.md).
- [jev-gateway (TexasOct)](https://github.com/TexasOct/jev-gateway) - OpenAI-compatible session-aware model-routing gateway with optional typed Choice decision providers (AGPL; distinct from vinilana/jev-gateway). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/texasoct-jev-gateway.md).
- [jev-gateway-bench](https://github.com/vinilana/jev-gateway-bench) - Benchmark suite for vinilana/jev-gateway: coding agents on chess-engine tasks with Jev routing on vs off. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-gateway-bench.md).
- [jev-gen-detect (AI-generated image detector)](https://github.com/eforus-overseer/jev-gen-detect) - Rust web UI and CLI that asks Jev Image whether a picture is generated or authentic, or which generator made it, and asks Jev Box for the most telling artifact region, showing probabilities, latency and cost. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-gen-detect.md).
- [jev-git-graph](https://github.com/CondorCommodore/jev-git-graph) - Evidence-backed TypeSafe Jev relationship graph for Git branch consolidation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-git-graph.md).
- [jev-governor](https://github.com/roma-vibe/jev-governor) - Claude Code mod for Jev-guided model/effort routing, verbatim context compaction, and output trimming. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-governor.md).
- [jev-ground](https://github.com/johnyoonh/jev-ground) - Evidence-grounded factuality verification for LLM and agent outputs using a Jev provider. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-ground.md).
- [jev-guard](https://github.com/leepokai/jev-guard) - Coding-agent security hooks: TypeSafe Jev risk-scores tool calls with session context (deny/ask/allow), flags prompt injection, and scans skills across Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, and more. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-guard.md).
- [jev-guard (CMaintz)](https://github.com/CMaintz/jev-guard) - Portable TypeSafe Jev allow/block/hold guard for agent tool calls with LangChain and Vercel AI SDK adapters (distinct from leepokai/jev-guard). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cmaintz-jev-guard.md).
- [jev-guard (klauswg)](https://github.com/klauswg/jev-guard) - Exchange deposit/withdrawal risk triage: TypeSafe Jev judges; Java code adjudicates (distinct from leepokai/CMaintz jev-guard). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/klauswg-jev-guard.md).
- [jev-guard (muratcakmak)](https://github.com/muratcakmak/jev-guard) - Claude Code hooks: TypeSafe Jev probability-scored rules deny bad edits/deploys; fail-open if scorer unavailable. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/muratcakmak-jev-guard.md).
- [jev-guard (rudra72r)](https://github.com/rudra72r/jev-guard) - Python LLM-app guardrails library: TypeSafe Jev (or local/Cloudflare backends) scores inputs/outputs with allow/review/block policies—distinct from leepokai/jev-guard agent hooks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rudra72r-jev-guard.md).
- [jev-guard (yelkhanyergali-sys)](https://github.com/yelkhanyergali-sys/jev-guard) - PI Mono extension: in-flight terminal pruning (prompt-cache safe) and surgical diff guard using TypeSafe Jev—distinct from rudra72r/leepokai jev-guard variants. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/yelkhanyergali-sys-jev-guard.md).
- [jev-guardbench](https://github.com/dfranco-projects/jev-guardbench) - Bench: can TypeSafe Jev / open-source Kev replace LLM-as-judge in agent guardrail callbacks? (license unspecified). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dfranco-projects-jev-guardbench.md).
- [jev-guardrail-benchmark](https://github.com/randilt/jev-guardrail-benchmark) - Benchmark of TypeSafe Jev content-safety guardrails in WSO2 AI Gateway vs Azure Content Safety and an LLM judge. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-guardrail-benchmark.md).
- [jev-guardrails (deepansh-saxena)](https://github.com/deepansh-saxena/jev-guardrails) - Eval harness: same 25 guardrail rules on a mock support agent comparing LLM-as-judge JSON vs TypeSafe Jev (license unspecified). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/deepansh-saxena-jev-guardrails.md).
- [jev-harness](https://github.com/AntonioCoppe/jev-harness) - TypeScript decision harness: confidence-gated TypeSafe Jev policies, shadow mode, recipes, and `jev-eval` (distinct from super-jev / jev-layer). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-harness.md).
- [jev-harness (Adityakk9031)](https://github.com/Adityakk9031/jev-harness) - Tests a coding-agent setup: replays disguised dangerous commands against your hooks and permission rules, checks whether a gate's confidence is calibrated, probes instruction loss after compaction and compares AI reviewers, with Jev as an optional judge or gate. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/adityakk-jev-harness.md).
- [jev-harness (Jev judgment gates for Claude Code)](https://github.com/ysys143/jev-harness) - Claude Code plugin with judgment gates: a Stop gate that catches turns ending by announcing work not yet done, and tool gates that keep secret files away from non-Anthropic providers, with rules first, Jev only where a rule cannot decide, and shadow mode before enforcement. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ysys143-jev-harness.md).
- [jev-harness (TypeSafeAI)](https://github.com/TypeSafeAI/jev-harness) - Research proposal-review harness: LLM proposes, Jev answers narrow questions, code leaves host evidence (TypeSafeAI community; distinct from AntonioCoppe/jev-harness). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafeai-jev-harness.md).
- [jev-healthcare-lab](https://github.com/JuneYaooo/jev-healthcare-lab) - Open Jev vs DeepSeek comparison on 96 healthcare tasks across 12 scenarios (quality/latency/cost). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-healthcare-lab.md).
- [jev-herdr](https://github.com/alexzfe/jev-herdr) - Spawn Claude Code agents in Herdr with TypeSafe Jev choosing model and effort per agent. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-herdr.md).
- [jev-hook-guard](https://github.com/tsetse012/jev-hook-guard) - Standard-library Python module and example hook implementing the checks a Claude Code hook should run before sending session text to TypeSafe Jev or another HTTP judge: kill switch, exclusion words with session pins, refusal of secret dumps, masking of keys and personal data, length cut, leftover re-check, pending log line and an https-only, no-redirect POST. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-hook-guard.md).
- [jev-hooks](https://github.com/7hemas7er/jev-hooks) - Claude Code commit-reviewer plugins driven by typed `/v1/systemone` decisions (self-hosted rizzo-flow or TypeSafe Jev) with a measured question bench. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-hooks.md).
- [jev-humanizer](https://github.com/snjrusmn/jev-humanizer) - Anti-slop skill: TypeSafe Jev flags AI-sounding paragraphs so the writing model rewrites only those spans (RU-focused humanizer). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/snjrusmn-jev-humanizer.md).
- [jev-imdb-benchmark](https://github.com/rahulbansalc6414/jev-imdb-benchmark) - Benchmark TypeSafe Jev on IMDB reviews (sentiment/spoilers/quality) with published accuracy, latency, and cost. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-imdb-benchmark.md).
- [jev-in-codex](https://github.com/teempai/jev-in-codex) - Codex MCP tools for TypeSafe Jev-ranked capability selection, workspace search, and output triage (experimental MVP). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-in-codex.md).
- [jev-inbox-triage](https://github.com/pentasir/jev-inbox-triage) - Store-inbox triage where TypeSafe Jev answers eight parallel questions per email (topic, urgency, sentiment and more) and rules route it to one of four lanes with every decision logged; the author reports 19/20 test emails in the right lane and no risky email automated. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-inbox-triage.md).
- [jev-inference-router](https://github.com/sunilmehta-si/jev-inference-router) - Observable TypeSafe Jev decision routing with local Apple Silicon LLM inference, retrieval, and reproducible evaluations. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-inference-router.md).
- [jev-intent-benchmark](https://github.com/minitru/jev-intent-benchmark) - Research check for indirect prompt injection at the moment an agent acts: asks Jev whether the act carries out something a tool result asked for but the user did not, with a benchmark script on InjecAgent. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-intent-benchmark.md).
- [jev-interceptor](https://github.com/rayopavri/jev-setup) - Claude Code `UserPromptSubmit` hook (repo `jev-setup`) that asks TypeSafe Jev a few typed questions about every prompt and tells Claude where the work should run — on Claude or an open-model lane (ClinePass via `pi`, or Codex) — returning the answer as `additionalContext`; a one-command installer also sets up companion skills, a fast Jev compaction plugin and a spend log, with OpenRouter as a backup path. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-interceptor.md).
- [jev-issue-radar](https://github.com/Patrick-SCH03/jev-issue-radar) - Local read-only dashboard: TypeSafe Jev (OpenRouter) classifies GitHub issue duplicates with evidence passages for maintainer review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-issue-radar.md).
- [jev-java](https://github.com/gudcks0305/jev-java) - Unofficial Java 17+ SDK for TypeSafe Jev with OpenRouter/Vercel adapters and optional Spring Boot starter (`jev-typesafe` 0.1.1). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-java.md).
- [jev-judge](https://github.com/00200200/jev-judge) - Pytest/Vitest-style CI evaluations of LLM and RAG outputs: declarative YAML tests (faithfulness, relevance, hallucination, guardrails) answered by TypeSafe Jev noul/score questions instead of an LLM-as-a-judge, with JSON/Markdown reports and an MCP server. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-judge.md).
- [jev-judge-mcp (PyModel)](https://github.com/PyModel/jev-judge-mcp) - Typed judgment MCP tools over TypeSafe Jev; policy decides auto/review/escalate (distinct from gecm0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pymodel-jev-judge-mcp.md).
- [jev-judge-rank](https://github.com/tsetse012/jev-judge-rank) - Three standard-library Python scripts (and an agent skill) that use TypeSafe Jev to rank a list on several written criteria, merge near-duplicates under one representative, and recall the corpus entries that help with a one-sentence situation; every request and response is logged locally, with a recorded example run and its costs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-judge-rank.md).
- [jev-lab (gregoryjayuk)](https://github.com/gregoryjayuk/jev-lab) - Two standard-library Python experiments with Jev: a feed sieve that judges a week of posts from 500+ RSS, Atom, and YouTube feeds against a reader profile and writes a reading list, and a bookmark re-filing eval of agreement, calibration, and cost. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-lab-gregoryjayuk.md).
- [jev-layer](https://github.com/typakon4/jev-layer) - Portable System-1 decision layer for agent harnesses: host-owned capability routing, receipts/replay, optional supervision, demo/OpenRouter/TypeSafe providers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-layer.md).
- [Jev-LCT](https://github.com/gitchw/LCT) - Open System One decision engine (Looped Calibration Transformer) with Jev-compatible Choice/Noul/Score serving and HF Qwen-based checkpoints—independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-lct.md).
- [jev-lgtm](https://github.com/bobbydeveaux/jev-lgtm) - GitHub Action that asks TypeSafe Jev to approve a PR (LGTM) or flag Review Advised from the diff alone. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-lgtm.md).
- [jev-libero](https://github.com/Dimweaker/jev-libero) - Fine-grained LIBERO robot control study loop: TypeSafe Jev layered decisions with local physics previews and inspectable records. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-libero.md).
- [jev-linkedin-saved-classifier](https://github.com/kraayenjon/jev-linkedin-saved-classifier) - LinkedIn saved-posts board classified into filters by TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-linkedin-saved-classifier.md).
- [jev-lint](https://github.com/mizchi/jev-lint) - Ast-grep + TypeSafe Jev natural-language semantic linter (name/body drift, stale comments, weak tests); distinct from huntedman/JevLint. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-lint.md).
- [jev-lint (ckorhonen)](https://github.com/ckorhonen/jev-lint) - Fuzzy agent linter (jevlint.dev): hooks check agent-written code against team best-practice packs while the agent works (distinct from mizchi/jev-lint and huntedman/JevLint). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ckorhonen-jev-lint.md).
- [jev-linter-action](https://github.com/sable-inc/jev-linter-action) - GitHub Action: configurable TypeSafe Jev yes/no suites over repo files with probability thresholds (distinct from jev-ci-selector and jev-lint CLIs). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-linter-action.md).
- [Jev-Lite](https://github.com/Atulmishra22/jev-lite) - Open System One–style FastAPI decision engine (Qwen backbone) inspired by TypeSafe Jev; independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-lite.md).
- [jev-llm-guard](https://github.com/gulbaki/jev-llm-guard) - Contextual OWASP LLM Top 10 guardrail powered by TypeSafe Jev, with a Turkish interactive demo. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-llm-guard.md).
- [jev-llm-router (Pi)](https://github.com/emo333/jev-llm-router) - Pi extension that uses Jev to select a model and thinking level for each user task, keeps the pair through tool continuations, and supports subagents selecting `jev/auto`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/emo333-jev-llm-router.md).
- [jev-local](https://github.com/7n1m4/jev) - Local CPU-only Jev-style 0.8B decision service plus an oh-my-pi extension that points the agent's `typesafe` judge provider at it, reading yes/no logits from llama.cpp. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-local-7n1m4.md).
- [jev-loop (King4s)](https://github.com/King4s/jev-loop) - TypeSafe Jev decides, Claude Code or Hermes builds—MCP server + skill (distinct from lvzhaobo/jev-loop). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/king4s-jev-loop.md).
- [jev-macos-loop](https://github.com/jcpsimmons/jev-macos-loop) - Native Apple silicon macOS computer-use agent: local OmniParser/Vision/Accessibility perception with text-only Jev action choice (BYOK Vercel/OpenRouter/TypesafeAI). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-macos-loop.md).
- [jev-map](https://github.com/krishhgg/jev-map) - Maps each Python function to the tests that run it for coding agents; optional TypeSafe Jev scores candidate links that static analysis misses, checked against real test runs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-map.md).
- [jev-max](https://github.com/saloni-garg/jev-max) - Jev browser agent aimed at pages ordinary Jev agents miss: a piercing snapshot walks open shadow roots and same-origin iframes into one indexed element table, a vision fallback handles canvas, clicks are occlusion-checked, one Jev request per cycle picks the operation and target (TypeSafe or OpenRouter), a small LLM writes text only for fill/select, and an independent Jev check verifies DONE; ships as a Python library, CLI and MCP server. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-max.md).
- [jev-mcp](https://github.com/jkudish/jev-mcp) - MCP server with ten purpose-built TypeSafe Jev judgment tools (verify, screen, find, classify, review, gate, and more). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-mcp.md).
- [jev-mcp (Afloat16)](https://github.com/Afloat16/jev-mcp) - Unofficial conservative MCP server for TypeSafe AI Jev (stdio) so agents can ask typed System One questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/afloat16-jev-mcp.md).
- [jev-mcp (drmedia)](https://github.com/drmedia/jev-mcp) - General-purpose MCP server for System One decision models: MCP clients ask typed yes/no, choice and score questions about structured state and get calibrated probabilities from TypeSafe Jev, OpenRouter (Jev, Cloudflare Clef) or a local server, with batching, retries, image input notes, stdio/HTTP transports and Docker. Distinct from the separately listed jev-mcp. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/drmedia-jev-mcp.md).
- [jev-mcp (legostin)](https://github.com/legostin/jev-mcp) - MCP server: Claude/Codex agents drive real Chrome with TypeSafe Jev (~100 ms typed decisions, confidence gates, traces); distinct from jkudish/jev-mcp. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/legostin-jev-mcp.md).
- [jev-mcp (pyck-ai)](https://github.com/pyck-ai/jev-mcp) - Fourteen fail-closed MCP tools over Jev via OpenRouter System One (`jev_score`, `jev_gate`, `jev_ask`, …)—distinct from markylaredo/openjev-mcp. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pyck-ai-jev-mcp.md).
