- [jev-mcp (rules over OpenRouter)](https://github.com/achapla/jev-mcp) - Personal MCP server that lets Claude and other assistants ask Jev yes/no and multiple-choice rules written in a YAML file, returning a decision with a probability and flagging uncertain results for the user. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/achapla-jev-mcp.md).
- [jev-mcp-router (ini8labs)](https://github.com/ini8labs/jev-mcp-router) - MCP gateway where TypeSafe Jev answers yes/no per server and tool, so the agent LLM only sees the tools a request needs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ini8labs-jev-mcp-router.md).
- [jev-mcp-server](https://github.com/wangkuangkuang/jev-mcp-server) - Python MCP server for official TypeSafe Jev choice/score/noul plus compare/verify/classify and a one-command client installer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-mcp-server.md).
- [Jev-Mem](https://github.com/libingzheren/Jev-Mem) - System-One–controlled agentic memory: TypeSafe Jev steers admission, multi-relational linking, and adaptive retrieval before a text model answers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-mem.md).
- [jev-mem (must-lioncho)](https://github.com/must-lioncho/jev-mem) - Memory plugin for Claude Code, Codex, and Antigravity that keeps claude-mem style fast search as a first pass and lets Jev judge which past records are relevant to your prompt, so one good record goes in or none. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/must-lioncho-jev-mem.md).
- [jev-mnist](https://github.com/kaitoy/jev-mnist) - Shows all 10,000 MNIST test digits to Jev 1.14's image input as a 0 to 9 Choice question: 92.45% accuracy at 0.17 s per image, with a confusion matrix that shows a strong bias toward answering 7. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-mnist.md).
- [Jev-Mod](https://github.com/i098/jev-mod) - Open-source Discord moderation bot where TypeSafe Jev rules detect spam, phishing, harassment, hate, threats and explicit text, with log/delete/timeout actions, local phrase and mention filters, exceptions and a case dashboard; use the hosted service with your own TypeSafe key or self-host. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-mod.md).
- [jev-mod (VictorGambarini)](https://github.com/VictorGambarini/jev-mod) - Claude Code mod that hands small decisions to a decision model — Jev via TypeSafe, OpenRouter, Venice or OpenCode Zen (or your own server): per-turn routing lanes that set effort and model, the one installed skill a prompt needs, screening of AI-directed instructions after WebFetch / WebSearch / MCP / fetching Bash, and `/compact-jev`, a summary-free compaction keeping only turns marked *keep*. Every decision fails open. Distinct from the listed Discord bot *Jev-Mod (undeemed)*. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/victorgambarini-jev-mod.md).
- [jev-model-router (m-esm)](https://github.com/m-esm/jev-model-router) - Claude Code hook where TypeSafe Jev scores each prompt and picks model and effort (distinct from the claude-code-templates mod). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/m-esm-jev-model-router.md).
- [jev-navigator](https://github.com/ajbmachon/jev-navigator) - Python code-navigation library and `jvn` CLI for agents: a model-free index (definitions, callers, references, imports, git history) plus small typed Jev judgments — check, pick, score — composed into searches like “find the code this sentence describes”. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-navigator.md).
- [jev-netlify-mcp](https://github.com/NighCross/jev-netlify-mcp) - Two parts for using TypeSafe Jev from Claude Code, Codex CLI, Claude Desktop or any MCP client: a key-locked Netlify function that exposes `POST /v1/systemone` and `GET /v1/models` through Netlify AI Gateway (which injects the gateway credentials, so no TypeSafe key is needed), and an MCP server with `jev_evaluate` / `jev_models` tools plus question-writing instructions; it also works directly against TypeSafe with your own key. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-netlify-mcp.md).
- [jev-oas-sentinel](https://github.com/ShuhanSun/jev-oas-sentinel) - Compares OpenAPI documents with deterministic structural checks plus TypeSafe Jev semantic questions; policy code owns pass/review/block. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-oas-sentinel.md).
- [Jev-Omni](https://huggingface.co/akhilaaa3/Jev-Omni) - Open multimodal System One–style decision classifier (text/image/audio/video → option probabilities) fine-tuned from Gemma 4 12B IT; independent of official Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-omni.md).
- [jev-omni.js](https://github.com/ai-ecoverse/jev-omni.js) - Run independent Jev-Omni decision classifier in-browser on WebGPU (onnxruntime-web; text+images). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-omni-js.md).
- [jev-ood-calibration](https://github.com/scienthoon/jev-ood-calibration) - Independent TypeSafe Jev calibration study with published raw responses: three public benches plus 900 OOD synthetic tickets. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-ood-calibration.md).
- [jev-opus](https://github.com/WXK-AI/jev-opus) - Claude Opus 5.5 with TypeSafe Jev re-picking effort every step via per-message statements (prompt-cache friendly). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-opus.md).
- [Jev-Orchestrated Decision Mesh (CIDM)](https://github.com/kentilaok/Jev-Orchestrated-Decision-Mesh) - Training-free orchestration design where Jev chooses the next unit and reviews each output across a five-unit network, with a Codex CLI broker and skill. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-orchestrated-decision-mesh.md).
- [jev-orchestrator (milan6190)](https://github.com/milan6190/jev-orchestrator) - Workflow orchestration for System One models: batch, gate, trace, and calibrate thousands of typed Jev decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/milan6190-jev-orchestrator.md).
- [jev-packs](https://github.com/dtduc-git/jev-packs) - Evidence-gated Jev question-pack registry with golden cases and a reproducible offline multi-backend scoreboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-packs.md).
- [jev-permission-gate (madisonrickert)](https://github.com/madisonrickert/jev-permission-gate) - Claude Code auto-mode tool-call gate using TypeSafe Jev to allow, deny, or defer to the built-in classifier. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/madisonrickert-jev-permission-gate.md).
- [jev-phone](https://github.com/Rajmeet/jev-phone) - Phone agent: TypeSafe Jev picks indexed UI actions; phone-use executes on iOS Simulator, Android, or cloud phones (BYOK via AI Gateway). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-phone.md).
- [jev-php (f-lombardo)](https://github.com/f-lombardo/jev-php) - PHP client library for TypeSafe Jev / System One APIs (LGPL-2.1). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/f-lombardo-jev-php.md).
- [jev-pi (weiping)](https://github.com/weiping/jev-pi) - pi package port of weiping/jev-claude-code: TypeSafe Jev permission gate, output ladder, conditional context, and agent router (extension + skills + prompts). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/weiping-jev-pi.md).
- [jev-pi-token-reduction](https://github.com/auschoi96/jev-pi-token-reduction) - Pi coding-agent extension: TypeSafe Jev trims tool-output chunks before the model sees them (~15% cost on held-out reads). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-pi-token-reduction.md).
- [jev-pii-checker](https://github.com/coo-quack/jev-pii-checker) - CLI that finds PII spans and sensitivity with TypeSafe Jev plus regex/segmentation layers (scanned text leaves the host). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-pii-checker.md).
- [jev-pilot (Akramovic1)](https://github.com/Akramovic1/jev-pilot) - Claude Code plugin: TypeSafe Jev picks effort, subagent model, strategy, and one skill per prompt (distinct from JevPilot driving demo). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/akramovic1-jev-pilot.md).
- [jev-pilot (wangzhezbz)](https://github.com/wangzhezbz/jev-pilot) - All-in-one Codex plugin: TypeSafe Jev for reasoning-effort routing, context filtering, and workflow assistance on macOS/Windows/Linux (distinct from Akramovic1/jev-pilot). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/wangzhezbz-jev-pilot.md).
- [jev-playground](https://github.com/frederico-kluser/jev-playground) - OpenRouter workbench for TypeSafe Jev: typed noul/choice/score questions with calibrated probabilities, latency/tokens/cost metrics, and Postman/cURL export. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/frederico-kluser-jev-playground.md).
- [jev-plays](https://github.com/mansicer/jev-plays) - TypeSafe Jev plays Craftax: macro/raw action Choice with optional LLM planner-as-facts and a local web UI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-plays.md).
- [jev-plays-runescape](https://github.com/vhsgreed/jev-plays-runescape) - Archived hobby demo where Jev chooses woodcutting and banking actions on a local 2004Scape server, with live probabilities and local safety vetoes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-plays-runescape.md).
- [jev-playwright (arthurfiorette)](https://github.com/arthurfiorette/jev-playwright) - Playwright reporter: TypeSafe Jev selects which E2E tests to run from the diff (distinct owner). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/arthurfiorette-jev-playwright.md).
- [jev-poke (Jev plays Pokémon Showdown)](https://github.com/fangzipei/let-jev-play-pokemon) - Node.js bot (README in Chinese) that plays Pokémon Showdown VGC doubles: at each team preview, turn and forced switch it sends structured questions about the full battle state to Jev through OpenRouter's Decisions API, with local fallbacks and an optional advisor model. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-poke-showdown.md).
- [jev-polymarket-test](https://github.com/truongxxxx/jev-polymarket-test) - Study of whether a TypeSafe Jev Noul beats Polymarket prices on resolved markets (blind vs priced, memorisation check). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-polymarket-test.md).
- [jev-pr-judge](https://github.com/juanegido/jev-pr-judge) - Typed pull-request verdicts via one parallel TypeSafe Jev call, code-owned policy profiles, Next.js UI, and a sticky-comment GitHub Action. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-pr-judge.md).
- [jev-pr-profiler](https://github.com/JevForge/jev-pr-profiler) - GitHub Action: TypeSafe Jev PR risk profile + review-depth outputs for CI (never merges alone). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-pr-profiler.md).
- [jev-pr-quality](https://github.com/rawtreedb/jev-pr-quality) - GitHub Action for TypeSafe Jev-assisted pull request quality reviews with a live multi-repository RawTree dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-pr-quality.md).
- [jev-pref](https://github.com/doeixd/jev-pref) - Turns AGENTS.md / project preferences into a TypeSafe Jev semantic linter for coding-agent diffs (`npx jev-pref`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-pref.md).
- [jev-preflight](https://github.com/muse0509/jev-preflight) - Claude Code Stop-hook risk check: TypeSafe Jev scores eight axes over a redacted turn diff, with optional one-shot reinspection. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-preflight.md).
- [jev-project-context](https://github.com/poiuyjie/jev_project_context) - Evidence-first experiment memory skill for coding agents with optional TypeSafe Jev semantic triage. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-project-context.md).
- [jev-prompt-sentry](https://github.com/ca7ai/jev-prompt-sentry) - Anthropic Messages reverse proxy that screens jailbreaks/injections with one batched TypeSafe Jev call (PolyForm Noncommercial). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-prompt-sentry.md).
- [jev-proxy](https://github.com/baptiste-mnh/jev-proxy) - Local TypeSafe System One proxy that records, caches, and replays calls with a web inspector. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-proxy.md).
- [jev-pruner](https://github.com/tamaratran/jev-pruner) - Prunes eligible Bash stdout with Jev before it reaches Claude Code or an opt-in Codex wrapper, while archiving the original output locally. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-pruner.md).
- [jev-py-sdk](https://github.com/ogarciarevett/jev-py-sdk) - Unofficial Python SDK and CLI for typed Jev noul/choice/score judgments that masks inputs before sending. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-py-sdk.md).
- [jev-qa (moonshot-partners)](https://github.com/moonshot-partners/jev-qa) - Parallel browser QA: TypeSafe Jev drives acceptance, adversarial, and smoke verification of web changes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/moonshot-partners-jev-qa.md).
- [jev-qa-demos](https://github.com/gauravkhuraana/jev-qa-demos) - TypeScript demos teaching QA/SDET engineers TypeSafe Jev via Vercel AI Gateway (triage, guardrail, local Ollama). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-qa-demos.md).
- [jev-quant](https://github.com/itsadrianxv/jev-quant) - Early C++20 paper-trading system that calls TypeSafe Jev through OpenRouter Decisions, with SimNow/CTP, Binance and simex adapters. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-quant.md).
- [jev-query](https://github.com/jaketimothy/jev-query) - TypeScript natural-language-to-PostgreSQL composer where the model never writes SQL: code enumerates the legal moves of a typed QueryPlan, Jev (or a self-hosted logprob oracle) assigns probabilities, and code compiles parameterized SQL or asks a clarifying question. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-query.md).
- [jev-ra](https://github.com/brnyxx/jev-ra) - MCP/CLI browser-use layer for coding agents: TypeSafe Jev picks each Chrome operation and target in one round trip (OpenRouter or TypeSafe key). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-ra.md).
- [jev-rag](https://github.com/aifabrice/jev-rag) - Vector-free local RAG: SQLite BM25 retrieval, TypeSafe Jev evidence reranking, optional grounded LLM answers (no embeddings). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-rag.md).
- [jev-rag-gate](https://github.com/breaker364/jev-rag-gate) - Semantic gating for RAG retrieval candidates (relevance, premise contradiction, prompt injection) with TypeSafe Jev System One. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-rag-gate.md).
- [jev-reach (Jev walks the browser for chrome-devtools-mcp)](https://github.com/slothg/jev-reach) - Drop-in chrome-devtools-mcp server with one extra `reach` tool: your agent states a goal once and Jev picks each click to walk the browser there, then the agent makes one devtools call on the same tab instead of one model turn per click. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-reach.md).
- [jev-realtime-observability](https://github.com/silex-ai-lab/jev-realtime-observability) - Real-time agent observability with a Jev-protocol judge (open default model path). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-realtime-observability.md).
- [jev-recipes](https://github.com/agencyenterprise/jev-recipes) - 66 composable TypeScript recipes for TypeSafe Jev decisions (rerank, verify, clarify, route, and more) via the official SDK. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-recipes.md).
- [jev-reflex (xnuonux)](https://github.com/xnuonux/jev-reflex) - Portable TypeSafe Jev decision sidecar (MCP/CLI/pi) with durable budgets, duplicate-call protection, and source-bound context plans. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-reflex-xnuonux.md).
- [jev-remix-cutter](https://github.com/gnipbao/jev-remix-cutter) - Agent Skill and Python CLI for local video remixing where TypeSafe Jev judges slot fit and originality alongside constrained editing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-remix-cutter.md).
- [jev-req-gate](https://github.com/thomaszta/jev-req-gate) - Quality gate for AI-generated requirements: TypeSafe Jev MECE questions route PASS/REVIEW/BLOCK with calibrated probabilities. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-req-gate.md).
- [jev-rerank-bench](https://github.com/anessbelbati/jev-rerank-bench) - Experiments comparing TypeSafe Jev rerank recipes to Cohere and open rerankers on shared BM25 sets. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-rerank-bench.md).
- [jev-reranker](https://github.com/shinpr/jev-reranker) - Pipe JSON search candidates through TypeSafe Jev to rerank, filter evidence, or compress passages for LLM context. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-reranker.md).
- [jev-reranker (hotchpotch)](https://github.com/hotchpotch/jev-reranker) - Python RAG relevance filter/reranker with TypeSafe Jev (listwise/pointwise/pairwise); distinct from the shinpr Rust CLI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hotchpotch-jev-reranker.md).
- [jev-research-pipeline](https://github.com/shimo4228/jev-research-pipeline) - Daily research monitor: Python owns the loop; TypeSafe Jev screens papers/repos per standing question; an LLM writes Obsidian notes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-research-pipeline.md).
- [jev-risk-check-provider](https://github.com/caiovicentino/jev-risk-check-provider) - x402 risk-check provider: TypeSafe Jev typed decisions plus ES256-signed attestations facilitators can verify for agent payments. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/caiovicentino-jev-risk-check-provider.md).
- [jev-robotics-eval](https://github.com/lose4578/jev-robotics-eval) - Evaluate JEV-compatible robot control on MetaWorld and RoboTwin (text/vision, privilege levels). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-robotics-eval.md).
- [jev-role-ranker](https://github.com/CapCurious/jev-role-ranker) - Ranks job postings against a profile with TypeSafe Jev (fit, seniority, remote, sponsorship flags). Scores only sort; sponsorship refusals are flagged for a human and postings are never auto-rejected. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-role-ranker.md).
- [jev-router](https://github.com/gargpratyush/jev-router) - Routes fresh Claude Code and Codex turns through Jev model selection and deterministic fallback policy, with locally inspectable routing exchanges. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-router.md).
- [jev-router (Covre81)](https://github.com/Covre81/jev-router) - Local Anthropic-compatible gateway for Claude Code (or any Messages-API client): on each new human turn TypeSafe Jev answers one ordinal score, sending simple work to a cheap OpenAI-compatible provider (Ollama Cloud free plan by default, Groq or OpenRouter) and structural work byte-for-byte to Anthropic; tool-result turns reuse the route, Jev failures fall back to the primary, and an `x-jev-route` header plus statusline show each decision. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/covre81-jev-router.md).
- [jev-router (Ex8-ca)](https://github.com/Ex8-ca/jev-router) - Hermes plugin: TypeSafe Jev skill routing via OpenRouter with cache-safe session-start pre-route. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ex8-ca-jev-router.md).
- [jev-router (FrancoisChastel)](https://github.com/FrancoisChastel/jev-router) - Route each coding-agent turn to the cheapest model that can finish it: Switchyard-style signals + TypeSafe Jev judge for Claude Code/Codex/OpenCode/Pi (`@french-castle/jev-router`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/francoischastel-jev-router.md).
- [jev-router (hectorj2f)](https://github.com/hectorj2f/jev-router) - Route Claude Code subagents by Jev depth/breadth/noul scores; publishes where the idea fails (distinct from other jev-router*). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hectorj2f-jev-router.md).
- [jev-router (Hoppin-Sorter)](https://github.com/Hoppin-Sorter/jev-router) - Before each prompt, asks TypeSafe Jev what the task is (difficulty, subject, output kind) and which of your skills fits; code then picks the Claude or OpenAI model and optional effort with a quality/cost focus slider. Ships as a Claude Code plugin, a TypeScript/Python router library, a Codex CLI launcher and an optional macOS menu-bar widget. Distinct from the separately listed jev-router. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hoppin-sorter-jev-router.md).
- [jev-router (jjjjjjjjjjjjjjjjacob)](https://github.com/jjjjjjjjjjjjjjjjacob/jev-router) - Claude Code plugin + skills: Jev picks effort level for every prompt and the right model for every subagent; off until you type /jev. Distinct from other jev-router listings. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jjjjjjjjjjjjjjjjacob-jev-router.md).
- [jev-router (KarenCWLin1228)](https://github.com/KarenCWLin1228/jev-router) - Small Python CLI that asks TypeSafe Jev which model tier (fast / standard / deep) a coding task needs and returns a recommendation Claude Code, Codex or Antigravity can act on, plus a `jev-model-router` agent skill wiring it into those hosts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/karencwlin1228-jev-router.md).
- [jev-router (peptidehackers)](https://github.com/peptidehackers/jev-router) - Numpy-only System One decision heads with Wilson-certified act-or-escalate routing (SalesRLAgent prior art)—independent of hosted Jev; distinct from gargpratyush/jev-router. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/peptidehackers-jev-router.md).
- [jev-router-benchmark](https://github.com/JTankariya/jev-router-benchmark) - Manufacturing routing benchmark with recorded Jev decisions for offline evaluation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-router-benchmark.md).
- [jev-router-mcp](https://github.com/humayunkabir/jev-router-mcp) - MCP server that routes a question to the best tool via a Jev-compatible `/v1/systemone` decision engine (works with Laya). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-router-mcp.md).
- [jev-rules](https://github.com/EliaAlberti/jev-rules) - Selects Claude Code project rules and map documents with Jev judgments over prompts and file paths, with session caching and fallback context. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-rules.md).
- [jev-safety-gateway](https://github.com/dark-hxx/jev-safety-gateway) - Nginx-front reverse proxy: TypeSafe Jev judges each user input before LLM backends (AGPL-3.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-safety-gateway.md).
- [jev-sap-commerce](https://github.com/Emenowicz/jev-sap-commerce) - SAP Commerce extension: TypeSafe Jev review moderation + category suggestions with dry runs and audit records. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/emenowicz-jev-sap-commerce.md).
- [jev-sdk (dev.isira, Java 25)](https://github.com/SL-Pirate/jev-sdk-java) - Small unofficial Java client for `https://api.typesafe.ai/v1/systemone`, published on Maven Central as `dev.isira:jev-sdk`: send a state with Choice, Score or Noul questions and get typed answers with probabilities and token usage. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-sdk-isira.md).
- [jev-sdk-java](https://github.com/luigivis/jev-sdk-java) - Unofficial Java 21 System One client with sealed Question/Answer types (TypeSafe-only; source-build until Maven Central lists 0.1.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-sdk-java.md).
- [jev-search (AnthonyDavidAdams)](https://github.com/AnthonyDavidAdams/jev-search) - Agentic search library/CLI: deterministic fetch/parse; only link/candidate decisions go to Jev (or local Laya). Distinct from superagents-lab Jev Search app. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/anthonydavidadams-jev-search.md).
- [jev-search (larguesa)](https://github.com/larguesa/jev-search) - Complementary semantic search CLI and agent skill: Jev scores whether each candidate line expresses the intent you are looking for, so agents find by meaning what keyword search misses across repo docs, LLM wikis and Obsidian notes; in six simulated tasks lexical + Jev recovered 29 of 34 labeled passages vs 22 for lexical alone (author-reported). Not the listed jev-search app. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/larguesa-jev-search.md).
- [jev-seatbelts](https://github.com/LocoLoboZ/jev-seatbelts) - Seven Claude Code hooks that catch expensive agent mistakes; TypeSafe Jev on judgment tiers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-seatbelts.md).
- [jev-sec-audit](https://github.com/DhanushNehru/jev-sec-audit) - Lightning-fast AI supply-chain security auditor: TypeSafe Jev scores typosquatting and malicious package scripts in milliseconds (CLI + GitHub Action). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dhanushnehru-jev-sec-audit.md).
- [jev-sec-bench](https://github.com/Gaurav-Gosain/jev-sec-bench) - Blind TypeSafe Jev security benchmarks (prompt injection + vulnerable code) on public corpora, with a results TUI (built on jev-go). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-sec-bench.md).
- [jev-secret-guard](https://github.com/BasmaAbouzied0/jev-secret-guard) - Claude Code hook: block known secret formats locally; send masked unknowns to TypeSafe Jev so checks never leak the secret. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-secret-guard.md).
- [jev-security-prioritization](https://github.com/san3ncrypt3d/jev-security-prioritization) - Experiment: TypeSafe Jev prioritizes SCA/SAST findings with context vs severity-only baselines. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-security-prioritization.md).
- [jev-security-sentinel](https://github.com/JevForge/jev-security-sentinel) - GitHub Action that gates SAST/SCA/IaC/secrets/container findings with TypeSafe Jev (`PASS`/`WARN`/`BLOCK`/`REVIEW`) without hiding findings. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-security-sentinel.md).
- [jev-seo](https://github.com/AkashPriyadarshii/jev-seo) - Agent-oriented SEO/GEO CLI and MCP with local DuckDuckGo helpers and optional TypeSafe Jev scoring. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-seo.md).
- [jev-seo (AgriciDaniel)](https://github.com/AgriciDaniel/jev-seo) - Live website SEO audit from one URL with TypeSafe Jev judgments and PDF/XLSX/Markdown reports (distinct from AkashPriyadarshii/jev-seo). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/agrici-jev-seo.md).
- [jev-seo-skills](https://github.com/jackson7705/jev-seo-skills) - Five tested SEO workflows powered by TypeSafe Jev: intent, internal links, cannibalization, brief QA, AI mention tracking. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jackson7705-jev-seo-skills.md).
- [jev-shadow-adapter](https://github.com/ianlintner/jev-shadow-adapter) - Shadow-mode observational adapter harness for TypeSafe Jev routing decisions, with an MkDocs GitHub Pages site. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ianlintner-jev-shadow-adapter.md).
- [jev-shell-history](https://github.com/mrnugget/jev-shell-history) - Ranks recent zsh commands with Jev for inline suggestions; sends selected history to TypeSafe and requires a user-supplied API key. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-shell-history.md).
- [jev-shield](https://github.com/caiovicentino/jev-shield) - Semantic MCP firewall and skill: TypeSafe Jev (Vercel AI Gateway) screens tool calls, results, and descriptions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-shield.md).
- [jev-sim-use](https://github.com/Ryu0118/jev-sim-use) - iOS Simulator/Android navigator: TypeSafe Jev picks the next on-screen action each step (sim-use). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-sim-use.md).
- [jev-skill (marcodicesare-dev)](https://github.com/marcodicesare-dev/jev-skill) - Community Jev skill for Claude Code/Codex: guide, Python CLI, five tested recipes, and measured findings from ~19k API calls (not TypeSafe's official skill). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/marcodicesare-dev-jev-skill.md).
- [jev-skill-gate](https://github.com/ShivamPansuriya/jev-skill-gate) - Gates Claude Code skill manifests with TypeSafe Jev relevance scores and `skillOverrides` so irrelevant skills stay out of context. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-skill-gate.md).
- [jev-skill-router-bench](https://github.com/OrMizL/jev-skill-router-bench) - Independent measurement of a Jev skill router on a Hermes roster (published scorecard; not a vendor replica). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-skill-router-bench.md).
- [jev-skill-scout](https://github.com/karanb192/jev-skill-scout) - Finds Claude Code turns where a skill should have loaded and did not (TypeSafe Jev audit CLI + live suggest mod). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-skill-scout.md).
- [jev-skill-suggestion](https://github.com/ray-amjad/jev-skill-suggestion) - Claude Code mod: TypeSafe Jev scores every skill against the prompt as you type (two-request cookbook method) and can observe, block or steer skill loading. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-skill-suggestion.md).
- [jev-skills](https://github.com/eran-broder/jev-skills) - Claude Code/Codex plugin: TypeSafe Jev selects which installed skills enter context each turn (zero always-on skill-list tokens). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-skills.md).
- [jev-state](https://github.com/suranjaychandra/jev-state) - TypeScript helpers that shape raw app and game state into state TypeSafe Jev reads well, with a reproducible benchmark. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-state.md).
- [jev-status](https://github.com/PeterFujiyu/jev-status-marketplace) - Claude Code plugin that asks TypeSafe Jev two independent questions after every turn — is the task done / needs action / failed, and is the work development- or production-ready — and shows both answers with probabilities above the prompt plus a toast. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-status.md).
- [jev-strict](https://github.com/Jubii100/jev-strict) - Unofficial, standard-library Python client for Jev that pins one model, meters usage in a crash-safe JSONL file, refuses calls past a hard monthly cap, and asks every choice and score in both option orders. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-strict.md).
- [jev-studio](https://github.com/utk2103/jev-studio) - Python CLI (`jev`) plus MCP server kit for TypeSafe Jev verify/screen/classify/extract flows and cookbook tools (Alpha 0.1.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-studio.md).
- [Jev-Style](https://github.com/lawrence3699/jev-style) - Local calibrated decision models with systemone-compatible server, agent skills, Claude Code guard, and MCP tools; HF weights—independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-style.md).
- [jev-subtitle-translator](https://github.com/GeekLinkDev/jev-subtitle-translator) - Translate SRT subtitles with structured LLM output and TypeSafe Jev QC checks (GPL-3.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-subtitle-translator.md).
- [jev-suite](https://github.com/klauswg/jev-suite) - Four Java decision-quality apps on one TypeSafe Jev kernel; deterministic code keeps thresholds and vetoes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-suite.md).
- [jev-supervisor](https://github.com/ryyyzer/jev-supervisor) - DeepSeek Harness macOS plugin that supervises agent execution with TypeSafe Jev decisions and budgets. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-supervisor.md).
- [jev-support-agents](https://github.com/RavioliCodes/jev-support-agents) - FastAPI multi-agent support reference: LLMs draft replies; TypeSafe Jev routes, evaluates, and retries/escalates (60-ticket bench). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-support-agents.md).
- [jev-support-desk](https://github.com/veermshah/jev-support-desk) - Developer-support queue tooling on TypeSafe Jev: confidence-gated triage and request doctor. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-support-desk.md).
- [jev-swap](https://github.com/0xjba/jev-swap) - Scan codebases for decision-shaped LLM calls, generate TypeSafe Jev modules, and shadow-test savings. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-swap.md).
- [jev-switch](https://github.com/ARCJ137442/jev-switch) - Local-first Jev protocol gateway (Rust/axum + React/Tauri): aggregate upstreams, editable routing DAG, playground—Vercel/Laya adapters today; TypeSafe/OpenRouter on the roadmap. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/arcj137442-jev-switch.md).
- [jev-switchboard](https://github.com/ZIJIAN004/jev-switchboard) - Semantic gate for parallel Claude Code/Codex agents: TypeSafe Jev chooses interrupt vs drop and which evidence to inject. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-switchboard.md).
- [jev-table](https://github.com/dtduc-git/jev-table) - Local-first CLI that adds TypeSafe Jev AI columns to CSV/JSONL with confidence, review queue, resume, and dry-run cost preview. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-table.md).
- [jev-test-filter](https://github.com/mizchi/jev-test-filter) - Scores each test against a git diff with TypeSafe Jev and emits filter args for vitest, Jest, node:test, Playwright, cargo, and go test. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-test-filter.md).
- [jev-test-impact](https://github.com/holasoymalva/jev-test-impact) - Test impact analysis CLI/Action: Git diff + static deps + TypeSafe Jev score candidate tests so CI runs only likely-affected Vitest/Jest files. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-test-impact.md).
- [jev-test-triage](https://github.com/cdubiel08/jev-test-triage) - Ranks surviving mutants by whether a killing test is worth writing, using batched TypeSafe Jev judgments (`jtt`); hooks for pre-commit and GitHub Actions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-test-triage.md).
- [jev-testbench](https://github.com/Charlie-Wang-03/jev-testbench) - Auditable Jev testbench: offline suite, call ceilings, append-only logs and evidence-labelled claims, with a stated negative headline result. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-testbench.md).
- [jev-testing](https://github.com/mdmintz/jev-testing) - SeleniumBase harness where Jev classifies CAPTCHA presence, confidence, and type from page HTML. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-testing.md).
- [jev-threshold-lab](https://github.com/CapCurious/jev-threshold-lab) - Fits act/review/escalate thresholds per Jev question on your labeled data, with precision/recall sweeps, expected-cost bands, calibration (ECE), and a debugging ladder. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-threshold-lab.md).
- [jev-ticket-triage](https://github.com/beese54/jev-ticket-triage) - Reproducible support-ticket triage eval: TypeSafe Jev vs Together.ai LLMs on accuracy/cost/latency/confidence (WIP). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/beese54-jev-ticket-triage.md).
- [jev-tokensaver](https://github.com/Dinesh-Sunny/jev-tokensaver) - Claude Code and Cowork pre-reader: a local `jev` CLI, MCP server and skill that let TypeSafe Jev read long test logs, big files and candidate-file lists (`prune`, `find`, `rank`, `map`) and pass Claude only the relevant lines — lossless, fail-open, secrets scrubbed locally. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-tokensaver.md).
- [jev-tool-search](https://github.com/kachar/jev-tool-search) - Benchmark and experimental engine comparing BM25, embeddings, rerankers, and TypeSafe Jev for selecting among 525 real MCP tools. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-tool-search.md).
- [jev-toolkit](https://github.com/jbt95/jev-toolkit) - MCP-first TypeSafe/Jev toolkit: `jev mcp` plus CLI triage/audit/label/route and optional Prometheus impact metrics. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-toolkit.md).
- [jev-tools (CMaintz)](https://github.com/CMaintz/jev-tools) - TypeScript TypeSafe Jev tools including an agent tool-call guardrail. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/cmaintz-jev-tools.md).
- [jev-tools (Rcidshacker)](https://github.com/Rcidshacker/jev-tools) - Claude Code plugin: OpenJev/Codiv for cheap typed agent judgments (edit hook, skill picker, review pre-check); distinct from CMaintz/jev-tools. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rcidshacker-jev-tools.md).
- [jev-torch](https://github.com/smha1012/jev-torch) - PyTorch stack to train JEV-style calibrated decision models (KL distill) and serve them via vLLM. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-torch.md).
- [jev-tree](https://github.com/reachjalil/jev-tree) - Recursive TypeSafe Jev Choice over a JSON taxonomy so catalogs can exceed the 255-option flat Choice cap. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-tree.md).
- [jev-triage](https://github.com/CMaintz/jev-triage) - GitHub Action that labels issues with TypeSafe or Cloudflare Jev typed questions and confidence-gated applies. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-triage.md).
- [jev-triage (OpenClaw)](https://github.com/CodeCave0000/jev-triage) - OpenClaw plugin that puts TypeSafe Jev in front of the agent's main model: each incoming message is classified thanks/spam/question/incident plus urgent yes/no, so thanks and spam end without waking the main model and other messages arrive with Jev's triage prepended; every decision is logged. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/codecave-jev-triage.md).
- [jev-triage (warehouse remarks benchmark)](https://github.com/mohammedahmadi1990/jev-triage) - Spring Boot benchmark comparing Java keyword rules, Jev and Claude Haiku 5.5 on triaging warehouse goods-receipt remarks (category, block from putaway, severity), with accuracy, latency and cost per 100k decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ahmadi-jev-triage.md).
- [jev-triage (ZephyrDeng)](https://github.com/ZephyrDeng/jev-triage) - Triage a GitHub or GitLab backlog with TypeSafe Jev: duplicates, related topics, issue types and dependency order in one HTML report (gh/glab). Distinct from other jev-triage listings. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/zephyrdeng-jev-triage.md).
- [jev-ui-test](https://github.com/buer2233/jev-ui-test) - Jev-driven UI test framework: natural-language cases + pytest/Allure; TypeSafe Jev scores candidate elements (fork of jev-ultrafast). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-ui-test.md).
- [jev-ultrafast-skill (SherseaHe)](https://github.com/SherseaHe/jev-ultrafast-skill) - Portable agent Skill wrapping browser-use/jev-ultrafast for Codex-style agents: bounded Chrome runs with result verification. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sherseahe-jev-ultrafast-skill.md).
- [jev-use (shitianfang)](https://github.com/shitianfang/jev-use) - Hands the Claude Code, Codex and pi steps that need no text output to Jev, returning anything it should not decide to the LLM under a typed escalation contract. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-use.md).
- [jev-verify (stillmarcus24)](https://github.com/stillmarcus24/jev-verify) - Checks published Jev outputs against the Yurin confidence identity (distinct from dsh-jev-verify). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/stillmarcus-jev-verify.md).
- [jev-vs-clef](https://github.com/rmax-ai/jev-vs-clef) - Head-to-head stress comparison of TypeSafe Jev (via the Vercel AI Gateway) and Cloudflare Workers AI Clef (27B) / Clef-flash (9B) on synthetic, production-shaped decisions: an identical 159-call battery run in Clef's launch week and again on 2026-10-06, covering edge cases (64-question max, bad-image 422, vision, array state) and a wire gap around `noul` in the swap story, with raw calls and per-call cost published. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-vs-clef.md).
- [jev-vs-pii (Jev for PII detection benchmark)](https://github.com/AlanViollier/jev-vs-pii) - Reproducible benchmark of Jev against rules, local PII models and LLMs for finding personal information; on court judgments Jev ties Claude Haiku 4.5 on F2, runs about six times faster and leaves the least personal information unmasked, while larger LLMs stay ahead on short synthetic text. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-vs-pii.md).
- [jev-web](https://github.com/warsang/jev-web) - Browser runtime for open-weight typed-decision models (open-jev/Laya/Strands/…); local after weight cache—independent of hosted TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-web.md).
- [jev-web-skills](https://github.com/jackson7705/jev-web-skills) - Five tested website-building workflows with TypeSafe Jev: page maps, content migration, silo and doorway QA, on-site routing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jackson7705-jev-web-skills.md).
- [jev-workflow](https://github.com/gbesse/jev-workflow) - Decision contracts, adversarial testing, tracing, stability, and privacy controls for TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/gbesse-jev-workflow.md).
- [jev-xray](https://github.com/viva-lee/jev-xray) - Sends every file in a repo to TypeSafe Jev with eight typed lenses in one request per file (role, blast radius, complexity, security-sensitive, tech debt, deserves tests, start here, legacy) and renders a live treemap; ask a new question to relight all files, drill down to the line Jev ranks highest, and read 'doubt' (1 − mean confidence) as a reading list. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-xray.md).
- [jev-xray (decision forensics)](https://github.com/aishwary-dongre/jev-xray) - Decision forensics for Jev: ablates parts of the state, re-asks the same question concurrently and reports which segments moved the probability, with a request/cost ledger. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/aishwary-jev-xray.md).
- [jev-zh-tw-eval](https://github.com/wuchengwei1996/jev-zh-tw-eval) - Traditional Chinese (zh-TW) evaluation of Jev-compatible System One models (Plumb-4B via Ollama), including adversarial probes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-zh-tw-eval.md).
- [jev-zork](https://github.com/Resadan-dev/jev-zork) - TypeSafe Jev plays Zork I: Choice over Jericho valid actions with anti-loop policy and a French replay dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-zork.md).
- [Jev.DotNet (altinburak)](https://github.com/altinburak/Jev.DotNet) - Unofficial .NET 8+ client for TypeSafe Jev published on NuGet as `Jev.DotNet`: typed Choice/Score/Noul questions and answers, enum-backed choices, retries with backoff, timeouts, logging and `IHttpClientFactory` / ASP.NET Core dependency-injection support. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-dotnet-altinburak.md).
- [Jev.Net](https://github.com/brightshore/jev-net) - Community .NET client for the TypeSafe System One API with a single dependency, built-in retries, and Native AOT compatibility. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-net.md).
- [jev.zig](https://github.com/jakeknowlton/jev.zig) - Unofficial Zig client for the TypeSafe System One API (Jev): compile-time typed noul/choice/score questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jakeknowlton-jev-zig.md).
- [jev2mcp](https://github.com/n8mirai/jev2mcp) - Local companion + Chrome extension: TypeSafe Jev picks ChatGPT MCP/plugin/tool mentions from a configured catalog. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev2mcp.md).
- [jev4j](https://github.com/maxsumrall/jev4j) - Java 17+ TypeSafe/OpenRouter Jev library (Maven Central) with enum choice/score helpers and Spring Boot starter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev4j.md).
- [jev4k](https://github.com/pambrose/jev4k) - Kotlin DSL/client for TypeSafe Jev Noul/Choice/Score (Maven Central `com.pambrose:jev4k`; distinct from jev-java / jev-android). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev4k.md).
- [jev4pg](https://github.com/Sheltercosmo/jev4pg) - Natural-language-to-SQL and semantic operators for PostgreSQL: TypeSafe Jev answers typed questions per record while PostgreSQL handles joins and aggregation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev4pg.md).
- [JEV_accel](https://github.com/poyen-tseng/jev-accel) - Traditional-Chinese Python package, CLI and MCP server that speeds up embedded bench work on a TI F280049C board with a RIGOL MSO5104 oscilloscope and Code Composer Studio: deterministic L0 rules answer first, TypeSafe Jev (`typesafe-sdk` Choice/Noul) is called only for ambiguous cases such as prioritising multiple violations, and the agent gets measurement state, a verdict and ready-to-send SCPI or next commands in one tool call. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-accel.md).
- [jev_naics](https://github.com/jmurray10/jev_naics) - Classifies a business's description of operations into one of the 1,012 NAICS 2022 industries for insurance applications, or holds it for a person; code builds the shortlist and Jev makes the single pick. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-naics.md).
- [Jev_validation_agent](https://github.com/omkarchougule19/Jev_validation_agent) - Python Jev Guard: TypeSafe Jev validates agent outputs with checks, reports, and a local demo UI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-validation-agent.md).
- [Jeva](https://github.com/LiQiiiii/Jeva) - Research System One model (Jeva-4B) designed for typed choice/noul/score decisions, with weights, fine-tuning code and a project page comparing it with Jev-1.13.0. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jeva.md).
- [jevai](https://github.com/tiendvlp/jevai) - Unofficial async Rust client for TypeSafe System One (noul/choice/score) against docs.typesafe.ai. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevai.md).
- [jeval](https://github.com/rlaope/jeval) - Measures probabilistic classifier calibration (incl. Jev confidence) and cost-optimal human hand-off thresholds; offline demo included. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jeval.md).
- [jevals](https://github.com/dayhaysoos/jevals) - Local TypeSafe Jev evaluation workbench: author Noul/Choice/Score cases with expected answers, run them, and compare saved results. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevals.md).
- [JEVals (duberblock)](https://github.com/duberblock/JEVals) - Open-source playground for System One / JEV-compatible evals: fidelity, semantic judging, and independent verification across web screens (distinct from dayhaysoos/jevals and Jevals.com). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/duberblock-jevals.md).
- [jevals (Openlayer)](https://github.com/openlayer-ai/jevals) - Evals and guardrails for agent traces (tool choice, grounding, relevancy, completeness, indirect injection, PHI) sent to Jev-style decision models as one request instead of an LLM judge. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/openlayer-jevals.md).
- [Jevals.com](https://jevals.com/) - Hosted independent boards: TypeSafe Jev vs LLMs on PubMedQA/Banking77/HelpSteer2 (calibration/cost/latency); data CC BY 4.0 (distinct from dayhaysoos/jevals). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevals-com.md).
- [JevAlt](https://github.com/mertkayacs/jevalt) - Open Jev-API–compatible Choice/Score/Noul models (EN/TR/DE) for CPU (~4 GB RAM); independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevalt.md).
- [Jevaluate](https://github.com/ElshinQ/jevaluate) - Confidence-gated web-app walkthroughs with TypeSafe Jev (optional DeepSeek vision) plus eval/judge scripts and an agent skill. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevaluate.md).
- [JevAny](https://github.com/SimpleJev/JevAny) - Open infrastructure for training, evaluating, and deploying System 1 decision models (independent of hosted TypeSafe Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevany.md).
- [JevApi](https://github.com/JawzoD3TH/JevApi) - .NET 10 TypeSafe Jev client (AOT) plus stdio MCP evaluate/batch tools. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevapi.md).
- [jevb](https://github.com/mitya777/jevb) - Browser and phone use for AI agents in plain English: the agent says what it wants (`jevb act mark "Buy oat milk" as done`, `jevb check …`) and TypeSafe Jev picks the element or judges the screen in about 150–300 ms, driving headless Chromium, your own Chrome, or real iOS and Android phones and returning short JSON instead of page snapshots. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevb.md).
- [jevbench (dhruvmehra)](https://github.com/dhruvmehra/jevbench) - Reproduce TypeSafe Jev vs LLM/BERT/Laya/NLI text-classification accuracy, calibration, latency, throughput, and cost on public datasets. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dhruvmehra-jevbench.md).
- [JevBench (Leanmcp)](https://github.com/Leanmcp/jevbench) - Open benchmark framework and dataset for typed decision models (Jev, Liquid D1, djev, Laya, Clef, pplx-decider and more) with paper and results site. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/leanmcp-jevbench.md).
- [JevBench (metamorphic)](https://github.com/JevBench/jevbench) - Metamorphic coherence benchmark for typed probabilistic decision models (50 probability/choice laws; no gold labels). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevbench.md).
- [JevBird](https://github.com/leftspace89/JevBird) - Pygame Flappy Bird where TypeSafe Jev picks one of several simulated flight paths through each pipe gap (roughly one API call per pipe) and the game draws the chosen and rejected paths. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevbird.md).
- [jevbrief](https://github.com/Parthkomalwad/jevbrief) - Playwright briefing for TypeSafe Jev: filter page elements with drop reasons, Choice next-click, local trace viewer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevbrief.md).
- [jevbus](https://github.com/zkjoie/jevbus) - Rust streaming event bus: TypeSafe Jev (or any Judge) decides route/subscribe/deliver dispositions with policy thresholds. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevbus.md).
- [jevc](https://github.com/doronp/jevc) - Compiles natural-language agent rules and JSON Schemas into TypeSafe Jev programs (typed questions + code reducers) you can test offline. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevc.md).
- [jevcache](https://github.com/kushals256/jevcache) - OpenAI-compatible local cache proxy: TypeSafe Jev (OpenRouter Decisions) admits same-intent paraphrases so expensive chat completions can be skipped (fail-open on Jev errors). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevcache.md).
- [jevcheck](https://github.com/mhingston/jevcheck) - Semantic linting: deterministic AST/text selection finds candidates, Jev answers one bounded yes/no question, then policy decides shadow vs owned enforcement—via `@mhingston5/jev-cli` provider selection. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevcheck.md).
- [jevci (sumant1122)](https://github.com/sumant1122/jevci) - Sub-second CI/diff/commit/doc quality gate powered by TypeSafe Jev System One (score+noul). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sumant1122-jevci.md).
- [jevclean (Pandas text cleaning)](https://github.com/hariPrasadCoder/jevclean) - Python library that maps messy Pandas text values onto your own vocabulary and classifies text into described categories, resolving exact matches, aliases and optional fuzzy matches locally and sending only distinct unresolved values to Jev, with caching and a review queue. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevclean-pandas.md).
- [JevClient (Swift)](https://github.com/enapiuz/jev-client-swift) - Unofficial Swift package for the TypeSafe System One API: send a state and named Choice, Score or Noul questions, get typed answers with probabilities and confidence, plus experimental images, Box answers and model listing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-client-swift.md).
- [JevClient.jl](https://github.com/AtelierArith/JevClient.jl) - Unofficial Julia client for TypeSafe System One (Noul/Choice/Score; endpoint policy to api.typesafe.ai). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevclient-jl.md).
- [jevcompat](https://github.com/mandu5/jevcompat) - Spec and conformance suite for Jev-compatible TypeSafe System One API servers (proxy, mock, GitHub Action). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevcompat.md).
- [jevcore (PerryLink)](https://github.com/PerryLink/jevcore) - TypeSafe Jev for DeepSeek Harness, MCP hosts and plain Node: typed noul/choice/score tools that are offline by default with disclosed egress. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/perrylink-jevcore.md).
- [JevCore Agent](https://github.com/carter1111/jevcore) - Local coding harness (`jevcoreagent`): TypeSafe Jev task/risk/mode judgments plus hard-policy Guard, MCP + CLI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/carter1111-jevcore.md).
- [jevcraft](https://github.com/mrubens/jevcraft) - Minecraft bot where code lists the actions it can really take and TypeSafe Jev picks one from that menu, with the project's own run statistics documented. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevcraft.md).
- [jevcrawl](https://github.com/RayenMlayeh/jevcrawl) - MCP server and CLI that crawls websites for AI agents, using Jev to pick which links to follow and which passages answer the agent's question. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevcrawl.md).
- [jevcut](https://github.com/VBS2004/jevcut) - Auto-clipper: code enumerates cut edges; TypeSafe Jev judges standalone clips for Shorts/Reels/TikTok (benchmarked on 38 videos). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevcut.md).
- [jevdedup](https://github.com/Ruivalim/jevdedup) - Bun CLI that finds duplicate files with size, quick-hash and SHA-256 checks, then asks Jev to confirm each group, flag mislabeled files, suggest which copy to keep and judge near-duplicate pairs; reports only, never deletes; self-described exercise project. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevdedup.md).
- [jevdev](https://github.com/ibrahimcesar/jevdev) - Rust coding-agent harness built around TypeSafe Jev System One decisions (HTTP or local transport). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevdev.md).
- [jevdevice](https://github.com/Xopher00/jevdevice) - MCP harness for Android (adb) or local shell: TypeSafe Jev (or local Laya) picks one runtime-discovered target per goal; code gates and executes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevdevice.md).
- [jevdict (typed decisions CLI)](https://github.com/Hashimlokasher/jevdict) - CLI that turns a described decision, such as ticket triage or whether a diff is risky, into typed Jev answers with probabilities that you can pluck in scripts or use as CI gates through exit codes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevdict.md).
- [JevDo](https://github.com/yinhong-zhou/jevdo) - Jev-first DeepSeek Harness agent loop that reuses validated Actions before calling a frontier model. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevdo.md).
- [JEVDrive](https://github.com/hasim2006/JEVDrive) - Highway driving simulation where Jev picks one of five manoeuvres (change left, keep course, change right, speed up, slow down) from a computed time-to-collision and headway assessment, with a Pygame cockpit HUD showing the action probabilities. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevdrive.md).
- [jevelin](https://github.com/henryzhangpku/jevelin) - Reference design for fast, low-cost real-time voice agents: small per-turn decisions (card number read-out, wants a human, risky draft) go to a System One classifier such as Jev off the critical path, scheduled as a dependency graph, with a browser demo running the same engine. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevelin.md).
- [jeveloper](https://github.com/pur4v/jeveloper) - Claude Code plugin: TypeSafe Jev reflex layer for route/gate/verify/completion and optional driver mode. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jeveloper.md).
- [JevEmbed](https://github.com/HITsz-TMG/JevEmbed) - Embedding-based Choice/Score/Noul framework with Jev-shaped API/CLI/HTTP server; independent of hosted TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevembed.md).
- [Jever](https://github.com/tituslhy/Jever) - Notebook companion to the article 'Your AI Agent Needs a Bouncer': a LangChain middleware where Jev labels each message as direct, decomposable, enrichable or needs clarification before a supervisor agent delegates to subagents. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jever.md).
- [JeVerifier](https://github.com/silvariasereneblossom/jeverifier) - Claude coding harness: TypeSafe Jev builds reading lists and contradiction/rule checks; Claude reviews. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jeverifier.md).
- [jevernetes](https://github.com/sunil-sadasivan/jevernetes) - Live Kubernetes log analysis (CLI/dashboard) with optional TypeSafe Jev judgments or offline keyword rules and agent handoff prompts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevernetes.md).
- [Jevers](https://github.com/clasen/Jevers) - Zero-dependency Node client for decision models: chain TypeSafe Jev with fallbacks (Liquid d1, Perplexity pplx-decider, Fastino GLiDE, Microsoft Decision-1, OpenAI Decisions, Cloudflare Clef, OpenRouter routes, local llama-server) and ask choice, noul or score questions over text, JSON, images or audio. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevers.md).
- [jevex-cli](https://github.com/trolloks/jevex-cli) - Jev-powered per-turn Codex model router with a terminal UI (jevex). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevex-cli.md).
- [jevey](https://github.com/CarloLepelaars/jevey) - Python helper client for TypeSafe Jev that makes typed Noul/Choice calls feel like building a small question set. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevey.md).
- [jevface](https://github.com/rbrisuda/jevface) - Typed judgments for Java: declare questions as an interface and let Jev answer them. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevface.md).
- [jevfetch (verbatim web reading for agents)](https://github.com/a2sys-ai/jevfetch) - Web-reading tool and agent skill that splits a page into blocks, asks Jev whether each block answers the question, and returns only matching passages verbatim with their heading paths, as a WebFetch alternative. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevfetch.md).
- [jevfilter](https://github.com/damiensmith1/jevfilter) - Python library/PyPI: filter or classify text with plain-English rules via TypeSafe Jev (choose/check/rate). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/damiensmith1-jevfilter.md).
- [JevFlow](https://github.com/Parth1811/JevFlow) - Claude Code plugin that keeps agents honest: plans as phases with checks; when Claude tries to stop, JevFlow asks Jev if the work is really done. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/parth1811-jevflow.md).
- [jevframe](https://github.com/ktaletsk/jevframe) - Semantic AI for pandas and Polars: TypeSafe Jev `.jev` accessor returns structured answers with full probabilities. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevframe.md).
- [jevgate](https://github.com/gordonbrander/jevgate) - Rust CLI that gates a shell pipeline on a yes/no Jev judgment: text passes through (or a command runs) only when P(yes) clears a threshold — a smarter cron/grep. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevgate.md).
- [jevgate (craxrev)](https://github.com/craxrev/jevgate) - Claude Code plugin: TypeSafe Jev facts gate Bash/Write allow/ask/deny (bypassPermissions-friendly). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/craxrev-jevgate.md).
- [JevGate (RichieLoco)](https://github.com/RichieLoco/JevGate) - Pure-MQL5 MetaTrader 5 module that vetoes EA trades using TypeSafe Jev calibrated judgments (distinct from Claude Code jevgate plugins). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/richieloco-jevgate.md).
- [JevGB](https://github.com/SuperfastSimon/jevgb) - Unofficial community extension of the listed grok-bot-jev: before Grok Bot does something expensive it calls `jevgb route` with a short JSON state and a router picks one action (e.g. hand off code work to Grok Build as a compact brief, or reuse a cached artifact), logging what each decision cost in a token ledger and dashboard; the default classifier is deterministic rules, with Jev used when `TYPESAFE_API_KEY` and `typesafe-sdk` are present. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevgb.md).
- [jevgo](https://github.com/devbackend/jevgo) - Unofficial Go client for TypeSafe System One: typed Noul/Choice/Score in one call (stdlib HTTP). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevgo.md).
- [jevgrep (dzhng)](https://github.com/dzhng/jevgrep) - Coding-agent CLI (`jg` / `@dzhng/jevgrep`): ask what code does and get relevant files plus excerpts via Jev (TypeSafe, Vercel AI Gateway, or OpenRouter)—distinct from kyu1204/jgrep npm jevgrep. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/dzhng-jevgrep.md).
- [JevGuard](https://github.com/Jhonnyr97/JevGuard) - Claude Code/Codex plugin: generate project rules from CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md and enforce them with TypeSafe Jev on PreToolUse/Stop (distinct from jev-guard risk scoring). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevguard.md).
- [jevguard (agent hook guard)](https://github.com/Dehumanizer77/jevguard) - Prompt-injection guard that runs from coding agents' own hooks in Claude Code, Grok Build and Codex CLI (adapters for Copilot CLI, Cursor and Hermes too): Jev scores web pages, search results, MCP responses and fetched files before the model reads them, and withholds results that look like injections. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevguard-agent-hooks.md).
- [JevGuard (blacksinisterx)](https://github.com/blacksinisterx/jev-guard) - Agent tool-execution security layer: hard rules then TypeSafe Jev allow/review/block (distinct from leepokai/jev-guard). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/blacksinisterx-jev-guard.md).
- [JevGuard (pablozr)](https://github.com/pablozr/JevGuard) - Semantic policy engine (distinct from Jhonnyr97/JevGuard) for coding agents: after each turn Jev makes one narrow judgment per repository rule on the attributed diff, and a local gate returns PASS, WARN or FAIL. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pablozr-jevguard.md).
- [jevi](https://github.com/bitomule/jevi) - Shell front end for Jev: pipe text in, ask a yes/no, multiple-choice or rubric question, and branch on the answer and probability; supports saved question sets, JSON output and images. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevi.md).
- [jevify MCP server](https://github.com/lucioamor/jevify-mcp-server) - Offline MCP server that audits code for decision-shaped AI calls and plans their migration to Jev in shadow mode. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevify-mcp-server.md).
- [Jevis](https://github.com/gldroger/jevis) - Hooks that give Codex, Claude Code and other coding agents a second pair of eyes: Jev reads each message, each command or edit the agent is about to make and each 'done' it is about to report, then injects lessons at the right moment, refuses likely mistakes and asks for a review before completion. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevis.md).
- [Jevitate](https://github.com/matt-cochran/jevitate) - Autonomous browser testing that explores your web app, reports only failures the browser can prove (HTTP 5xx, uncaught exceptions, hangs, failed checks) and turns each one into a replayable recording and deterministic regression test; Jev handles its judgment questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevitate.md).
- [jevkit](https://github.com/ariel-frischer/jevkit) - Rust CLI for TypeSafe Jev typed decisions plus offline `jev lint` before paid calls (distinct from Hermes's internal Python `jevkit` client). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevkit.md).
- [jevkit (bragamat)](https://github.com/bragamat/jevkit) - Unofficial `jev` CLI (Homebrew, Scoop, install script) that puts TypeSafe Jev in a coding agent's toolbox: `find`/`check` to read less, `decide`/`yesno`/`score` with ACT/CONFIRM/REPHRASE verdicts tuned to risk, and a local gateway that predicts the next tool for Claude Code or Codex; distinct from the other jevkit listing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/bragamat-jevkit.md).
- [jevlab](https://github.com/itsamejoshab/jevlab) - Offline search lab for Trick Jev: bandit-driven phrase search, calibration, and local leaderboards. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevlab.md).
- [JevLadder (rules, Jev judge and human gates for coding agents)](https://github.com/HaraldSigvartsen/jevladder) - Small decision authority for coding agents: deterministic rules run first, a cheap judge such as Jev covers only the grey zone, and uncertain or irreversible actions go to a human, with measurable allow, block or human exits for merge gates and model-tier routing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevladder.md).
- [jevlang (RoyWiggins)](https://github.com/RoyWiggins/jevlang) - Python codec preprocessor: if/while/match conditions decided by TypeSafe Jev (including English conditions); upstream LICENSE file missing. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/roywiggins-jevlang.md).
- [JevLang (TimMikeladze)](https://github.com/TimMikeladze/JevLang) - Policy engine for routes/gates/actions: Jev answers only policy questions; signed journal (distinct from sumanmichael/jevlang). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/timmikeladze-jevlang.md).
- [JevLangGraph (blacksinisterx)](https://github.com/blacksinisterx/jev-langgraph) - LangGraph agent where TypeSafe Jev picks continue/search/tool/retry/finish/escalate (distinct from other jev-langgraph owners). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/blacksinisterx-jev-langgraph.md).
- [jevlens](https://github.com/k4its1t/jevlens) - Evaluate, calibrate, replay, and monitor TypeSafe Jev decisions from labeled datasets with threshold suggestions and optional Streamlit/CI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevlens.md).
- [Jevlet](https://github.com/NAME0x0/Jevlet) - Research reconstruction of a System One decision model (Noul/Choice/Score) plus a Windows command-palette daily driver; HF weights—independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevlet.md).
- [Jevlike](https://github.com/vinnylarouge/jevlike) - Trains a small option-attention scorer with synthetic data and optional frozen encoders; independent of official Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevlike.md).
- [Jevlin](https://github.com/copyleftdev/jevlin) - Zig 0.16 SDK for TypeSafe Jev Choice/Score/Noul with bounded buffers, retries, and deadlines (independent of TypeSafe). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/copyleftdev-jevlin.md).
- [JevLint](https://github.com/huntedman/JevLint) - Semantic lint CLI: plain-English conventions scored as file-level TypeSafe Jev Noul judgments (`@jevlint/cli`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevlint.md).
- [Jevlint (codegirl-007)](https://github.com/codegirl-007/jevlint) - Go linter that checks code against plain-language team rules: Tree-sitter extracts code units and TypeSafe Jev judges whether each passes (distinct from huntedman/JevLint and the jev-lint projects). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/codegirl-007-jevlint.md).
- [jevlint (Ice-Hazymoon)](https://github.com/Ice-Hazymoon/jevlint) - Semantic lint CLI (`@hazymoon/jevlint`): plain-English yes/no rules judged by TypeSafe Jev with calibrated probabilities (distinct from huntedman/JevLint and mizchi/jev-lint). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ice-hazymoon-jevlint.md).
- [JevLint-LE](https://github.com/nolindnaidoo/jevlint-le) - VS Code/Open VSX extension, CLI and MCP server that lint the questions you send to Jev (and OpenAI Decisions) offline. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevlint-le.md).
- [jevmate](https://github.com/GastonGelhorn/jevmate) - Shell/CLI, Claude Code plugin, and MCP server for coding-agent triage, test selection, and risk review via TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevmate.md).
- [JevMC](https://github.com/PabloB07/JevMC) - Unofficial dependency-free Java SDK for the TypeSafe Jev API aimed at PaperMC Minecraft plugins, with typed Noul/Choice/Score answers, async calls and a chat-moderation example. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevmc.md).
- [jevmcp (eaisdevelopment)](https://github.com/eaisdevelopment/jevmcp) - Agent plugin + MCP: TypeSafe Jev screens spec-drift, CI triage, and code-audit checks for Claude Code/Codex. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/eaisdevelopment-jevmcp.md).
- [jevmem](https://github.com/Avinash-jetwani/jevmem) - Git-tracked JEVMEM.md project memory for Claude Code/Cursor/Codex; TypeSafe Jev decides what to save and recall, and checks tool calls against saved rules. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevmem.md).
- [jevmem (illescasDaniel)](https://github.com/illescasDaniel/jev-mem) - Agent long-term memory via TypeSafe Jev typed decisions (MCP, Claude Code hooks, CLI, SQLite)—no generative LLM in the memory loop (MIT; distinct from libingzheren/Jev-Mem). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/illescasdaniel-jev-mem.md).
- [jevmetrics](https://github.com/ishantanu/jevmetrics) - Experimental OpenTelemetry Collector metrics processor: TypeSafe Jev assesses instrument metadata for retention; deterministic keep/reduce policy with caching (alpha). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevmetrics.md).
- [jevmod](https://github.com/ohernandezdev/jevmod) - Moderation CLI/SDK/API/MCP and optional chat bots with per-category TypeSafe Jev probabilities and thresholds you own. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevmod.md).
- [jevmory](https://github.com/romiluz13/jevmory) - Local coding-agent memory with verbatim quotes graded by TypeSafe Jev confidence and receipt-backed MEMORY.md audits. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevmory.md).
- [jevnav](https://github.com/dtduc-git/jevnav) - Browser automation with TypeSafe Jev element choice, JSONL decision traces, risk gates, and offline CI replay (distinct from Ultrafast). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevnav.md).
- [Jevometry](https://github.com/Kunyanli230/Jevometry) - Information-geometric toolkit for System One (Jev/Jevlike) decision probabilities—Fisher sensitivity over declared models. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevometry.md).
- [jevonian](https://github.com/xinyao27/jevonian) - Local OpenAI/Anthropic/Responses proxy for coding agents where one Jev call answers both the model route and the thinking level for `jevonian/auto`, after code has filtered candidates by protocol, context window, effort floor, and spent quota windows; a pinned model or explicit `jevonian/<route>` skips Jev entirely, and costs in the ledger are estimates. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevonian.md).
- [JevOnly](https://github.com/buluoray/JevOnly) - Pure-Jev browser agent: code enumerates page/goal options, Jev only picks, with verify/undo and an irreversible-action gate. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevonly.md).
- [jevos](https://github.com/feder-cr/jev) - Self-hosted, Jev-compatible `/v1/systemone` server answering yes/no (Noul) questions from a CPU-only 1B GGUF model (MIT); independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevos.md).
- [JevOss](https://github.com/mertkayacs/jevoss) - Probe suite for any Jev-API decision model: accuracy, calibration, and adversarial recipes (Jev/Laya/etc.). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevoss.md).
- [jevotron](https://github.com/cmungall/jevotron) - CLI for field-level anomaly detection in tables, files, SQLite and DuckDB with Jev, exporting a review queue. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevotron.md).
- [JevPaper](https://github.com/SRjoeee/jev-paper) - Chrome extension that uses TypeSafe Jev to highlight arXiv claim evidence and caveats on the authors' own sentences. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-paper.md).
- [jevper](https://github.com/zhulinchng/jevper) - Jev-shaped System One noul/choice/score client over any OpenAI-compatible model (no hosted TypeSafe API). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevper.md).
- [JEVPFN](https://github.com/andreasgoethals/JEVPFN) - Research code testing whether label-free Jev features extracted from text improve tabular predictors such as TabPFN and CatBoost on MulTaBench text datasets. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevpfn.md).
- [JevPilot](https://github.com/standardagents/jevpilot) - Demonstrates Jev maneuver selection in a browser driving simulation with local collision checks; application licensing is unspecified. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevpilot.md).
- [jevpilot (bloudhood)](https://github.com/bloudhood/jevpilot) - MCP browser that finishes goals with TypeSafe Jev picking each step (distinct from standardagents/jevpilot driving demo). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/bloudhood-jevpilot.md).
- [JevPokerBench](https://github.com/Prophetlab/JevPokerBench) - Texas Hold'em benchmark/playground with official TypeSafe Jev, cash/SNG boards, and BYOK agents. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-poker-bench.md).
- [JevPolicy](https://github.com/sanoy24/jevpolicy) - TypeScript decision runtime that turns Jev probabilities (via Vercel AI Gateway) into versioned, replayable YAML-policy decisions with audit traces. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevpolicy.md).
- [jevq](https://github.com/who/jevq) - Jev filter sidecar for jq pipelines (`jq | jevq | jq`): keep JSONL values that pass a yes/no TypeSafe Jev question (or emit scores). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/who-jevq.md).
- [jevql](https://github.com/kylemclaren/jevql) - psql-style CLI, MCP server, and Go/TypeScript/Python SDKs that add TypeSafe Jev predicates, probabilities, choices, and scores to queries against vanilla PostgreSQL without an extension; distinct from pg-jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevql.md).
- [jevql (hemanth)](https://github.com/hemanth/jevql) - npm `jev-ql` calibrated semantic SQL over unstructured data with TypeSafe Jev (distinct from kylemclaren/jevql). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hemanth-jevql.md).
- [jevrake](https://github.com/RKasai127/jevrake) - Selector-free scraping CLI/library: code collects typed candidate values from the page and TypeSafe Jev picks the one matching each field description. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevrake.md).
- [jevraulio](https://github.com/braulito/jevraulio) - Spanish-language study stack wiring Jev decisions into chat/MCP (jeveador) APIs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevraulio.md).
- [jevreel](https://github.com/DeepAleph/jevreel) - Zero-embedding video search for agents: lays a video out on labelled contact sheets, has Jev judge every sheet in parallel, re-checks the best windows on full frames and returns timed hits over MCP. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevreel.md).
- [JevRepoTriage](https://github.com/murongg/JevRepoTriage) - Self-hosted GitHub issue/PR triage assistant powered by TypeSafe Jev (web UI + workers). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevrepo-triage.md).
- [Jevris](https://github.com/CryptVenture/Jevris) - Local decide/verify/route control plane for coding agents using TypeSafe System One Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevris.md).
- [jevroute](https://github.com/maxkulish/jevroute) - Claude Code skill-hint hook via TypeSafe Jev—STOPPED after baseline pilot (69/75 without hints); published negative findings. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevroute.md).
- [JevRoute (suncirkles)](https://github.com/suncirkles/jev-router) - Decision-only coding-task model routing (JevRoute) with a separate evaluation harness and recorded experiment evidence. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/suncirkles-jev-router.md).
- [JevRouter](https://github.com/BillionsBobby/JevRouter) - Capability router: TypeSafe Jev (or OpenRouter Decisions) picks among models/subagents/skills/MCP/CLIs while code enforces permissions, risk, confirmation, and receipts. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevrouter.md).
- [jevrules](https://github.com/chrisDeFouRire/jevRules) - Checks code against Cursor's `.cursor/rules` from Claude Code, Codex, a git hook or CI by asking Jev whether each applicable file follows each rule, with probabilities and next commands. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevrules.md).
- [jevry](https://github.com/jeffrydegrande/jevry) - Small Go command-line tool for TypeSafe Jev: pipe text with `--question` to print the probability of yes, or send a full request JSON to print the raw response (defaults to `jev-latest`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevry.md).
- [Jevs](https://github.com/nshcr/jevs) - Bun/TypeScript MCP server and Codex plugin for TypeSafe Jev classify/score/check/batch via the official JS SDK. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevs.md).
- [JevSceneMiner](https://github.com/bskkimm/JevSceneMiner) - Mines driving maneuvers from Autoware rosbags and nuPlan logs with TypeSafe Jev: motion, lane geometry and object interactions become a readable script, Jev classifies lateral and longitudinal labels with probabilities, and answers merge into timestamped scenes with a camera/BEV/timeline review viewer and ground-truth editor. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-scene-miner.md).
- [JevScope](https://github.com/jeiel85/jevscope) - Local-first visual workbench and JSONL regression testbench for TypeSafe Jev projects. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevscope.md).
- [jevsearch](https://github.com/kylemclaren/jevsearch) - shadcn/ui registry block for ⌘K site search that shows keyword hits immediately, then re-ranks the top candidates with one TypeSafe Jev request; no embeddings or vector database. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevsearch.md).
- [jevseek](https://github.com/blingdivinity/jevseek) - DeepSeek proposes next tokens; TypeSafe Jev via OpenRouter System One chooses which to append. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevseek.md).
- [JevServe](https://github.com/anonNo2/jevserve) - Docker server for open-weight Jev-style decision models on NVIDIA GPUs (typed Choice/Noul/Score; not TypeSafe's hosted Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevserve.md).
- [jevsh](https://github.com/takeshiue/jevsh) - Bash wrapper: TypeSafe Jev scores shell command risk (LOW–CRITICAL) before you confirm run. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevsh.md).
- [JevSheet: Spreadsheet Enricher](https://github.com/handsontable-sandbox/jev-sheet) - Handsontable demo that adds a Jev-classified column to a CSV or Excel sheet: describe the column in plain English (yes/no, score or choice), and every row gets an answer plus a confidence you can sort to review only the uncertain rows. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevsheet.md).
- [JevShelf](https://github.com/ham-zax/jevshelf) - Portable Skillshare-compatible skill shelf: one router skill, lexical or TypeSafe Jev selection, digest-checked lazy loading of specialist SKILL.md files. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevshelf.md).
- [JevShield](https://github.com/lgy1027/jevshield) - Python/LangChain tool-call security gate using TypeSafe Jev Choice/Noul/Score with local heuristic fallback (distinct from Node jev-shield). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevshield.md).
- [jevskillz](https://github.com/calelamb/jevskillz) - Claude Code skills + `jev` CLI for calibrated TypeSafe Jev checks over claims, tests, acceptance, and triage evidence. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevskillz.md).
- [jevSociety (social feed simulation judged by Jev)](https://github.com/isaacbitran/jev-society) - Agent-based simulation of 10,000 social-media accounts in which Jev is the only judge of which opinions fit together, asked live for every like and change of mind, to compare a feed ranked by predicted likes against a random feed. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-society.md).
- [JevSQL](https://github.com/EugeneBoondock/jevsql) - Adds TypeSafe Jev natural-language predicates and decision tables to SQLite SQL with batching, caching, and review queues; distinct from pg-jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevsql.md).
- [JevT++](https://github.com/wiatrM/jevtpp) - C++20 library for typed System One–style decisions (enums/booleans/scores) with optional local Laya (ONNX/ggml) or remote backends—independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevtpp.md).
- [JevTalk](https://github.com/Promptable-Technologies/jevtalk) - Experiment that makes Jev spell short plain-English answers one choice at a time. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevtalk.md).
- [JevTape](https://github.com/Hugo-DDT/JevTape) - Record/replay/inspect TypeSafe Jev decisions as JSON cassettes with Decision Contract fingerprints for offline CI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevtape.md).
- [jevtok](https://github.com/LabGuy94/jevtok) - Exact token counting and request-cost prediction for TypeSafe Jev (tiktoken-style encoder reconstructed from API usage). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jevtok.md).
