- [pi-zereight-router](https://github.com/zereight/pi-zereight-router) - Jev-backed virtual model for Pi: each user message is classified by Jev (`openrouter/typesafe/jev-1.13`) as planning, research, codebase explore, review, implementation or other and routed to a fixed Cursor-provider model and effort, with a mid-turn handoff to the implementation model after the first file edit and a local phase decision log. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pi-zereight-router.md).
- [picode-decisions-jev (Jev for COGNIX[WS])](https://github.com/cognixws/picode-decisions-jev) - COGNIX[WS] extension that lets TypeSafe Jev answer the agents' fast browser decisions, such as which page element matches a description and yes/no checks about a page, so agents act without reading the whole page each step. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/picode-decisions-jev.md).
- [Pinot Jev](https://github.com/gbesse/pinot-jev) - Apache Pinot scalar-function plugin for semantic predicates backed by TypeSafe Jev (`jevAll`, `jevAny`, `jevProbability`), fusing up to eight conditions per row into one request with per-server caching. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pinot-jev.md).
- [plain](https://github.com/gabe4coding/plain) - Write browser, desktop and mobile end-to-end tests in plain English YAML: Playwright, xa11y or Appium perform the actions while Jev reads the accessibility tree to select the element each step describes and to judge each `expect` claim; coding agents can explore a flow via MCP and save it as a spec. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/plain.md).
- [plain-language-gate](https://github.com/Wujiaowang/plain-language-gate) - Agent skill/CLI: TypeSafe Jev runs six plain-language readability checks and returns pass/review/rewrite (author model rewrites). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/plain-language-gate.md).
- [plan-anchor](https://github.com/randlee/plan-anchor) - Claude Code and Codex skill that captures your planning statements as anchors, turns them into typed TypeSafe Jev questions, and checks both the plan and the delivered work for drift. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/plan-anchor.md).
- [PlayJev](https://github.com/OmniJev/PlayJev) - Plays ten browser games from the frame alone with an open 0.8B model that scores the moves the game lists in one forward pass; independent of official Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/playjev.md)
- [PlayJev (filed)](https://github.com/filedcom/playjev) - "Stagehand, but with Jev": natural-language Playwright automation where TypeSafe Jev selects real browser nodes (`check`, `choose`, `rate`, `act`) and deterministic code performs actions; distinct from the listed OmniJev/PlayJev game player. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/filedcom-playjev.md).
- [playwright-jev](https://github.com/criguex/playwright-jev) - Semantic Playwright Test assertions (`toMean`, `toMeanAll`, `toChoose`, `toBeScored`) powered by TypeSafe Jev with fail-closed ambiguity handling and record/replay. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/criguex-playwright-jev.md).
- [playwright-jev (AdriaanVE)](https://github.com/AdriaanVE/playwright-jev) - Structured TypeSafe Jev decisions for Playwright failure triage, smart retries, healer gating, snapshot pruning, locator healing, and test impact ordering. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/adriaanve-playwright-jev.md).
- [pm-jev](https://github.com/unbraind/pm-jev) - pm-cli extension/library for typed System One decisions on project items via the TypeSafe JS SDK, local Ollama by default. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pm-jev.md).
- [Pointsman](https://github.com/Kai-Leee/pointsman) - Jev-powered model and task routing for Claude Code and Codex: structured decisions become dependency DAGs that dispatch ready tasks to coding workers (formerly Jev Router). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pointsman.md).
- [PokéSurvivor x Jev learning bot](https://github.com/skyhilam/pokesurvivor) - Experimental bot for the browser survival game PokéSurvivor: a Chrome extension handles 60 FPS dodging locally, and a local bridge asks Jev for level-up cards, items and tactics, learning only from what it sees on screen (README in Cantonese). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pokesurvivor-jev.md).
- [PolicyJEV](https://github.com/AbbasRana001/PolicyJEV) - Frozen-protocol comparison of zero-shot Jev with TF-IDF logistic regression and fine-tuned DistilBERT on Jigsaw toxic comments: Jev ranks worse (mean AP 0.527 against 0.583 and 0.677), and as a three-way allow/review/action policy it sends the most comments to human review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/policyjev.md).
- [Poltergeist](https://github.com/ghostsecurity/poltergeist) - Ghost Security's fast Go secret scanner with an opt-in advisory classification that asks Jev how likely each detected value is a real secret, without changing findings or exit codes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/poltergeist.md).
- [polyjev](https://github.com/PraveenAShukla/polyjev) - Typed calibrated decisions (Noul/Choice/Score/Span) from any LLM with a drop-in Jev-compatible `/v1/systemone` server; independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/polyjev.md).
- [postgres-search](https://github.com/alexforman1/postgres-search) - Algolia-style product search in plain PostgreSQL (whole and partial words, typos, barcodes, typeahead, facets) with an optional TypeSafe Jev second stage asking two questions per search — keep or sink each result, and which spelling the user meant; the README doubles as a dated evaluation report on 440k USDA products with failures, costs and threats to validity. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/postgres-search.md).
- [prime-typesafe-jev](https://github.com/japorto100/prime-typesafe-jev) - Host bridge for Prime Agent that adds Jev decisions behind a Unix-socket credential boundary, deterministic hard blocks, egress redaction and daily budget caps. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/prime-typesafe-jev.md).
- [Privatemode Decisions benchmark](https://github.com/edgelesssys/privatemode-decisions-benchmark) - Edgeless Systems' benchmark comparing its Privatemode Decisions library (GLM-5.3-Flash in an attested enclave) with TypeSafe Jev and local Laya on speed, accuracy, calibration and cost across 29 labelled public datasets, plus JevBench comparisons. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/privatemode-decisions-benchmark.md).
- [ProbabilityBench](https://github.com/ishida-lab/ProbabilityBench) - Benchmark of text worlds with exact ground-truth class probabilities (peer review, covert transfer, credit risk), with an example that scores decision models such as TypeSafe Jev against the true probabilities. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/probabilitybench.md).
- [Prompt Rejector](https://github.com/revsmoke/promptrejectormcp) - MCP/HTTPS screening for prompts, skills, and tool descriptions with TypeSafe Jev plus deterministic checks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/prompt-rejector.md).
- [prompt2jev](https://github.com/sumleo/prompt2jev) - Agent skill and CLI that convert natural language, an LLM prompt, or prompt-running code into a TypeSafe Jev decision (typed questions + runnable script). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/prompt2jev.md).
- [prose-check](https://github.com/vgeshel/prose-check) - Claude Code / Codex / Muse Code plugin that checks each final reply against project writing rules with Jev and requests one rewrite on violations. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/prose-check.md).
- [protoc-gen-jev](https://github.com/bufbuild/protoc-gen-jev) - Experimental Buf (bufbuild) protoc plugin that turns Protobuf schemas annotated with questions, rubrics and thresholds into strongly typed Jev clients for Go, TypeScript and Python (or a language-agnostic `.jev.json` spec), mapping fields to noul, choice and score decisions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/protoc-gen-jev.md).
- [Pulumi self-driving software factory](https://github.com/pulumi/devx-pulumi-software-factory) - Pulumi example from the Pulumi org that deploys a self-driving software factory on AWS: a Qwen3-8B model on your own GPUs, Mastra Factory for coding agents, a Mastra agent, and an issue triage service where Jev accepts, rejects or asks for detail on each new item. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pulumi-software-factory.md).
- [purge-email](https://github.com/bhdoggett/purge-email) - Gmail clean-up CLI: Gmail search does the cheap filtering (age, no attachments, not starred), then TypeSafe Jev judges whether each remaining message is likely personal correspondence, a receipt/financial record or an account/legal/medical/government record; `plan` only labels (`purge` below 0.1 keep-probability, `review` in between) and nothing moves to Trash until you run `apply --yes`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/purge-email.md).
- [px-triage](https://github.com/cephalization/px-triage) - Keyboard-driven CLI for triaging a GitHub repository's `triage`-labelled issues and PRs: TypeSafe Jev reads each item and proposes the next step (ask for info, bug + owner, schedule, review, close), you confirm with one key or override; GitHub changes run in the background, decisions are logged and traced to Arize Phoenix. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/px-triage.md).
- [pydecide](https://github.com/gopaljigaur/decide) - One Python client for typed decisions (Choice, Score, Noul) over Jev, OpenRouter, OpenAI Decisions, Laya, MLX and cross-encoders, with a confidence-gated fallback chain. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pydecide.md).
- [Pyro](https://github.com/delvisorlabs/pyro) - Open-source, self-hostable policy engine that checks text before your AI application uses it: write word, pattern and semantic checks, test each branch and trace why each allow, review or block decision was made; semantic checks use TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pyro.md).
- [pytest-jev](https://github.com/allebee/pytest-jev) - Pytest plugin for semantic assertions: TypeSafe Jev judges whether text holds or lacks plain-language claims. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/pytest-jev.md).
- [QAJev](https://github.com/HanamoriLabs/qajev) - Test a website, game or app the way a person uses it: write a plain-language goal and Jev (TypeSafe or OpenRouter) clicks through a real Chrome, iOS Simulator, Android emulator or Godot/Electron game, while your expectations — not Jev's "done" — decide pass/fail; read-only by default with a hard cost cap. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/qajev.md).
- [quackd](https://github.com/rokbenko/quackd) - Multi-robot CLI with LLM pilots and an optional TypeSafe Jev discrete stepper for closed-set skill choices. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/quackd.md).
- [Qualixar Jev Decision Layer](https://github.com/qualixar/jev-decision-layer) - Typed MCP decision layer for Claude Code/Codex/Hermes/VS Code agents: 20 tools, 36 recipes, offline fixtures; TypeSafe Jev primary with optional local Laya. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/qualixar-jev-decision-layer.md).
- [Quarkus LangChain4j decision routing (Jev/Kev/Laya)](https://github.com/kdubois/quarkus-langchain4j-jev-laya) - Proof of concept for Quarkus 4 + Quarkus LangChain4j 2: LangChain4j's `DecisionRouterPlanner` uses a small decision model to route car-rental questions to specialist chat agents and merge their answers, with the same typed decisions runnable against hosted TypeSafe Jev, local Kev, an included Laya sidecar or a deterministic stub — plus Jev-backed reply guardrails. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/quarkus-langchain4j-jev-laya.md).
- [quick-qa-helper](https://github.com/ouor/quick-qa-helper) - Android UI QA loop for coding agents: a planner model picks the next action, TypeSafe Jev picks the target element (Box on a screenshot when the UI tree lacks it) and independently verifies done, adb acts, and you get a pass/fail report with one summary image. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/quick-qa-helper.md).
- [QuickE2E](https://github.com/dmoka/quicke2e) - Plain-English end-to-end testing library for web apps: you write a goal and inputs, a small decision model picks each browser step — hosted Jev (`typesafe/jev-1.13` via OpenRouter, the default) or a local Shisa DE-1 engine — Playwright acts, and an explicit check (e.g. one SQL query) verifies the result; specs can be turned into generated Playwright code. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/quicke2e.md).
- [Quicksilver](https://github.com/UditAkhourii/quicksilver) - Claude Code skill that hands bulk judgment/shortlist work to TypeSafe Jev so Claude spends tokens on harder reasoning. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/quicksilver.md).
- [rea-jev](https://github.com/juan-viox/rea-jev) - Claude Code plugin that pairs REA (Reverse Engineer Anything) with TypeSafe Jev as a System 1 reflex layer — routing the target and workflow, gating risky REA calls, scoring evidence after tools and checking whether "done" was earned — plus a `jev` rank/classify/verify CLI; every hook fails open. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rea-jev.md).
- [ReadyCode Reader](https://github.com/ReadycodeAI/readycode-reader) - Local PDF/DOCX/XLSX evidence for agents: TypeSafe Jev checks passages before cited answers (MCP + browser). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/readycode-reader.md).
- [reasoning-router](https://github.com/robertn702/reasoning-router) - Per-step reasoning effort for OpenCode, Pi and Codex CLI picked by a classifier such as TypeSafe Jev, keeping the prompt cache intact. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/reasoning-router.md).
- [Reddit product sentiment](https://github.com/STVPJ/reddit-sentiment) - Daily pipeline that collects product subreddit posts and comments, asks Jev typed questions about each comment and product pair (is it about this product, a five-level sentiment score, five aspects and nine themes), stores answers in SQLite, and shows trends with a drill-down to the source text. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/reddit-product-sentiment.md).
- [Redline](https://github.com/vvedantb/redline) - Visual PR review for Vite and Next.js apps: builds two commits, screenshots every page, and reports changed, new, broken, and removed pages; with a TypeSafe key, Jev picks the changed file most likely behind each visual change. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/redline.md).
- [reflex (Alertmanager router)](https://github.com/Starttoaster/reflex) - Alertmanager webhook that routes alert groups to urgent or info Discord channels with one TypeSafe Jev Choice (lab project). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/starttoaster-reflex.md).
- [Reflex (Datadog Labs)](https://github.com/datadog-labs/reflex) - Datadog Labs' Rust library for control loops on observability data: Datadog metrics and Toto forecasts become typed state, a judge such as TypeSafe Jev recommends an action (open, probe or leave a circuit), and Reflex's state machine applies it only if guards and invariants pass; includes a circuit-breaker simulator and a `reflex-typesafe` judge crate. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/datadog-reflex.md).
- [Reflex (kaustav1996)](https://github.com/kaustav1996/reflex) - Pi coding agent with TypeSafe Jev gates on tool calls, model-tier/skill routing, completion checks, and outside-result screening; code owns thresholds. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/kaustav1996-reflex.md).
- [Reflex (ursuciprian)](https://github.com/ursuciprian/reflex) - Pre-execution risk gate and prompt-injection guard for coding agents; optional TypeSafe Jev (≠ kaustav1996/reflex). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ursuciprian-reflex.md).
- [Reflex System One](https://github.com/JRodrigoTech/reflex-system-one) - Windows-first, one-click local server for open decision models (Laya, Decider, Sol, Nox) behind a System One–style `/v1/systemone` API, with a portable runtime, model manager and console demo; independent of hosted TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/reflex-system-one.md).
- [Reflex-1](https://github.com/matu79go/reflex-1) - Reflex-1: 4B open decision model aiming for frontier-LLM accuracy at Jev speed (instant classification + latent reasoning); independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/matu79go-reflex-1.md).
- [reflex-router](https://github.com/ziyacivan/reflex-router) - Alpha loopback proxy for Claude Code: at the start of each piece of work a decision model (TypeSafe Jev by default, local Laya or TypeLLM) judges how much reasoning the task needs; in opt-in route mode cheap work goes to a cheaper model when it will not break the prompt cache, and every decision is recorded with signals of whether it looked wrong afterwards (`reflex report`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/reflex-router.md).
- [refsure (browser judgment for coding agents)](https://github.com/leovvay/leovvayRefsure) - CLI and agent skill that coding agents in Orca terminals or next to an agent-browser session call to answer browser questions they often get wrong (which control means X, how to pass a banner, whether a click landed), using Jev decisions over a hidden page in the agent's own tab. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/refsure.md).
- [ReJev](https://github.com/Joe-rq/ReJev) - Independent Jev-style decision-model post-training reproduction on MiniCPM5-2B (stage 1 Choice classification) with sealed holdout eval. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rejev.md).
- [REPLAY](https://github.com/balue8246-maker/replay) - macOS computer- and browser-use toolkit (Chinese docs, English summary) splitting work fast and slow: a slow model such as Opus plans, handles exceptions and approves risky actions, TypeSafe Jev (~0.5–1 s per judgment) decides each screen — what to look at, click or fill, and whether it's done — and code acts through Ego Lite for web, cua-driver and Peekaboo for apps and native dialogs, with Apple Vision OCR as fallback; recorded demonstrations become reusable task chains. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/replay-jev.md).
- [Repo Graph](https://github.com/fakoli/repo-graph) - Local repository architecture diagrams and semantic search packaged for Pi, Codex and Claude Code, with an optional, off-by-default TypeSafe Jev reranker that judges a shortlist in one bounded batched request. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/repo-graph.md).
- [RepoFinder](https://github.com/SelmanBoray/RepoFinder) - Codebase map and cited code search where TypeSafe Jev optionally reranks BM25 hits. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/repofinder.md).
- [Responsible AI Harness](https://github.com/syabdulr/responsible-ai-harness) - Model-agnostic safety assessment harness: hard rules plus optional TypeSafe Jev judge for injection, leakage, unsafe tools, and policy bypass; checksummed evidence + report UI. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/responsible-ai-harness.md).
- [Retrace](https://github.com/akbokha/retrace) - GitHub Action that keeps a ledger of every review thread on a pull request and, on each push, marks threads as likely fixed, partially fixed, still open or made worse using one Jev call per changed thread. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/retrace.md).
- [Reverie](https://github.com/r3c0nc1l3r/reverie) - Spec-driven browser test agent: Markdown specs are run step by step by a multimodal pilot while hosted TypeSafe Jev (default, via OpenRouter) or local Laya makes each click and keystroke, with a text model escalating when the decision engine is unsure, commit-button guards and run evidence under `.reverie/runs/`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/reverie.md).
- [riff](https://github.com/scale-venture-partners/riff) - Prose linter with ruff-style rule codes: local static rules plus optional TypeSafe Jev judgment rules (`riff-lint` 0.1.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/riff.md).
- [RimeJev](https://github.com/IIIIOvOIIII/RimeJev) - Context-aware Chinese candidate recommendations for Squirrel (鼠须管) + Rime + Wanxiang on Apple Silicon macOS: asynchronously reorders native candidates using cloud TypeSafe Jev, a local model, or both, with per-app overrides and enhancement off by default. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rimejev.md).
- [rippy](https://github.com/mpecan/rippy) - Rust shell-command safety hook for AI coding tools; a separate opt-in `rippy-jev` build can ask Jev to approve only commands rippy is unsure about, never ones that require human approval. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rippy.md).
- [RLCD Gateway](https://github.com/JimmyWesley/rlcd-gateway) - Self-hosted Go gateway for LLMs and Jev/open-rlcd System One with pruning, audit, and a live dashboard. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rlcd-gateway.md).
- [robo-jev](https://github.com/STEERIX-home/robo-jev) - Robotics / embodied decision bench configs that include TypeSafe Jev (`~typesafe/jev-latest` and related ids) among decision-model baselines for evaluating fast typed policies. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/robo-jev.md).
- [rreia-skill](https://github.com/GabOnezio/rreia-skill) - RREIA agent skill bundling a local rreia-jev Rust decision asset and Jev-local references. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rreia-skill.md).
- [RSI-Jev](https://github.com/Shanghua-Gao/RSI-Jev) - Recursively self-improving research loop that trains Jev-style System One (noul/choice/score) models and serves them at `/v1/systemone`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rsi-jev.md).
- [ruby_decision_model](https://github.com/obie/ruby_decision_model) - Stdlib-lean Ruby client for Noul/Choice/Score decision models via Typesafe or OpenRouter. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ruby-decision-model.md).
- [ruby_llm-typesafe](https://github.com/kieranklaassen/ruby_llm-typesafe) - RubyLLM 2 provider for TypeSafe System One / Jev structured judgments (Choice/Noul/Score). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ruby-llm-typesafe.md).
- [ruling](https://github.com/bradAGI/ruling) - Turns an open local chat model into a Jev-style decision engine served on `/v1/systemone`, with training, eval, and calibration commands; independent of TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ruling.md).
- [RYOTIDE](https://github.com/csabag/ryotide) - Local Jev-style typed decisions from one LLM forward pass (MLX/PyTorch), measured on JevBench. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ryotide.md).
- [s1 (s1-rs)](https://github.com/AbdelStark/s1-rs) - Rust derive layer for TypeSafe System One Choice/Score/Noul over optional `typesafe-rs` (distinct from typesafe-api). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/s1-rs.md).
- [S1-Core](https://github.com/acbcore-labs/s1-core) - Zero-dependency Python harness for System 1 decision models like Jev: choice/score/noul specs, fail-closed calibration gates, wave-parallel fan-out and a replayable audit ledger. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/s1-core.md).
- [s1-tui](https://github.com/tnaftali/s1-tui) - Terminal UI for System One typed decisions (noul/choice/score) over laya (local MLX) and TypeSafe Jev backends. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/s1-tui.md).
- [s1doom (System1Doom)](https://github.com/PanikParty/System1Doom) - Harness where text-only System One models (TypeSafe Jev or open-weight Laya) play DOOM through ViZDoom: code computes geometry, the model makes the judgment. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/system1doom.md).
- [SafeDecide](https://github.com/yotambraun/safedecide) - Certified automate/escalate thresholds for System One decision models (Jev, Clef, d3, Laya, …): pick the lowest confidence line that keeps automated error rate under a bound on labeled data, plus a 140M on-device model and adapters for `/v1/systemone`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/safedecide.md).
- [sales-pipeline-revival-jev](https://github.com/developer943/sales-pipeline-revival-jev) - Open benchmark: TypeSafe Jev vs Gemini triage on 203 dead sales-deal email threads with published metrics (Salesgear companion). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sales-pipeline-revival-jev.md).
- [scala-jev-sdk](https://github.com/ticofab/scala-jev-sdk) - Unofficial Scala 3.3 LTS System One client (sttp 4, no bundled effect system; Maven Central `scala-jev-sdk_3` 0.1.0, Apache-2.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/scala-jev-sdk.md).
- [Scientific Decision Evaluation](https://github.com/bydeng01/scientific-decision-eval) - Code and data for evaluating Jev and other models on scientific Choice decisions where deterministic code computes downstream counts, relations and claim labels, reporting semantic, downstream and final-label correctness separately. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/scientific-decision-eval.md).
- [Scope (code context selector)](https://github.com/tjeastmond/scope) - CLI that selects the smallest useful code context for a software task: static analysis shortlists up to 30 candidate chunks, TypeSafe Jev judges the relevance of each through `@typesafe-ai/sdk`, and TypeScript adds supporting declarations to return traceable chunks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tjeastmond-scope.md).
- [Screen Intelligence (desk)](https://github.com/zacfink/screen-intelligence) - macOS command-line "hands and eyes" for Claude Code: Claude writes a plan and Jev does the looking and clicking, using the accessibility tree first and a two-pass Box search on pixels second; 47 of 48 buttons found in about 0.6 s each, and it stops before send, submit, delete or pay. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/screen-intelligence-desk.md).
- [Scruple](https://github.com/NAlexPear/scruple) - Semantic-aware linter for enforcing team taste: an OXC parser selects candidate code, rules decide what to ask, and a decision provider — TypeSafe Jev by default (pinned to `jev-1.13.0`), or Cloudflare Clef, local Decider, self-hosted Kev or OpenAI Decisions — classifies each one into diagnostics that fail CI on errors. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/scruple.md).
- [Search Gateway](https://github.com/alvarovillalbaa/search-gateway) - Self-hostable gateway and SDK for 13 AI search providers (Exa, Perplexity, Tavily, Brave and more) with Jev-first auto routing that picks the provider and search breadth, falling back to OpenAI Decisions and then rules. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/search-gateway.md).
- [searxng-jev-mcp](https://github.com/arashbehmand/searxng-jev-mcp) - MCP server that gives coding agents one search tool over your own SearXNG: it cleans the query, picks vertical engines, merges duplicates, ranks with BM25F and can rerank results with a Jev-compatible decision model. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/searxng-jev-mcp.md).
- [second-brain (knowledge refresh skill)](https://github.com/strickland-inc/second-brain) - Claude Code agent skill that finds stale pages in a wiki, memory or rules folder, asks Jev whether each was superseded or duplicates another, moves outdated pages to `_archive/` and rebuilds the index Claude reads first. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/second-brain-refresh.md).
- [Sedum](https://github.com/sedum-dev/sedum) - Pre-alpha browser-testing framework: write Playwright tests in TypeScript with plain-English `ai(...)` steps, where TypeSafe Jev (default) resolves which element a sentence means and judges whether a claim holds on the page. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sedum.md).
- [SelfJev](https://github.com/Jwuthri/SelfJev) - Open decisions model with a Jev-shaped typed API (yes/no, choice, score, multi) from forward-pass probabilities — Qwen3.5-4B + LoRA, one GPU; independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jwuthri-selfjev.md).
- [semantic-assert](https://github.com/mondaychen/semantic-assert) - Assert plain-English claims about captured UI/text state with TypeSafe Jev (Playwright helpers; thresholds in code). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/semantic-assert.md).
- [semantic-test-matcher](https://github.com/JustasMonkev/semantic-test-matcher) - TypeScript CLI (`rbt`) that ranks which test files to re-run for a changed source file: it profiles paths, code structure and diffs, then TypeSafe Jev judges each candidate test in batched requests, with a local answer cache, score blending, an OpenAI Decisions ranker option and a heuristics fallback. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/semantic-test-matcher.md).
- [Semble + Jev (semble-jev)](https://github.com/fatelei/semble-jev) - Local Semble retrieval + TypeSafe Jev relevance filter returning original source locations for agents (`sj`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/semble-jev.md).
- [SemDecide](https://github.com/sharziki/semdecide) - Typed semantic decisions for Unix pipelines and CI with TypeSafe Jev predicates, routes, scores, and filters. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/semdecide.md).
- [semgate](https://github.com/m-mizutani/semgate) - Go net/http middlewares that ask TypeSafe Jev typed questions about each request, then allow, block, or route. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/semgate.md).
- [SemIf](https://github.com/TheoLeeCJ/SemIf) - Explores typed option scoring and shared-state reuse with local open models; independent of official Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/semif.md).
- [Sensibility](https://github.com/rajnandan1/sensibility) - Claude Code plugin that lets Claude ask Jev quick typed questions (does this diff match the ticket, is this command safe, did I finish) and turn the answers into an act, confirm or escalate verdict, with reusable question sets and optional risk and finish gates. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sensibility.md).
- [sensored](https://github.com/atomicpages/sensored) - Streaming-first TypeScript PII redaction library (155 regex detectors, optional NER, Node/Bun/Deno/browser/edge) with opt-in Jev semantic confirmation (`semantic: { provider: "jev" }`) that asks TypeSafe Jev to confirm detected candidates before redacting. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sensored.md).
- [Session Board](https://github.com/kolotovalexander/session-board) - Local macOS panel for Codex that turns a conversation into cards of open questions, tasks and answers, with opt-in TypeSafe Jev analysis (TypeSafe API or OpenRouter's Jev endpoint) to match messages to cards; listens only on localhost and documents what is stored and sent. Docs are in Russian. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/session-board.md).
- [sgrep](https://github.com/Lagnajit09/sgrep) - Semantic grep for codebases: chunk files and ask TypeSafe Jev which chunks match a plain-English query (includes offline mock). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sgrep.md).
- [Shopify Jev Flow](https://github.com/gbesse/shopify-jev-flow) - Shopify Flow action that returns a typed Jev decision (finite route, calibrated confidence, explicit `reviewRequired`) instead of prose; the first example routes return requests to auto-approve, manual review or reject. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/shopify-jev-flow.md).
- [sieve](https://github.com/derwells/sieve) - Local MCP server that enumerates candidates and scores them with TypeSafe Jev (`jev_grep` / `jev_rank` / `jev_search`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sieve.md).
- [sift-light](https://github.com/lightsifter/sift-light) - Evidence-first search tool for coding agents (Pi, OMP, Claude Code/Codex via MCP) with exact, concept and hybrid modes and an optional Jev semantic judge that classifies retained concept candidates to improve ordering. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sift-light.md).
- [similarity-ts-jev](https://github.com/kongyo2/similarity-ts-jev) - Runs similarity-ts and fallow on TypeScript, then keeps only the pairs TypeSafe Jev judges worth merging, each with the change to make (copy, derive, or extract), plus tools to calibrate the cutoff. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/similarity-ts-jev.md).
- [SiteEye](https://github.com/seominpapa/siteeye) - Construction-site near-miss prediction prototype: YOLO vision plus TypeSafe Jev 1.14 noul questions over detected statements, served as a local web app. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/siteeye.md).
- [Skill Authoring Lab](https://github.com/sebastienblanc/skill-authoring-lab) - Short loop for authoring a Quarkus MCP skill: edit, run one JUnit test, read a Jev-backed score. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skill-authoring-lab.md).
- [Skill Dash](https://github.com/48Nauts-Operator/skill-dash) - Local dashboard that judges Claude Code/Codex skills with TypeSafe Jev (usefulness, redundancy, clarity, action) plus transcript evidence. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skill-dash.md).
- [Skill Router (LangChain)](https://github.com/deyna256/langchain-skill-router) - Drop-in replacement for the deepagents `SkillsMiddleware` that picks which `SKILL.md` skills to load each turn with a pluggable judge; the included `JevJudge` adapter uses TypeSafe Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/langchain-skill-router.md).
- [skill-scanner](https://github.com/FrancoisChastel/skill-scanner) - Offline static analysis for Agent Skills (prompt-injection, exfil, hidden text) before install; optional TypeSafe Jev second opinion. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skill-scanner.md).
- [Skillbox](https://github.com/kitze/skillbox) - Self-hosts a versioned agent skill library with optional Jev recommendations over authorized skills and explicit search fallback. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skillbox.md).
- [skillmine](https://github.com/flaviomartil/skillmine) - Mines coding-agent sessions (Claude Code, Codex CLI, Kimi CLI, OpenCode, Antigravity) for reusable knowledge: local embeddings cluster near-duplicate windows, TypeSafe Jev (or Laya / Haiku) gates whether a cluster taught something reusable, and a planner model writes skills or runbooks with provenance and one-command undo. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skillmine.md).
- [SkillRanker](https://github.com/Dicklesworthstone/skillranker) - Ranks agent skills for the next step from live session context using TypeSafe Jev, with offline demos and a Claude Code hook; MIT plus an OpenAI/Anthropic license rider. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skillranker.md).
- [skimmer](https://github.com/dorkitude/skimmer) - Go CLI that splits pages, files or lists into chunks and asks Jev one yes/no relevance question per chunk, keeping only what helps with your goal to save agent tokens. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skimmer.md).
- [Skylos](https://github.com/duriantaco/skylos) - Local-first PR scanner for dead code, security bugs, and secrets with an optional Jev-only or Jev-plus-LLM review mode for dead-code findings (pinned `jev-1.13.0`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/skylos.md).
- [SlidePilot](https://github.com/harshil1712/slidepilot) - Experimental Slidev addon that uses presenter voice, Cloudflare STT, and TypeSafe Jev to auto-advance when policy agrees. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/slidepilot.md).
- [slophound](https://github.com/JosXa/slophound) - Deterministic linter for prose written by language models (regex, grammar and statistics rules in TOML) where Jev resolves ambiguous findings in context — e.g. clearing a three-noun cluster that is an established term, or a semicolon splice that reads fine — and runs automatically once a TypeSafe key is configured. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/slophound.md).
- [SlopSearX](https://github.com/magnus919/SlopSearX) - Stateless, AI-agent-first SearXNG-compatible meta search engine (50 engines, MCP server) with optional TypeSafe Jev specialist-engine routing and bounded result reranking when `TYPESAFE_API_KEY` is set; deterministic routing/RRF order otherwise. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/slopsearx.md).
- [SmartMoney-Cub](https://github.com/myc0576/SmartMoney-Cub) - Read-only trading journal and review harness with optional TypeSafe Jev typed judgments and a frozen finance-jev offline benchmark (no orders). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/smartmoney-cub.md).
- [snake-ai (Snake learning lab)](https://github.com/markmysler/snake-ai) - Hands-on lab and nine-lesson course where neural networks learn Snake with a genetic algorithm or DQN, plus an untrained third player where Jev picks each move from a text description of what the snake senses. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/snake-ai-lab.md).
- [snap-vs-jev-swe](https://github.com/kprzybys/snap-vs-jev-swe) - Reproducible SWE benchmark of SubQ Snap, TypeSafe Jev and open decision models (file localization, long context, option order). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/snap-vs-jev-swe.md).
- [Snapif](https://github.com/snapif/snapif) - Rust crate and CLI that scores one agent tool call against a shipped policy and returns Auto, Review or Escalate, using TypeSafe Jev (`SNAPIF_BACKEND=typesafe`) or any compatible endpoint, with a shadow mode before enforcement. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/snapif.md).
- [Sniff Test](https://github.com/DanRWilloughby/snifftest) - Prose linter with free local countable rules and optional TypeSafe Jev judgment rules after confirmation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/snifftest.md).
- [Social Risk · Qwen3-VL + Jev](https://github.com/TianJinWeiBoss520/social-risk-qwen-jev) - Concluded research project (Chinese, with English README) on multimodal risk moderation for Chinese social posts (LT-EDI Chinese misogyny memes): Qwen3-VL with a language-side LoRA extracts structured evidence JSON, TypeSafe Jev makes the policy decision, and a frozen 170-item test leaderboard compares 13 methods — where a TF-IDF + logistic-regression baseline ranked first — with pure-Jev vs multimodal+Jev ablations and CPU regression tests. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/social-risk-qwen-jev.md).
- [soft-lint](https://github.com/c9r-dev/plugins) - Claude Code plugin and npm CLI that asks a System One classifier — TypeSafe Jev by default, or Jev / Clef on Cloudflare Workers AI — plain-English yes/no rules about the lines a diff adds (comments that restate code, a getter that writes, swallowed errors) and reports each 'yes' over the rule's cutoff; an incomplete check exits 2, never a silent pass. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/soft-lint.md).
- [sokudan](https://github.com/hiroki-abe-58/sokudan) - Japanese System One decision model (Jev-style): typed answers and probabilities in one forward pass, no text generation; bench_ja/bench_en and Laya bias repro. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/hiroki-abe-58-sokudan.md).
- [SOLO](https://github.com/0814wdwd/solo_jev) - Layout optimizer that reorders table rows and fields so per-row decision-model judgements reuse the prefix cache. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/solo-jev.md).
- [sommelier](https://github.com/wide3751/sommelier) - Claude Code plugin (Chinese and English READMEs) that has Jev judge reasoning depth per request and picks model and effort, upgrading freely but downgrading cautiously for cache reasons. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sommelier.md).
- [SoulsBench](https://github.com/AWoLnik/SoulsBench) - Dark Souls III realtime combat benchmark for decision models: TypeSafe Jev, OpenAI Decisions, Perplexity's decider and local Kev / Laya each fight Iudex Gundyr through the same harness, state, 16 actions and 200 ms budget on the live game; the author's 50-seed results table reports wins, damage, latency and cost per fight. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/soulsbench.md).
- [Spam → Lead Gen Pipeline](https://github.com/automatewithuday/spam-leadgen-pipeline) - Mines your Gmail spam folder for ICP-fit senders: Jev classifies and scores each email, then the pipeline audits the sender's email infrastructure and drafts (never sends) a reverse pitch. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/spam-leadgen-pipeline.md).
- [SpamGuard (tb-spam-guard)](https://github.com/Helicon1968/tb-spam-guard) - Thunderbird add-on that flags phishing mail impersonating Japanese organizations with header rules, asking TypeSafe Jev for a second opinion only on borderline messages (Japanese UI). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tb-spam-guard.md).
- [specpi-jev-guard](https://github.com/TannerMidd/specpi-jev-guard) - Pi extension: local deny/fast-pass rules, then TypeSafe Jev danger scores for uncertain shell/file commands. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/specpi-jev-guard.md).
- [sports-image-sorter](https://github.com/nematatu/sports-image-sorter-local) - Python CLI and review UI that sorts sports photos into per-sport folders with local vision engines and conservative fusion, with an optional Jev cloud engine for image Choice questions behind explicit off-device gates. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sports-image-sorter.md).
- [Spotlight (Buried Signals)](https://github.com/buriedsignals/spotlight) - OSINT investigation orchestrator for journalists working with AI agents, with opt-in decision checks via OpenRouter (default `typesafe/jev-1.13`) that flag findings their stored sources do not support, report prose that overstates findings, and knowledge-base drift — flags can only lower confidence. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/spotlight.md).
- [Spring AI TypeSafe](https://github.com/spring-ai-community/spring-ai-typesafe) - Unofficial Java/Spring AI System One client with JevJudge, guardrail/RAG advisors, and starter (Maven `org.springaicommunity`; distinct from jev-java). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/spring-ai-typesafe.md).
- [sq (System One Query)](https://github.com/LIghtJUNction/sq) - Rust CLI and local relay that runs Choice, Score and Noul decisions over shell pipelines with bounded concurrency and ordered JSON-line output, for Jev, Drex or a local Laya server, plus a typed Python SDK. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sq-system-one-query.md).
- [SREGym](https://github.com/SREGym/SREGym) - Platform and benchmark for AI agents that resolve production incidents in live Kubernetes environments (90 SRE problems, SREGym-Lite starter set), with optional Jev decision support that reviews an agent's diagnostic tests and submissions using evidence sent to TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sregym.md).
- [standard_model_for_jev_tasks (jev-shim)](https://github.com/NeuralNotwerk/standard_model_for_jev_tasks) - Dependency-free shim that serves an ordinary instruct LLM (vLLM or llama.cpp) behind TypeSafe's `POST /v1/systemone` interface, constraining every answer with a GBNF grammar and reading probabilities from logits, so Jev clients and JevBench can drive a self-hosted model. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/standard-model-for-jev-tasks.md).
- [Stanley Code](https://github.com/devagrawal09/stanley-code) - Routes coding requests into bounded Jev review and triage workflows, with trusted repository extensions and an optional Pi coding-agent fallback. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/stanley-code.md).
- [StartLux-Decision](https://github.com/StartLuxLabs/StartLux-Decision) - Independent open typed decision models (0.8B to 35B-A3B, text and images) served on the TypeSafe `/v1/systemone` request format, with inference code, evaluation scripts, and game logs; not affiliated with TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/startlux-decision.md).
- [stil-lint](https://github.com/fredrsat/stil-lint) - Style and quality linter for Norwegian (and English) text, delivered as a CLI and MCP server, that flags prose reading like unedited LLM output through several independent layers, with TypeSafe Jev as the optional judgment layer (directly or via OpenRouter); a style linter, not an authorship detector. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/stil-lint.md).
- [stingray](https://github.com/Nanako0129/stingray) - Claude Code/Codex Stop hook: TypeSafe Jev catches half-done turns (no action, broken promise, empty watch, wrong language). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/stingray.md).
- [StockSignal](https://github.com/kingabzpro/StocksSignal) - Terminal dashboard that combines yfinance data and Olostep news with TypeSafe Jev choice and score questions to show BUY/HOLD/SELL probabilities for up to five stocks (not financial advice). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/stocksignal.md).
- [stop-rules](https://github.com/haystackeditor/stop-rules) - Coding-agent stop hook that asks TypeSafe Jev yes/no per changed piece against written team rules (multi-agent; optional team server). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/stop-rules.md).
- [Strands Decider](https://github.com/strands-labs/strands-decider) - Run a small fast System One–style decision model for agentic option/score/yes-no workflows (independent of hosted Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/strands-decider.md).
- [strapi-plugin-jev-review](https://github.com/gbesse/strapi-plugin-jev-review) - Strapi 5 editorial review/publish guard: TypeSafe Jev chooses approve/escalate/revise with confidence. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/strapi-plugin-jev-review.md).
- [stuntd](https://github.com/bladedevoff/stuntd) - Local Jev-compatible proxy that serves or learns typed System One decisions on a Laya head (optional OpenAI/Jev upstream). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/stuntd.md).
- [Sudus](https://github.com/eas4ai/sudus) - Plugin (Claude Code, Codex, Muse; formerly Cairn) that keeps AI-assisted development tied to agreed scope, with an optional TypeSafe Jev evaluator (`typesafeai.enabled`) that measures the agent's draft at Consequential choices on five weighted dimensions instead of a harness review model. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sudus.md).
- [super (superpowers-jev)](https://github.com/mindlace/super) - Claude Code plugin marketplace whose `superpowers-jev` plugin adds optional TypeSafe Jev typed judgments at 13 decision points (shadow mode, fail-open). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mindlace-super.md).
- [super-jev](https://github.com/Kevthetech143/super-jev) - Evidence-to-action TypeScript harness: typed Jev judgments, permitted tools, verified outcomes, and JSONL journals. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/super-jev.md).
- [Supercov](https://github.com/supercorp-ai/supercov) - Code quality and test coverage for coding agents: Jev scores each source file so the agent knows what to fix first. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/supercov.md).
- [SuperLocalMemory](https://github.com/qualixar/superlocalmemory) - Local-first, governed memory for AI agents (SQLite, hybrid recall, time-aware facts) with an optional answer check that asks Laya on-device or Jev online whether the top recalled memories actually answer the question. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/superlocalmemory.md).
- [Supply Chain Hunter](https://github.com/SecOps-AG/Supply-Chain-Hunter) - Self-hosted monitor for software supply-chain attacks: diffs each new release of the top npm and PyPI packages (and lookalike names) against the previous version, has Jev answer many narrow yes/no questions about the diff as the Scout stage, and escalates only suspicious releases to triage and analyst models. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/supply-chain-hunter.md).
- [Support Ticket Router](https://github.com/khaledjamal/support-ticket-router) - FastAPI service that asks Jev three questions per support ticket in one call (team Choice, urgency Score, churn-risk Noul) and routes by expected cost of a mistake — auto, quick review or human; dashboard, review queue, offline fake backend and a Banking77 calibration benchmark of Jev vs three LLMs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/support-ticket-router.md).
- [Sureband](https://github.com/TejaPriyan/Sureband) - Split conformal prediction wrappers that turn System One (Jev/Laya/…) confidences into coverage-guaranteed prediction sets. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sureband.md).
- [Surf CLI](https://github.com/nicobailon/surf-cli) - Chrome-control CLI for AI agents with opt-in `surf semantic.*` commands: TypeSafe Jev selects the next action from Surf's allowed menu and checks whether the goal was reached. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/surf-cli.md).
- [SWAT](https://github.com/r0075h3ll/SWAT) - Python CLI that finds domains impersonating a brand: an LLM proposes lookalike and typosquat candidates, live ones are checked via certificate logs and DNS/HTTP, then TypeSafe Jev answers yes/no on domain and homepage impersonation. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/swat.md).
- [swift-jev](https://github.com/d-date/swift-jev) - Swift library/CLI for TypeSafe Jev typed Choice/Noul/Score judgments (distinct from krzyzanowskim/TypeSafe). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/swift-jev.md).
- [sys1 (alvarobartt)](https://github.com/alvarobartt/sys1) - Rust System One–compatible API for open decision models such as Laya (local `/v1/systemone`). Distinct from hraness/sys1. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/alvarobartt-sys1.md).
- [sys1grep](https://github.com/uehaj/sys1grep) - Meaning-grep CLI: TypeSafe Jev scores each line against a proposition (AND/OR/NOT); multilingual; npm `@uehaj/sys1grep` (formerly `@uehaj/semgrep` / jev-semgrep). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-semgrep.md).
- [sys1npc](https://github.com/SafiullahRattar/sys1npc) - Hot-swap scripted game NPC decision points with System One models (Jev / OpenAI Decisions) and confidence-gated fallbacks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/sys1npc.md).
- [System One Arcade and @system-one/ai](https://github.com/desplega-ai/system-one) - Bun workspace with one typed TypeScript API over System One decision providers (TypeSafe Jev, OpenRouter, OpenAI, Cloudflare Clef, Vercel AI Gateway) and a React arcade where those models play games against each other while a server logs every call and ranks them. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/system-one-arcade.md).
- [System One Auto Tag (Obsidian)](https://github.com/holy-max/obsidian-systemone-auto-tag) - Obsidian plugin that scores each note against your vault's existing tags with Jev Noul probabilities, sorts them into write, review and ignore bands, and only writes tags you accept from a heat-map matrix. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/obsidian-systemone-auto-tag.md).
- [System One categorizer demo (Bulgarian)](https://github.com/SciScend/system-one-categorizer-demo) - Small blog-category app and side-by-side test of Jev (TypeSafe API) and open Laya multilingual on 50 labelled Bulgarian posts, with a local BgGPT model naming new categories. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/system-one-categorizer-demo.md).
- [System One Connector](https://github.com/itsmostafa/system-one-connector) - Go MCP `evaluate` connector giving agents typed Jev (or local System One / Laya) judgments with probabilities for Claude Code, Codex, Hermes, and pi. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/system-one-connector.md).
- [System One for Delphi](https://github.com/danieleteti/systemone-delphi) - Single-unit Delphi client for System One models: ask choice, score and noul questions about a text and get typed answers with every option's probability, against TypeSafe's Jev API, a local Ollama model, or any OpenAI-compatible chat model through an adapter with the same interface. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/systemone-delphi.md).
- [System One for n8n (taifoon)](https://github.com/taifoon-io/n8n-nodes-system-one) - n8n community node (`@taifoon/n8n-nodes-system-one`) that sends an item and your yes/no, pick-one and score questions to a System One host such as TypeSafe Jev or Venice and returns every answer with its probabilities, so n8n IF and Switch nodes can route on them. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/taifoon-n8n-system-one.md).
- [System One Harness](https://github.com/mev0lent/system-one-harness) - Reproducible experiment asking whether TypeSafe Jev (and peers) can gate expensive LLM triage on HTTP responses without missing anomalies. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/system-one-harness.md).
- [System One Harness](https://github.com/HarnessRouter/SystemOneHarness) - Python controller: finite-action agent loops with TypeSafe Jev (OpenRouter or TypeSafe), confidence gates, and step traces. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/systemone-harness.md).
- [System One Models — Getting Started](https://github.com/gsaini/system-one-models-getting-started) - Turns the six claims of TypeSafe's Jev launch post into runnable experiments where Jev, Claude (via TypeSafe's system-one-adapter) and keyword rules answer the same typed questions and code measures latency, tokens, cost and calibration. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/system-one-models-getting-started.md).
- [System One on Snapdragon](https://github.com/esterhuizen/system-one-on-snapdragon) - Pinned snapshot of setup scripts, Jev-compatible servers and a benchmark harness for running open System One models on Snapdragon X Elite NPU/GPU/CPU and comparing them with Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/system-one-on-snapdragon.md).
- [System One Playground](https://github.com/DonaldMurillo/system-one-playground) - SysOneScript CLI/VS Code plus Go TypeSafe System One client and Studio; start offline, add live Jev when needed. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/system-one-playground.md).
- [System One routing for Apache APISIX](https://github.com/surak/semantic-routing-llm-plugin) - Adds a `system_one` load-balancing algorithm to Apache APISIX's ai-proxy-multi plugin: a System One model (Jev, Clef) ranks the configured LLM instances for each prompt and the request goes to the highest-ranked healthy one. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/apisix-system-one-routing.md).
- [System-1 decision benchmark](https://github.com/Sj0605-DataSci/system1-decision-benchmark) - Common-ground benchmark of 50 System-1 decision-model configurations — Jev, Clef, Clef-flash, Kev, Nimble and Tev1 against embedding, reranker, NLI and prompted-LLM baselines — on 22 BTZSC datasets (500 test items each), with saved scores, a living report and a paper draft. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/system1-decision-benchmark.md).
- [system-one (asynq-io)](https://github.com/asynq-io/system-one) - Vendor-neutral Python SDK for typed System One decisions (hosted TypeSafe/OpenRouter or local ONNX). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/asynq-io-system-one.md).
- [system-one skill (magnus919)](https://github.com/magnus919/agent-skills) - Agent Skills-format skill for designing, integrating, evaluating and troubleshooting typed System One decisions with TypeSafe Jev (and open models such as Laya), keeping policy and side effects in code. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/magnus919-system-one-skill.md).
- [system-one-chess](https://github.com/dperezcabrera/system-one-chess) - Browser chess vs TypeSafe Jev (Vercel AI Gateway or OpenRouter) with Stockfish analysis; optional local Laya (GPL-3.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/system-one-chess.md).
- [system-one-mcp](https://github.com/es11225599/system-one-mcp) - Small MCP server that gives agents two tools, evaluate typed Choice/Score/Noul questions and list models, against any System One backend: TypeSafe Jev, local Ollama System One models, Kev or Laya. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/system-one-mcp.md).
- [system-one-parsers](https://github.com/Engid/system-one-parsers) - Lab building a domain-free Universal Dependencies parser out of many small Jev questions — one Choice per word for part of speech, then head and relation choices — decoded into a tree in plain code, with offline oracle/dry clients, recorded runs and a rules-only floor; work in progress. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/system-one-parsers.md).
- [system-one-reviewer](https://github.com/equationalapplications/system-one-reviewer) - Local PR reviewer: deterministic diff plumbing plus TypeSafe Jev (or local System One) judgments, with a precision/recall evaluation harness. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/system-one-reviewer.md).
- [system-one-security](https://github.com/ankushchadha/system-one-security) - Rerunnable security experiments on System One models (TypeSafe Jev, Cloudflare Clef): truncation, injection, poisoning, tripwires. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/system-one-security.md).
- [systemone (gray plugin)](https://github.com/vstaln/systemone) - Rust sidecar plugin for the gray agent that puts System One decision models (TypeSafe Jev, Laya via Ollaya, any `/v1/systemone` server) behind `judge` and `semantic_find` tools plus a `/s1` chat command. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/systemone-gray.md).
- [systemone (justintout)](https://github.com/justintout/systemone) - Unofficial Go System One/Jev client with compile-time typed Choice/Noul/Score (distinct from TypeSafe Go / go-jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/systemone-justintout.md).
- [systemone-poc](https://github.com/joskr2/systemone-poc) - POC wiring Jev/Laya as a pre-routing decision layer for Pi/OpenCode/DSH agents, with measured cost notes. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/systemone-poc.md).
- [systemone-probe](https://github.com/mokko/systemone-probe) - Conformance and behaviour test harness for System One endpoints (TypeSafe Jev or self-hosted Jev-like models): checks the answer contract, Python-computed accuracy, calibration, determinism, batching, robustness and limits. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/systemone-probe.md).
- [SystemOneClient](https://github.com/schainks/system_one_client) - Elixir client for System One decision models (TypeSafe Jev, Cloudflare Clef, OpenRouter decisions, self-hosted `/v1/systemone` servers) that returns full probability distributions for Choice, Score and Noul questions, with a test stub, retries honouring `Retry-After` and telemetry that does not leak state or keys. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/system-one-client.md).
- [Tacet](https://github.com/suranjaychandra/jev-decision-layer) - Marketing decision layer that can choose to send nothing: code applies consent and frequency caps, Jev picks the next best action or budget move among what is allowed, and anything under 60% confidence goes to a person. Compares raw CRM records with compact jev-state input. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tacet.md).
- [Tacit](https://github.com/wujiajunhahah/tacit) - Local-first browser context harness: reads the page and navigation trail on your machine and hands them to Claude Code, Cursor, or any MCP agent, with a TypeSafe Jev-backed DecisionEngine (`core/jev.ts` → `/v1/systemone`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tacit.md).
- [Talos](https://github.com/zatzk/talos) - Chat-led multi-agent orchestration TUI forked from thurbox: conversational routing, tmux worker panes, a kanban board and spec-driven execution, with a built-in Jev decision engine that powers the Auto model/agent selection. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/talos.md).
- [Taste Lint](https://github.com/mblode/taste-lint) - Catch AI-sloppy UI motion, copy, and typography before you ship; optional TypeSafe Jev under-review notes via Vercel AI Gateway or direct. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/taste-lint.md).
- [taurus-jev-sdk-go](https://github.com/KKloudTarus/taurus-jev-sdk-go) - Unofficial stdlib Go client for TypeSafe System One/Jev with validated responses and masked credentials (distinct from jmelahman). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/taurus-jev-sdk-go.md).
- [tax-doc-classifier](https://github.com/kyotofin/tax-doc-classifier) - Classifies tax PDF pages into IRS forms and page kinds with TypeSafe Jev Choice over a shipped criteria JSON (261 forms). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tax-doc-classifier.md).
- [tdd-gate](https://github.com/bensheridan/tdd-gate) - Keeps test and code agents on one plan: TypeSafe Jev judges coverage, blame, gaming, weakening, and drift. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tdd-gate.md).
- [telltale-systemone](https://github.com/christian-taillon/telltale-systemone) - Reference analyzer extension for the Telltale agent-monitoring tool that asks Jev narrow typed questions about agent actions from configurable guard packs, beside Telltale's deterministic rules. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/telltale-systemone.md).
- [Temporal Agent Harness](https://github.com/temporal-community/temporal-agent-harness) - Temporal-native durable multi-agent harness whose optional `jev` extra provides `agent.jev_evaluator`, a Jev-backed auto mode for tool approvals. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/temporal-agent-harness.md).
- [Ten Levels of Jev](https://github.com/disler/ten-levels-of-jev) - Teaching lab: ten incremental Jev levels from a smart if-statement to a pi coding agent that reaches for Jev on its own (offline mocks + live OpenRouter). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/ten-levels-of-jev.md).
- [TensorSharp](https://github.com/zhongkaifu/TensorSharp) - Native .NET GGUF inference engine and agent runtime that serves a Jev-compatible `POST /v1/systemone` with DiffusionGemma, answering typed questions over text, images, documents, video frames and transcripts; `jev-latest` is an alias for the local model, not hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tensorsharp.md).
- [Terraform Plan Verdict](https://github.com/nandotorres/terraform-plan-verdict) - GitHub Action that turns a Terraform plan into a graded LOW / MEDIUM / HIGH / CRITICAL risk verdict across blast radius, destructiveness and security impact, surfaced as a job summary, sticky PR comment, labels and outputs; the default provider is deterministic rules, and the optional `systemone` provider asks Jev (`jev-latest`) for calibrated scores from a redacted plan summary. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/terraform-plan-verdict.md).
- [Testimonial miner](https://github.com/AppitStudio/testimonial-miner) - Python CLI that finds quotable user praise in Gmail mailboxes with one Jev request per email (message kind, app, praise quality, and a Noul per sentence) and stores verbatim quotes for review; requires a TypeSafe key and Google app passwords, and sends cleaned email text to TypeSafe. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/testimonial-miner.md).
- [TetraJev](https://github.com/FeiLiuEM/tetrajev) - Zero-training local decision layer: two frozen readers × four readings per item, fit-free fusion and agreement routing, with published coverage–accuracy across eight suites and a RAG reranking pass; independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tetrajev.md).
- [tev1 (Together)](https://github.com/togethercomputer/tev1) - Open-weight Jev-inspired decision model (Qwen3.5-4B fine-tune) with training recipe and published results on Together. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tev1.md).
- [tf-jev](https://github.com/alexsosn/tf-jev) - Research prototype that uses TypeSafe Jev bounded decisions to fill a typed query IR, then deterministically compiles native Text-Fabric search templates; the model never writes query text. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tf-jev.md).
- [tg-x-copilot (Telegram to X drafting copilot)](https://github.com/stackoracode/tg-x-copilot) - Telegram bot that turns forwarded posts into original X drafts: Jev triages each post with typed value, type and risk questions so confident low-value posts are skipped early, then an LLM rewrites the rest and you approve and post yourself. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tg-x-copilot.md).
- [The Jev-enator](https://github.com/jakenbear/the-jev-enator) - Claude Code hooks using TypeSafe Jev for a danger gate, failure notice, and optional completion check (cassette-tested offline). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/the-jev-enator.md).
- [Theme Tab Filter (jev-tab-filter)](https://github.com/mpeddicord/jev-tab-filter) - Chrome extension: type a theme; TypeSafe Jev scores open tabs so you can group, hide, window, or close matches. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-tab-filter.md).
- [thinkdial](https://github.com/DmitryBMsk/thinkdial) - Claude Code mod: TypeSafe Jev sets per-task reasoning effort for the main loop, subagents, and Codex delegations (effort-only by default). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/thinkdial.md).
- [ThinkThen](https://github.com/botassembly/thinkthen) - CLI and SDK for typed questions about text: `decide`, `choose` and `score` return values and exit codes a shell can branch on, with TypeSafe Jev as one backend. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/thinkthen.md).
- [Thorkell (Valheim companion)](https://github.com/lofcgi/thorkell) - Research project: a Valheim companion that joins your world as a real second player, reads its own game screen, takes chat requests like "make us an axe" or "follow me", and uses Jev for fast typed decisions under a deadline while a larger model plans and talks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/thorkell-valheim.md).
- [Tidepool](https://github.com/tidepool-heavy-industries/tidepool) - Tidepool/Exomonad: a live Haskell notebook and programmable agent harness where reasoning LLMs write procedures and Jev supplies cheap typed System 1 judgments inside ordinary code; ships a Jev skill for question batteries and settlement policies. Source available (PolyForm Shield). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tidepool.md).
- [tiergear](https://github.com/jang2162/tiergear) - Claude Code plugin and CLI where a decision model judge — TypeSafe Jev by default, or local Laya/Kev — picks a tier (trivial → max) for each typed prompt and maps it to a model and reasoning effort, with floors, ceilings and cache-aware switching. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tiergear.md).
- [tink-route](https://github.com/jon-devlapaz/tink-route) - Confidence-aware Agent Skills router: TypeSafe Jev Noul/Choice gate, optional Tink install/prune. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tink-route.md).
- [Tiny Bouncer](https://github.com/radupopescu/tiny-bouncer) - Screens shell commands that LLM agents request in the OpenCode V2 harness: each command goes to TypeSafe Jev (default) for a judgment, and code thresholds turn the probabilities into allow, deny or the normal interactive prompt; it fails safe to asking and never grants permission your config denies. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tiny-bouncer.md).
- [tinycomputer](https://github.com/tinyhumansai/tinycomputer) - Rust TinyBus module for desktop (a11y) and Chrome automation: primitives, plain-language flows, and tasks that ask TypeSafe Jev many small questions. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tinyhumansai-tinycomputer.md).
- [TinyJev](https://github.com/ankit-aglawe/tinyjev) - Offline ~0.6B System One–compatible Choice/Noul/Score model on MLX or PyTorch (independent of hosted Jev). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tinyjev.md).
- [todo-jev](https://github.com/maker-KK/todo-jev) - Task classifier and 3-tier router (local rule / Jev skill / foundation model) with skill profiles and offline fallback. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/todo-jev.md).
- [tokengate (jev-model-tokengate)](https://github.com/Thanh-Mathieu95/jev-model-tokengate) - Streaming OpenAI-compatible proxy that buffers tokens and runs TypeSafe Jev safety criteria before release, aiming for zero UI leakage vs post-hoc redaction. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-model-tokengate.md).
- [ToolDiscoveryBench](https://github.com/SarathChandraBellam/ToolDiscoveryBench) - Benchmark of how accurately and quickly a router picks the right MCP tool: the same golden questions run against real MCP tool catalogs (with seeded distractors at several catalog sizes) through TypeSafe Jev (flat, factored and hierarchical strategies), Strands Agents LLM tool calling, BM25 and embeddings, reporting accuracy, latency, cost and Jev calibration (ECE, Brier). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tooldiscoverybench.md).
- [toolgate](https://github.com/RiskAverseTech/toolgate) - Open Claude Code PreToolUse and MCP tool-call firewall with static rules, TypeSafe Jev judgments, YAML policy, and a local audit log. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/toolgate.md).
- [toolhunch](https://github.com/dfm88/toolhunch) - Python library for agents with too many tools: hybrid search narrows a tool catalog, then a fast decider — TypeSafe Jev, Cloudflare Clef, or Jev-compatible local servers (Strands Decider, rizzo-flow, Laya) — or an LLM picks one tool or answers 'none'; maintainer benchmarks on 40–101-tool catalogs and ToolRet's 44,453 tools, with Pydantic AI integration. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/toolhunch.md).
- [TraceRoot](https://github.com/traceroot-ai/traceroot) - Open-source agent tracing and improvement platform that auto-instruments TypeSafe Jev SDK calls as decision spans (model version, questions, answers, probabilities, cost) and uses Jev in its detection worker. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/traceroot.md).
- [train-jev](https://github.com/shibing624/train-jev) - Open training recipe for JEV-style System 1 models on Qwen3.5 with LoRA decision head and System One-compatible serve. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/train-jev.md).
- [Traits](https://github.com/crosshatch/traits) - Reusable coding preferences for AI agents: feedback becomes Markdown trait files in `.agents/traits/`, and the agent proposes, gets approval for, and checks its work against them over MCP, with TypeSafe available as a model provider. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/traits.md).
- [Trax .NET](https://github.com/TraxSharp/Trax) - Headless .NET web framework that records every run in Postgres; its `Trax.Effect.Decisions.SystemOne` package adds System One decisions pinned to models such as `jev-1.13.0`. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/trax-net.md).
- [triagedy](https://github.com/m0rphtail/triagedy) - UNIX-filter security-alert triage: JSONL in, typed TypeSafe Jev decisions out; policy routing stays in Rust code. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/triagedy.md).
- [trirouter](https://github.com/hajdu-patrik/trirouter) - One prompt router for Claude Code, Codex and Antigravity (formerly jev-router): JEV — via a TypeSafe token or an OpenRouter key — classifies each prompt's task and difficulty to pick the model tier and adds a destructive-command verdict on top of a regex safety net; a built-in local classifier with the same answer format is the fallback. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/trirouter.md).
- [Truffler](https://github.com/kieranklaassen/truffler) - Rails gem for intent search: TypeSafe Jev builds index-time labels and query understanding on top of your keyword/embedding search, with optional streamed reranking. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/truffler.md).
- [tsjev for DuckDB](https://github.com/Querio-App/duckdb-jev) - DuckDB extension from Querio that adds SQL functions to classify, filter, and score table rows with TypeSafe Jev, with deduplication of identical rows, memory and disk caching, and 32 concurrent requests. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tsjev-duckdb.md).
- [Typed Evals](https://github.com/TrustifAI/typed_evals) - Evaluate LLM/RAG/agent outputs with TypeSafe Jev judges, optional calibration, and tool guards. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typed-evals.md).
- [typed-lm](https://github.com/neurono-ml/typed-lm) - Rust Candle stack: turn dense LLMs into a Jev-style typed Choice/Noul/Score API (LoRA/QLoRA, /v1/systemone)—independent of hosted Jev. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typed-lm.md).
- [TypeSafe (Swift)](https://github.com/krzyzanowskim/TypeSafe) - SwiftPM client for TypeSafe System One Noul/Choice/Score questions, aligned with the official JS SDK shape. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-swift.md).
- [TypeSafe .NET SDK (mehdihadeli)](https://github.com/mehdihadeli/typesafe-dotnet-sdk) - Community C# SDK for TypeSafe System One (netstandard2.0/net10.0): typed Noul/Choice/Score questions and answers; project reference until its NuGet ID is resolved. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/mehdihadeli-typesafe-dotnet-sdk.md).
- [TypeSafe AI for Agent Zero](https://github.com/3clyp50/a0-typesafe-ai) - Agent Zero plugin for typed Jev Choice/Noul/Score judgments with probability cards; bundles the official TypeSafe agent skill. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/a0-typesafe-ai.md).
- [TypeSafe AI unofficial guide (typesafe-docs)](https://github.com/typesafe-docs/typesafeai) - Unofficial guide to the TypeSafe System One API (Choice/Score/Noul, 18 cookbooks, Python + TypeScript examples, mock server). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafeai-unofficial-guide.md).
- [TypeSafe AI, from fundamentals to use cases (notebooks)](https://github.com/rickysullivan/typesafe-tutorial) - Deno Jupyter notebooks that teach Jev from the basics up: what a System One model is, Choice, Score and Noul, structure and confidence, then the four documented patterns (fan-out, confidence-gated routing, composite scoring, intent routing) and cookbooks. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/rickysullivan-typesafe-tutorial.md).
- [TypeSafe C++ SDK](https://github.com/pewriebontal/typesafe-sdk-cpp) - C++20 client for TypeSafe System One typed Choice/Score/Noul questions (Jev-ready). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-sdk-cpp.md).
- [TypeSafe Go](https://github.com/stacklok/typesafe-go) - Unofficial Go System One client (Stacklok): explicit auth options, no implicit env reads; distinct from jevgo. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-go.md).
- [TypeSafe Jev custom connector](https://github.com/markus-tobler/jev-custom-connector) - Power Platform managed custom connector for TypeSafe Jev System One (Power Automate / Copilot Studio). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/jev-custom-connector.md).
- [TypeSafe Jev × Salesforce](https://github.com/divingsbysangam/typesafe-jev-salesforce) - Salesforce DX project that calls Jev from Apex for Service Cloud case triage (classify, score, route) and as an Agentforce guardrail that screens each guest message before the agent may act, with a switchable OpenAI Decisions provider for comparison. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-jev-salesforce.md).
- [TypeSafe Mario](https://github.com/fhshaik/typesafe-mario) - Selects emulator controller inputs with Jev from structured Mario telemetry; includes a synthetic state demo, with upstream licensing unspecified. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-mario.md).
- [TypeSafe MCP](https://github.com/itsmostafa/typesafe-mcp) - Exposes typed Jev questions to MCP clients and pi, preserving provider responses with configurable direct or OpenRouter access. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-mcp.md).
- [typesafe-agent-gates](https://github.com/ThiagaoBR/typesafe_agent_gates) - LangChain/Deep Agents middleware using TypeSafe Jev for shell gates, issue triage, MR detection, and test-spec review. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-agent-gates.md).
- [typesafe-ai PHP client](https://github.com/basi/typesafe-ai-php-client) - Unofficial PHP 8.4 client for the Jev System One API: typed request objects for Noul, Choice and Score (plus experimental Box with images), typed responses with confidence and usage, and a mock HTTP client for tests. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-ai-php-client.md).
- [typesafe-api (Rust)](https://github.com/noahbclarkson/typesafe-api-rs) - Ergonomic Rust client for TypeSafe System One typed questions and answers (`typesafe-api` 0.1.0). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-api-rs.md).
- [TypeSafe-as-a-Judge](https://github.com/E-FL/typesafe-as-a-judge) - Unofficial Codex/Claude Code MCP plugin for bounded TypeSafe Jev route/rank/extract/verify/judge tools with proceed/review gates. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-as-a-judge.md).
- [typesafe-cli](https://github.com/y0usaf/typesafe-cli) - Shell `jev` CLI for TypeSafe Jev noul/choice/score answers as numbers (distinct from Python typesafeai-cli). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-cli.md).
- [typesafe-client (haileyok)](https://github.com/haileyok/typesafe-client) - Unofficial Go and Rust clients for TypeSafe System One (Jev): Noul/Choice/Score helpers, official-style retries, and env configuration—alongside the official Python/JS SDKs. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/haileyok-typesafe-client.md).
- [typesafe-computer-use](https://github.com/awlevin/typesafe-computer-use) - Combines local OCR and Accessibility observations with Jev decisions to operate macOS, with an optional writing model. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-computer-use.md).
- [typesafe-computer-use-win](https://github.com/Vatsa10/typesafe-computer-use-win) - Windows port of typesafe-computer-use: UI Automation + OCR with TypeSafe Jev decisions (`winclicker`). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-computer-use-win.md).
- [typesafe-decisions](https://github.com/kaischinnen/typesafe-decisions) - Typed Python helpers for semantic scoring, classification, and routing on top of TypeSafe System One (`TypeSafeClient`), with docs site examples. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-decisions.md).
- [typesafe-gates](https://github.com/mpointer/typesafe-gates) - TypeScript decision gates for LLM pipelines on Jev: define Score, Choice or yes/no questions per item, get probability tables, and keep the keep-rule, threshold and fail-open or fail-closed policy in your own code. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-gates.md).
- [typesafe-go (zhirschtritt)](https://github.com/zhirschtritt/typesafe-go) - Unofficial idiomatic Go SDK for TypeSafe System One; distinct from listed stacklok/typesafe-go. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/zhirschtritt-typesafe-go.md).
- [typesafe-haystack (Haystack integration)](https://github.com/deepset-ai/haystack-core-integrations/tree/main/integrations/typesafe) - Official Haystack integration (`typesafe-haystack`) adding two pipeline components that call TypeSafe System One models such as Jev: `TypeSafeDocumentClassifier` answers typed `choice` / `score` / `noul` questions about each document and stores the probabilities in its metadata, and a text router sends inputs down pipeline branches by Jev's answer. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-haystack.md).
- [typesafe-sdk-csharp (tylerwarner33)](https://github.com/tylerwarner33/typesafe-sdk-csharp) - Unofficial C# TypeSafe API client mirroring official JS/Python SDK shapes (distinct from typesafe-sdk-csharp/typesafe-sdk and CMaintz/jev-dotnet). [Project guide](community/projects/tools/tylerwarner33-typesafe-sdk-csharp.md).
- [typesafe-sdk-go (jmelahman)](https://github.com/jmelahman/typesafe-sdk-go) - Unofficial stdlib-only Go client for TypeSafe System One with Noul/Choice/Score helpers. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-sdk-go-jmelahman.md).
- [typesafe-sdk-rust (zchee)](https://github.com/zchee/typesafe-sdk-rust) - Unofficial async Rust SDK for TypeSafe System One (`typesafe-sdk-rust` on crates.io): `#[derive(QuestionSet)]`, port of typesafe-sdk-python 0.7.1. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/zchee-typesafe-sdk-rust.md).
- [TypeSafe.AI (.NET SDK)](https://github.com/typesafe-sdk-csharp/typesafe-sdk) - Unofficial .NET System One client (NuGet TypeSafe.AI) with DI, resilience, and OTel; distinct from TypeSafeAI.Net. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-sdk-csharp.md).
- [typesafe_rs](https://github.com/ppweni/typesafe_rs) - Unofficial async Rust client for Jev: send state and typed Choice, Score and Noul questions, attach image assets and try experimental Jev 1.14 Box questions, with typed responses and a configurable retry policy. [Project guide](community/projects/tools/typesafe-rs.md).
